### [23] [Model of Models: When Does Emitting a Specialist Beat Attending, Adapting, or Tuning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21386)
*John C. Howell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了四种任务特化机制（零样本、上下文注意力、测试时梯度适应、超网络生成专家权重），在六个跨域任务上做了相同设置的四路对比，发现超网络发射（emission）在成本上具有优势，但无法在高维序列建模中匹敌上下文注意力，并提出了一个可证伪的条件机制选择理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已有四种任务特化机制（零样本、上下文注意力、测试时梯度适应、超网络发射专家权重），但超网络发射这一机制的适用范围（operating regime）很少被系统映射。作者希望通过统一、公平的四路对比，明确每种机制的优势边界。

Method: 在六个任务（回归、生成、语言建模、强化学习、临床与基因组分类）中运行完全相同的四机制对比实验，固定专家模型、上下文和训练预算。此外进行了多组消融实验：包括LoRA秩扫描以探测容量限制、机制消融（排除记忆先验）、权重空间插值测试专家权重的组合性，并提出了基于每任务分辨率指标的框架来界定各机制的使用边界。

Result: 发射机制在匹配质量下成本最优：在临床少样本分类上与TabPFN持平但可复用专家而非逐查询重算，在形状生成上只需132个浮点数的每实例程序；在少样本正弦回归上比零梯度步的MAML低2-3个数量级（预算相等后缩小至约30倍）。但在高维序列建模中发射无法匹敌上下文注意力——匹配预算预训练下，单次适配器仅恢复14.0±0.9%（5M）和11.2±0.5%（15M）的上下文增益；LoRA秩扫描显示容量从5%提升到21%但远低于完全恢复。消融证实发射的专家确为任务条件化而非记忆先验，且专家权重可在权重空间中进行插值组合。

Conclusion: 发射机制在低维/低成本场景下优势明显（成本效率高、可复用、可组合），但在高维序列建模中存在不可忽视的容量差距。作者提出一个基于分辨率度量的可证伪论点，为不同条件下各机制的选择提供了边界框架。

Abstract: Given a task described by a few examples, how should a model be specialized to it? Four mechanisms are available -- zero-shot, in-context attention, test-time gradient adaptation, and emitting specialist weights from a hypernetwork -- yet the operating regime of the last is rarely mapped. We run the identical four-way comparison across six tasks spanning regression, generation, language modeling, reinforcement learning, and clinical and genomic classification, holding the specialist, the context, and (where we can) the training budget fixed. The clearest wins for emission are about cost at matched quality: it ties the state-of-the-art amortized tabular model (TabPFN) on clinical few-shot classification while emitting a reusable specialist instead of re-attending the support set per query, and reaches noise-floor shape generation with a $132$-float per-instance program. On few-shot sinusoid regression it is $2$--$3$ orders of magnitude below MAML at zero test-time gradient steps -- a margin that narrows to $\sim$$30\times$ but persists once training budgets are equalized. Emission cannot match in-context attention on high-dimensional sequence modeling: under matched-budget pre-training a one-pass adapter recovers only a minority of the in-context gain ($14.0\pm0.9\%$ at $5$M, $11.2\pm0.5\%$ at $15$M), and a LoRA-rank sweep shows this shortfall is a partial capacity limit -- capture climbs from $5\%$ to $21\%$ as rank grows but plateaus far below full recovery. Mechanism ablations confirm the emitted specialist is genuinely task-conditioned, not a memorized prior; and, more speculatively, emitted specialists compose in weight space -- interpolating two of them tracks the corresponding blend of their functions. We close with a falsifiable thesis, operationalized through a per-task resolution measure, bounding when each conditioning mechanism should be preferred.

</details>


### [24] [Runtime Action Interference for AI Control of AlphaStar in StarCraft II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21398)
*Jaymari Chua,Chen Wang,Liming Zhu,Lina Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"运行时动作干扰"（RAI）机制，这是一种AI控制手段，在推理后通过冷却条件和内容检测器来调节强化学习策略的动作节奏与过滤，而非修改策略参数。在《星际争霸II》人类参与者研究中，同一RAI控制的对手在披露能力信息与否的情况下，公平感、信任感和毒性感知呈现显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一个经过训练的强化学习策略并不能完全决定用户遇到的行为，部署代码仍会调度、接纳、抑制或替换其建议的动作。研究者希望探索如何在保留策略参数的同时，通过后置机制控制动作节奏与过滤特定动作模式，并考察这种控制在人类感知层面（公平、信任、毒性）的影响。

Method: 提出RAI（Runtime Action Interference）机制：仅在冷却条件满足且内容检测器未标记动作时才释放提议的动作，否则派发no-op。通过冷却控制动作速率，检测器覆盖特定有毒行为（如工人单位骚扰）。在AlphaStar actor.py的复现版中实现，并在《星际争霸II》人类参与者研究中部署，比较同一高能力对手在有/无能力披露两种呈现下的感知差异。

Result: 在1-5评分量表上，隐瞒能力时的公平感、信任感、毒性均值分别为3.90、3.50、2.00；披露时为2.62、4.31、2.85。披露与所有专家组中更低的公平感和更高的毒性感知相关；信任方面新手和专家增加但中级参与者下降。

Conclusion: 人类评估必须将执行栈内的控制与能力披露分开，并将公平感、信任感和毒性视为人类体验的不同维度进行评估。通过RAI控制的对手，其感知会随呈现给用户的能力信息而显著变化，即使配置的控制保持不变。

Abstract: A trained reinforcement learning policy does not determine the complete behavior that users encounter: deployment code still schedules, admits, suppresses, or replaces its proposed actions. We contribute \emph{runtime action interference} (RAI), an AI control mechanism that preserves policy parameters while regulating action pacing and filtering configured action patterns after inference. RAI releases a proposed action only when its cooldown condition is satisfied and its content detector does not flag the action; otherwise, it dispatches a no-op. The detector covers specified toxic behaviors, including worker-unit harassment, while the cooldown controls action rate. We implement RAI in a replication of AlphaStar actor.py and make the implementation and reproducibility materials available through an open source code repository. We deployed RAI in a \textit{StarCraft~II} human participant study that compared two presentations of the same opponent with high capability and rate limited actions; we withheld its capability claim in one presentation and disclosed it in the other. On response scales from 1 to 5, we observed pooled fairness, trust, and toxicity means of 3.90, 3.50, and 2.00 under claim withholding, compared with 2.62, 4.31, and 2.85 under disclosure. Disclosure corresponded with lower perceived fairness and higher perceived toxicity across every expertise group, whereas trust increased among novices and experts but decreased among intermediate participants. Our human evaluation therefore shows that perceptions of an opponent controlled through RAI can vary substantially with the capability information presented to users, even when the configured control remains constant. We conclude that human-computer evaluations must separate control within the execution stack from capability disclosure and assess fairness, trust, and toxicity as distinct dimensions of human experience.

</details>


### [25] [Federated Ensemble Forecasting Under Supply-Chain Market Volatility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21399)
*Shunmukha Sagar Puppala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FEF-NCL，一种面向供应链预测的联邦集成学习方法，通过负相关学习训练跨客户端节点的专业化预测专家，并在合成数据集上验证了其在市场波动场景下相比联邦基线（如SCAFFOLD）在错误率、风险指标和高波动场景上的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 供应链预测系统面临市场冲击、区域需求非独立同分布以及商业数据集中意愿低等挑战，现有集中式预测方法难以应对这些分布式、异构且隐私受限的数据环境，需要一种能够在客户节点间协作训练且抑制冗余预测误差的联邦学习方案。

Method: FEF-NCL框架由四部分组成：时序特征编码器、客户端漂移评分、可靠性加权聚合和可解释性层。其核心思想是通过负相关学习使各客户端节点的预测专家相互专业化、降低误差间的冗余相关性，同时结合客户端级数据漂移评分进行可靠性加权的模型聚合。

Result: 在包含124,800条周级SKU-区域观测、覆盖十个客户端节点、60个产品族、40个供应商和五类大宗商品的合成数据集上，FEF-NCL将加权平均绝对百分比误差从联邦基线最优的13.9%降至12.4%，延迟风险宏F1从0.755提升至0.801，高波动分位误差相对SCAFFOLD降低2.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 负相关专业化的联邦学习在客户端面临不同供应商、货运和商品条件时具有明显优势，但实际部署仍需更严格的隐私分析、实时漂移监控和运行校准。由于数据集为合成数据，结果为内部一致性的受控证据而非真实世界验证。

Abstract: Supply chain forecasting systems increasingly operate under market shocks, non-identically distributed regional demand, and limited willingness to centralize commercial data. This work proposes Federated Ensemble Forecasting with Negative-Correlation Learning (FEF NCL), a distributed method that trains specialized forecasting experts across client nodes while discouraging redundant model errors. The framework combines temporal feature encoders, client level drift scoring, reliability-weighted aggregation, and an explain ability layer that exposes the market and supplier variables most responsible for each forecast. A single synthetic dataset is used to evaluate the design. It contains 124,800 weekly SKU region observations from ten regional client nodes, 60 product families, 40 suppliers, five commodity groups, and a 2021-2024 volatility profile with explicit price-shock regimes. Because the dataset is synthetic, the reported results should be interpreted as controlled evidence of internal consistency rather than real-world validation. Across the synthetic test split, FEF NCL reduces weighted mean absolute percentage error from 13.9% for the best federated baseline to 12.4%, improves delay-risk macro-F1 from 0.755 to 0.801, and lowers the high volatility quintile error by 2.1 percentage points relative to SCAFFOLD. The analysis suggests that negative-correlation specialization is useful when clients face different supplier, freight, and commodity conditions, although deployment would require stronger privacy analysis, live drift monitoring, and operational calibration. Index Terms federated learning, ensemble learning, negative correlation learning, supply chain forecasting, market volatility, data drift, demand planning, risk governance

</details>


### [26] [Class-Conditioned Gaussian Mixture Modeling for Imbalanced Time Series Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21473)
*Md Shahriar Kabir,Mayesha Maliha R. Mithila,Anne H. H. Ngu,Mylène C. Q. Farias,Byron Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CC-GMNet-TS，一种针对不平衡时间序列数据的类条件高斯混合量化器。它结合Transformer特征提取器和每个类独立的紧致混合模型，在限定潜空间中为每类分配独立混合，并通过类特定组件对段嵌入进行评分以生成包级表示，强调稀有但有信息量的模式。在三个基准（EMG手势数据、SmartFallMM和UCI-HAR）上，该方法相比传统聚合器和最新深度量化器取得了更低的误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非实例标签重要而聚合统计量更关键的领域（如生物信号监测、跌倒检测、活动识别），量化估计包中未标注实例的类别流行度至关重要。现有混合量化器为所有类别共享单个高斯混合，难以应对不平衡时间序列数据中稀有但关键的模式。

Method: CC-GMNet-TS由三部分构成：(1) Transformer特征提取器提取时间序列片段的嵌入表示；(2) 每个类别在限定潜空间中被分配独立的紧致高斯基元混合，段嵌入通过这些类特定组件评分生成包级表示；(3) 使用Artificial Prevalence Protocol和prior shift bag sampling构造覆盖广泛流行度场景的包，并以量化导向损失端到端训练。

Result: 在EMG手势数据、SmartFallMM和UCI-HAR三个基准上，CC-GMNet-TS相比传统聚合器和最新深度量化器均取得更低误差；消融实验证实了Transformer主干和PShift下类条件混合二者的贡献。

Conclusion: 类条件高斯混合与Transformer特征提取的结合能有效改善不平衡时间序列数据的类别流行度量化精度，特别是对稀有模式的表征能力提升显著。

Abstract: Quantification, estimating class prevalences in bags of unlabeled instances is vital in domains where aggregate statistics are more important than individual instance labels, such as biosignal monitoring, fall detection, and activity recognition. We investigate this issue in the challenging setting of imbalanced time series data and develop CC-GMNet-TS, a class-conditioned Gaussian mixture quantifier that combines a Transformer-based feature extractor with per-class latent mixtures. Unlike previous mixture-based quantifiers, which use a single Gaussian mixture shared by all classes, CC-GMNet-TS assigns each class its own compact mixture in a bounded latent space and scores segment embeddings against these class-specific components to create bag-level representations that emphasize rare but informative patterns. Bags are constructed from labeled pools using the Artificial Prevalence Protocol (APP) and prior shift bag sampling (PShift) to cover a wide range of class prevalence scenarios, and the model is trained end-to-end with a quantification-oriented loss. Experiments on three benchmarks: EMG Data for Gestures, SmartFallMM, and UCI-HAR show that CC-GMNet-TS achieves lower error across the three benchmarks compared to traditional aggregators and recent deep quantifiers, while ablations confirm the contributions of both the Transformer backbone and class-conditioned mixtures during PShift.

</details>


### [27] [Spiking Neural Networks for Continuous Control: Neuromorphic Reinforcement Learning in Conventional Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22729)
*Jessica Hunter,Md Maruf Hossain Shuvo,Krishna Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SANSAC（Spiking Actor Network Soft Actor Critic），一种基于脉冲神经网络（SNN）的演员网络变体，用于连续控制任务的强化学习。作者在传统硬件上将SANSAC与标准SAC进行了系统性对比验证，结果表明两者性能近似，证明了SNN在连续RL框架中的可行性，为未来神经形态硬件上的RL研究建立了基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管RL算法在过去十年取得了显著进展，但将RL部署到神经形态硬件上用于连续控制任务仍缺乏充分验证。核心问题在于：在硬件特定优势尚未显现之前，用SNN替换传统演员网络是否会影响智能体性能。目前缺乏对最小化且神经形态可行的SNN演员变体的系统性基准测试。

Method: 提出SANSAC框架，即一种最小化、可在神经形态硬件上实现的脉冲演员网络变体，基于Soft Actor-Critic（SAC）架构。在传统计算机上将SANSAC与标准SAC进行对比实验，并研究了隐藏维度（隐藏层尺寸）对性能的影响。

Result: SANSAC与传统SAC在连续控制任务中表现出近等效的性能，同时SNN算法在复杂连续环境中展现出可行性，其性能在传统计算机上与传统神经网络具有竞争力。研究还分析了隐藏维度对性能的影响。

Conclusion: SNN在连续RL框架中具有可行性，SANSAC为未来神经形态硬件上的RL研究提供了基线和基础，证明了基于SNN的算法在连续环境中可达到与传统方法相当的性能水平。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms have made strides over the past decade applying them to a wide range of problems and control tasks. However, the deployment of RL on neuromorphic hardware for continuous control tasks remains under-validated. Namely it is unclear whether replacing a conventional actor network with a spiking neural network (SNN) affects the performance of an agent before any hardware-specific benefits manifest. We provide a systematic validation of a minimal, neuromorphically viable spiking actor variant of Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) on conventional hardware, establishing a baseline for future neuromorphic RL research. In this paper, we propose the Spiking Actor Network Soft Actor Critic (SANSAC) to address the use of RL frameworks in continuous environments, designed as a framework that can be implemented on neuromorphic hardware. We compare a traditional Soft Actor Critic (SAC) network to SANSAC in a traditional computer. We demonstrate the near equivalent performance of SANSAC and SAC, while addressing the impact of hidden dimensions. Our results demonstrate the viability of SNN based algorithms in complex continuous environments, as well as competitive performance to traditional neural networks in traditional computers, providing a basis to continue exploring the use of SNNs in continuous RL frameworks.

</details>


### [28] [Congruence Decomposition with Neural Block Solvers for Large-Scale PCI Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21485)
*Yeqing Qiu,Chengpiao Huang,Ye Xue,Akang Wang,Fan Xu,Zhipeng Jiang,Dong Zhang,Ruoyu Sun,Qingjiang Shi,Zhi-Quan Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于同余分解框架和神经模块求解器的大规模5G网络物理小区标识(PCI)分配方法。该方法利用PCI值的算术结构将多目标模块化干扰优化解耦为多个块级Min-k-Partition子问题，并结合图神经网络求解这些NP-hard子问题，通过条件期望舍入恢复离散分配。实验表明该方法在模块化干扰抑制、冲突消除和计算效率上均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着5G网络规模扩大，PCI复用不可避免，引发碰撞、混淆和多种形式的模块化干扰。联合缓解这些效应构成大规模多目标组合优化问题，在实用网络规模下难以高效求解。现有方法在模块化干扰抑制和计算效率上存在不足，亟需可扩展的高效PCI分配方案。

Method: 提出同余分解框架，利用PCI值的算术同余结构将多模块化干扰目标解耦为若干块级Min-k-Partition子问题，再用图着色过程解决PCI冲突。针对NP-hard的Min-k-Partition子问题，用图神经网络参数化其松弛二次规划形式，开发神经块求解器实现大规模高效优化，最后通过条件期望舍入恢复离散赋值并提供理论保证。

Result: 在合成蜂窝图和真实5G网络上的实验表明，所提方法在模块化干扰降低、冲突消除和计算效率方面均一致优于现有的模块化干扰感知基线方法。

Conclusion: 该同余分解与神经求解器相结合的框架为大规模PCI分配提供了一种高效且可扩展的解决方案，在性能上全面超越现有方法，具有良好的实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Physical Cell Identity (PCI) assignment is essential for interference management in dense 5G networks. As cellular networks scale, PCI reuse becomes unavoidable, which may cause collisions, confusions, and multiple forms of modular interference. Jointly mitigating these effects gives rise to a large-scale, multi-objective combinatorial optimization problem that is difficult to solve efficiently at practical network scales. In this work, we propose a congruence decomposition framework with neural block solvers for large-scale PCI assignment. The proposed decomposition exploits the arithmetic structure of PCI values to decouple multiple modular interference objectives into a collection of blockwise Min-$k$-Partition subproblems, followed by a graph coloring procedure to resolve PCI conflicts. For the resulting NP-hard Min-$k$-Partition subproblems, we develop neural block solvers by parameterizing their relaxed quadratic formulations with graph neural networks, enabling efficient optimization at large scales. Discrete assignments are recovered through conditional expectation rounding with theoretical guarantees. Experiments on synthetic cellular graphs and real-world 5G networks show that the proposed method consistently outperforms existing modular-interference-aware baselines in modular interference reduction, conflict elimination, and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [29] [KAN-Robust-Bench: A Benchmark for Evaluating the Robustness of Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21488)
*Mohammad Meymani,Roozbeh Razavi-Far*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多种Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）架构在面对强逃避攻击时的认证鲁棒性和经验鲁棒性。作者首先提供了随机平滑和区间边界传播的数学基础，并报告了在随机平滑下模型的ℓ2认证鲁棒性；随后系统评估了多种有防御和无防御的KAN模型在FGSM、PGD和C&W攻击下的鲁棒性，以确定最优防御策略和架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在许多领域表现优异，但面对对抗性威胁时展现出严重漏洞。逃避攻击（evasion）是最突出的攻击类别，攻击者生成人眼不可见的扰动样本即可高置信度地欺骗模型，构成严重的安全威胁。因此需要系统研究KAN模型在面对此类攻击时的鲁棒性问题。

Method: 本文首先给出了随机平滑（randomized smoothing）和区间边界传播（interval bound propagation）的数学基础，用于获取模型的ℓ2认证鲁棒性保证；然后系统地评估了多种有防御（defended）和无防御（undefended）的KAN架构在FGSM、PGD和C&W三类强逃避攻击下的经验鲁棒性，通过比较不同防御策略和架构来寻找最优方案。

Result: 报告了KAN模型在随机平滑下的ℓ2认证鲁棒性结果，并通过系统性实验评估了多种KAN架构在FGSM、PGD和C&W攻击下的经验鲁棒性，给出了不同防御策略和架构的性能对比。

Conclusion: 通过认证鲁棒性与经验鲁棒性的双重评估，本文得出了针对KAN模型的最优防御策略和架构选择结论，为KAN模型在实际安全敏感场景中的部署提供了鲁棒性参考。

Abstract: While machine learning models have demonstrated strong performance in many domains, these models have shown profound vulnerabilities when they are exposed to adversarial threats. While adversarial attacks fall into various categories, the most prominent category in research studies is evasion. In evasion attacks, the adversary generates perturbed versions of samples, which might not be observable by human eyes. These samples generally fool the machine learning models with high confidence. This phenomenon poses a significant security violation against machine learning models. In this paper, we investigate the certified and empirical robustness of various Kolmogorov-Arnold network architectures against strong evasion attacks. At first, we provide the mathematical foundations for randomized smoothing and interval bound propagation, and report the $\ell_2$-certified robustness of the models under randomized smoothing. After that, we systematically evaluate the robustness of various defended and undefended KAN models under FGSM, PGD, and C&W attacks in order to find out the optimal defense strategies and architectures.

</details>


### [30] [The geometry of AI validation: Exact certification limits for iid best-of-N search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21496)
*Ricardo Fitas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将验证与部署规则表示为可靠性表面上的核函数，其跨度几何将"复制（减少采样噪声）"与"新的干预方向（减少结构性盲区）"分离，并针对iid best-of-N搜索给出精确的歧义宽度公式，进而提出两门（two-gate）审计规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统越来越多地生成备选方案、检查证据并部署选定输出，其验证是目标相对的：证据只在产生它的干预所解析的方向上证明部署有效。现有方法未能精确刻画"复制已有干预"与"探索新干预方向"之间的本质区别，缺乏在给定有限观测下可靠性估计歧义宽度的精确理论。

Method: 将验证与部署规则表示为可靠性表面上的核函数，利用其跨度几何分离复制（降低采样噪声）与新干预方向（降低结构性盲区）。对iid best-of-N搜索，在标量排序、随机打结、最大选择、有界二元真理及稳定的秩-真理关系假设下，推导出通过n=m的best-of-n可靠性估计到n=N时剩余歧义宽度B_{m,N}=1+2∑_{r=1}^{m}(-1)^r cos^{2N}{rπ/[2(m+1)]}。证明显式有界世界可达整个区间，且完整前缀在限于n≤m的可靠性均值审计中是信息最大化的。通过单调性给出精确的一致逼近前沿，通过Lipschitz界给出精确的封顶尾部对偶与阶最优的L/m²歧义。

Result: 关键尺度为m²/N：当m与√N成比例时歧义约为0.83，而达到宽度ε需要m阶为√(N log(1/ε))。据此提出两门审计规则：先建立结构覆盖，再添加独立任务以提升精度。在数学推理与代码选择的回顾性研究中，构造了宽分离的兼容部署值，显示冻结在82个发现任务上的分数尾部审计规则显著降低了留出误差。

Conclusion: 对于iid best-of-N搜索，验证目标相对性的核表示给出了精确的可计算歧义界限与尺度律。两门审计规则为实践提供了操作性指导。但该几何仅适用于已知或独立估计的核函数，对iid搜索之外的情形适用性有限；经验分析为说明性回顾而非前瞻性干预。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly generate alternatives, inspect evidence, and deploy a selected output. Validation is therefore target-relative: evidence certifies deployment only in directions resolved by the interventions that produced it. We represent validation and deployment rules as kernels over a reliability surface. Their span geometry separates replication, which reduces sampling noise, from new intervention directions, which reduce structural blindness. We make this principle exact for iid best-of-$N$ search. Under scalar ranking, randomized ties, maximum selection, bounded binary truth, and a stable rank-truth relation, knowing best-of-$n$ reliability through $n=m$ leaves exact ambiguity width $B_{m,N}=1+2\sum_{r=1}^{m}(-1)^r\cos^{2N}{rπ/[2(m+1)]}$. Explicit bounded worlds attain the entire interval, and the complete prefix is information-maximal among reliability-mean audits confined to $n\le m$. The governing scale is $m^2/N$: when $m$ is proportional to $\sqrt{N}$, ambiguity remains about 0.83, while width $\varepsilon$ requires $m$ of order $\sqrt{N\log(1/\varepsilon)}$. Monotonicity gives an exact uniform-approximation frontier; a Lipschitz bound gives an exact capped-tail dual and order-sharp $L/m^2$ ambiguity. These results yield a two-gate audit rule: establish structural coverage, then add independent tasks for precision. Retrospective studies of mathematical reasoning and code selection construct compatible deployment values with wide separation and show that a score-tail audit rule frozen on 82 discovery tasks substantially reduces held-out error. Beyond iid search, the geometry applies only to known or independently estimated kernels; the empirical analyses are illustrative rather than prospective interventions.

</details>


### [31] [Selection of Heart Sound Segments for Synchronous Classification of Multi-channel Heart Sounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21499)
*Marcelo Nogueira,Jorge H. Oliveira,Carlos F. Ferreira,Miguel T. Coimbra,Alípio M. Jorge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种同步多通道心音分析方法，通过段选择算法从四个听诊区选取最优心音段，并输入多输入CNN进行分类，在CirCor DigiScope数据集上达到96.5%的准确率，比最佳单通道和异步多通道方法高出9.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏听诊是心血管疾病最具成本效益的筛查手段，需要听取四个主要听诊区。然而现有自动心音分析算法要么使用单通道，要么使用多通道但逐个独立分析，没有工作遵循医生的实际流程——同步分析多通道心音。作者希望探究同步多通道分析是否优于单通道和异步多通道方法，以捕捉通道间干扰现象。

Method: 提出一个心音段选择算法，从四个听诊区各识别出最优心音段，然后将四个选段同步输入多输入CNN（multi-input CNN）进行分类，实现同步多通道的患者分类。

Result: 在735名来自CirCor DigiScope数据集、具有四个听诊区完整记录的患者上进行实验，同步方法达到96.5%的总体准确率，比最佳的単通道和异步多通道方法高出9.1%（绝对增益）。段选择策略相比随机选择通过配对统计显著性检验（p=0.003）证明了其有效性。

Conclusion: 同步多通道心音分析优于单通道和异步多通道方法，基于段选择算法与多输入CNN的组合能够更有效地利用四个听诊区的信息进行患者分类。作者讨论了结果的适用范围与泛化性，及其它方法学考量。

Abstract: Cardiac auscultation remains the most cost-effective screening procedure for cardiovascular diseases, and requires listening at the four main auscultation spots. Despite this, automatic heart sound analysis algorithms mostly classify patients using a single heart sound (single-channel), or, when using more than one (multi-channel), analyze each channel individually. To our knowledge, no prior work classifies patients through the synchronous analysis of multi-channel heart sounds, following the procedure used by physicians. This motivates us to study whether synchronous multi-channel analysis outperforms single-channel approaches, and whether it holds an advantage over asynchronous multi-channel methods that analyze channels one by one, potentially by capturing inter-channel interference phenomena. To answer these questions, we introduce a selection algorithm that identifies optimal heart sound segments from each of the four auscultation spots, which are then fed into a multi-input CNN that classifies patients by analyzing the four selected sounds simultaneously. Our synchronous approach, combining the proposed selection algorithm with a multi-input CNN, achieves a superior overall accuracy of 96.5\%, a 9.1\% gain over the best-performing single-channel and asynchronous multi-channel methods. The benefit of the proposed segment selection strategy over random selection is confirmed by a paired statistical significance test ($p = 0.003$). These results were obtained on 735 patients from the CirCor DigiScope dataset with complete recordings from all four spots, and their scope and generalizability are discussed in light of this and other methodological considerations.

</details>


### [32] [ChemDIRT: A Diversified Instruction, Representation, and Task Benchmark for Robust Chemistry-LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21504)
*Eric Inae,Tim Gunn,Chris Bond,Meng Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ChemDIRT，一个用于评估大语言模型在化学推理中鲁棒性的综合基准框架，通过系统化地对指令和分子表示进行扰动，覆盖八类化学任务来评估模型的准确性和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有化学基准仅关注有限任务集，忽视了模型对问题表述和化学表征变化的鲁棒性，导致报告的性能可能高估模型在真实场景中一致推理的能力。

Method: ChemDIRT通过系统化地施加指令变化和分子表示变化，覆盖八类化学任务，同时评估模型在受控扰动下的准确性和一致性。

Result: 对多种开源和闭源LLM的基准测试揭示出显著的提示敏感性、表示依赖性以及任务族之间不均衡的性能表现。

Conclusion: ChemDIRT相比传统单格式基准能提供更可靠的模型推理能力评估，证明了LLM在化学推理中缺乏跨变体的一致性。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs) has led to increasing interest in their application to scientific domains such as chemistry. However, existing chemistry benchmarks often provide only a narrow view of model capability, focusing on limited task sets while overlooking robustness to variations in problem formulation and chemical representation. As a result, reported performance may overestimate a model's true ability to reason consistently across realistic settings. To address this challenge, we introduce ChemDIRT (Diversified Instruction, Representation, and Task Benchmark), a comprehensive evaluation framework designed to assess the robustness of chemical reasoning in LLMs. ChemDIRT systematically measures model performance across variations in instructions and molecular representations while spanning eight categories of chemistry tasks. By evaluating both accuracy and consistency under these controlled perturbations, ChemDIRT provides a more reliable assessment of model reasoning capabilities than conventional single-format benchmarks. We benchmark a diverse set of open- and closed-source LLMs, revealing substantial prompt sensitivity, representation dependence, and uneven performance across task families.

</details>


### [33] [Multimodal Injury Risk and Performance Prediction in Tennis Using Weighted Ensemble Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21530)
*Weihao Qu,Dongyang Wang,Ling Zheng,Francisco E. Alvarez,Shobharani Polasa,Jiacun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为PART的多模态加权集成学习框架，用于监测网球运动员的健康状况并预测近期受伤风险。该框架整合了生理指标、可穿戴设备数据、问卷、垂直跳跃评估和比赛视频运动分析等多种异构数据源，通过专门的机器学习和深度学习模型提取整体健康状况、受伤风险、身体能力和打球风格四个特征，并采用监督加权集成策略自适应地为每个预测模型分配权重。在九名大学网球运动员上的评估显示了良好的性能，并展示了扩展到业余运动员的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动员表现和受伤风险预测研究受限于数据可用性以及对主观观察和专家评估的依赖。虽然足球、篮球和摔跤等运动已开始整合可穿戴设备等多模态数据源，但类似方法在网球运动中尚未得到充分探索，需要一种多模态框架来更准确地监测运动员健康并预测受伤风险。

Method: PART框架采用多模态加权集成学习策略：(1)整合生理指标、训练和比赛数据、可穿戴设备睡眠信息、自我报告问卷、垂直跳跃评估和比赛视频运动分析等多样化输入；(2)利用专门的机器学习和深度学习模型从这些模态中独立提取四个运动员特征：整体健康、受伤风险、身体能力和打球风格；(3)通过监督加权集成策略，根据每个模型的可信度自适应分配权重，以处理多模态数据组合的复杂性。

Result: 在九名大学网球运动员的多模态数据上评估，PART在监测运动员健康和估计近期受伤易感性方面表现出强劲性能。此外，该框架对休闲网球运动员也显示出应用前景，能够提供个性化见解以降低受伤风险并优化表现。

Conclusion: PART作为一种多模态加权集成框架，有效克服了单一模态和主观评估在运动员健康监测中的局限，在网球这一此前未被充分探索的运动领域实现了可靠的健康监测和受伤风险预估，并具有向更广泛人群扩展的潜力。

Abstract: Machine learning has had a positive impact on the sports industry, with one of its most promising applications being the prediction of athlete performance and injury risk. Recent advances have employed state-of-the-art models to improve prediction accuracy, yet progress remains limited by data availability and the reliance on subjective observations or expert assessments. To address these limitations, researchers in sports such as soccer, basketball, and wrestling have begun integrating heterogeneous data sources, such as wearable device readings, with traditional subjective assessments. However, similar multimodal approaches remain underexplored in tennis. In this work, we propose a multimodal weighted ensemble learning framework, Predictive Athlete Readiness for Tennis (PART), to monitor athlete wellness and estimate near-term injury risk in tennis players. PART processes a wide range of inputs, including physiological metrics, training and match data, sleep information from wearable devices, self-reported questionnaires, vertical jump assessments, and motion analysis from match-play videos. From these modalities, specialized machine learning and deep learning models independently extract four athlete-specific characteristics: overall wellness, injury risk, physical capability, and playing style. To overcome the complexity of combining these diverse modalities, PART employs a supervised weighted ensemble integration strategy, assigning adaptive weights to each predictive model based on its reliability. Evaluation of multimodal data collected from nine collegiate tennis players demonstrates that PART achieves strong performance in monitoring athlete wellness and estimating near-term injury susceptibility. Beyond collegiate athletes, the framework also shows promise for recreational tennis players, offering personalized insights to mitigate injury risk and optimize performance.

</details>


### [34] [Federated Continual Learning as a Distributed Drift-Plus-Penalty Control Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21539)
*Nazreen Shah,Naveen Kumar Reddy Somireddy,Zubair Shaban,Ranjitha Prasad,B. N. Bharath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedQCL，一个基于Lyapunov漂移加惩罚（DPP）优化的联邦持续学习框架，通过虚拟队列显式追踪遗忘累积，实现对稳定性-可塑性权衡的可控调节，在多个基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦持续学习（FCL）中的遗忘本质上是长期、分布式的现象，由任务时间演化与客户端间异构性交互产生，但现有方法将CL视为本地逐任务优化序列，仅通过重放、正则化或投影约束等启发式机制间接处理，未对分布式遗忘进行显式调控。

Method: 将FCL形式化为随机控制问题，提出Federated Queue-regulated Continual Learning（FedQCL），基于Lyapunov漂移加惩罚（DPP）优化。引入虚拟队列追踪任务和客户端间的遗忘累积，显式控制稳定性-可塑性权衡。通过优化DPP目标，在提升当前任务性能的同时，使用单一参数提供可解释、可调谐的平衡机制，无需梯度投影或额外通信开销。

Result: 在Split-CIFAR-10、Split-CIFAR-100和Split-TinyImageNet标准基准上的实验表明，FedQCL在异构数据分布下相比最先进基线在准确率上表现更优，同时显著降低遗忘。

Conclusion: 将FCL重新形式化为随机控制问题并引入队列调节机制，能够显式、可解释地管理分布式长期遗忘，为联邦持续学习提供了新的理论视角和实用框架。

Abstract: Federated Continual Learning (FCL) is fundamental to real-world distributed learning systems, requiring models to adapt to sequential, non-IID data across clients while mitigating catastrophic forgetting and client drift. Existing approaches formulate continual learning (CL) as a sequence of per-task optimization problems, applied locally at each client and coupled through aggregation, using heuristic mechanisms such as replay, regularization, or projection-based constraints. However, forgetting in FCL is inherently a long-term, distributed phenomenon, arising from the interaction of temporal task evolution and cross-client heterogeneity, which is not explicitly regulated. In this work, we cast FCL as a stochastic control problem and propose Federated Queue-regulated Continual Learning (FedQCL), a framework based on Lyapunov drift-plus-penalty (DPP) optimization. FedQCL introduces virtual queues to track the accumulation of forgetting across tasks and clients, enabling explicit control of the stability-plasticity trade-off. By optimizing a DPP objective, the method jointly improves current-task performance while the queue-based formulation provides an interpretable and tunable mechanism to balance adaptation and retention through a single parameter, without requiring gradient projection or additional communication overhead. Empirical evaluations on standard benchmarks, including Split-CIFAR-10, Split-CIFAR-100, and Split-TinyImageNet, demonstrate that FedQCL outperforms state-of-the-art baselines with respect to accuracy while significantly reducing forgetting under heterogeneous data distributions.

</details>

*Chiwun Yang,Xiaoyu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑"注意力图大权重即代表可压缩性"的常见观点，指出需要分别考察全局分数差距、被省略值的加权和以及任务目标，并提出训练免费的KV缓存压缩器CertKV，通过每头保留一个尾部汇总槽位并按值离散度分配其余预算来实现高效压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩大多以注意力图中少量大权重为依据，但这并不充分：大权重未必包含大部分质量，被省略的值可能相互抵消，且保留注意力输出也不等于保留任务性能。作者希望厘清影响可压缩性的真正因素。

Method: 提出三个分离的视角：全局分数差距（而非阈值计数）决定保留目标质量所需的token数；对已实现的行，省略值的加权和是精确的缺失统计量；用受控的"检索-聚合"模型解释截断何时有益何时有害。基于此设计CertKV：训练自由，为每个注意力头保留一个尾部汇总槽位，其余预算按值离散度分配。

Result: 在匹配预算下，CertKV在LongBench-v2的九项设置中进入前两名七次；在128K RULER上保持领先压缩档位；在打包的Llama原型中实现10倍缓存预算。

Conclusion: 可压缩性取决于质量（mass）、值、未来查询和任务本身，而非仅凭稀疏的外观图谱。

Abstract: KV-cache compression is often justified by attention maps with a few large weights. This is incomplete: large weights may not contain most of the mass, omitted values can cancel, and preserving the attention output may not preserve the task. We separate these questions. Global score gaps -- not threshold counts -- determine how many tokens are needed to retain a target mass. For a realized row, the weighted sum of omitted values is the exact missing statistic. A controlled retrieval--aggregation model explains when truncation helps and when it hurts. These results motivate CertKV, a training-free compressor that reserves one tail-summary slot per head and allocates the rest by value dispersion. Under matched budgets, CertKV is top-two in seven of nine LongBench-v2 settings, remains in the leading compressed tier on 128K RULER, and realizes a ten-fold cache budget in a packed Llama prototype. Compressibility depends on the mass, values, future queries, and task -- not on a sparse-looking map alone.

</details>


### [36] [Loss-Parameterized Fisher Width Along Learning Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21561)
*Vu Khac Ky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Fisher宽度沿学习轨迹的演化，探讨训练损失能否作为该量的有效坐标。作者推导了精确的迹-形状分解和确定性的稳定性界；在群体高斯教师逻辑回归模型中证明了教师对齐态在每条低于log 2的损失水平上的极值性，并证明群体梯度流渐近选择该分支。在高维极限下得到w_F/√L收敛到常数的显式结果。实验支持分支式的损失参数化而非普适的那种。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fisher宽度衡量探测集在局部Fisher几何变形下的高斯宽度，是理解深度学习中参数空间几何特性的重要量。然而其沿学习轨迹的演化规律及与训练损失的对应关系尚未明确，研究者希望探究训练损失能否作为Fisher宽度的一个有效坐标。

Method: 首先推导Fisher宽度的精确迹-形状分解和确定性稳定性界；然后在群体高斯教师逻辑回归模型中分析教师对齐状态的极值性（最小参数范数，最大化Fisher迹和欧氏球Fisher宽度），证明群体梯度流对该分支的渐近选择及显式收敛率；最后通过全Fisher实验和非线性MLP（对角模型Fisher逼近）比较GD/SGD/Adam在匹配损失下的行为差异。

Result: 对于d≥2，得到极限关系w_F(B_2^d;θ(t))/√L(θ(t)) → (√6/π)E[χ_{d-1}]。全Fisher实验支持匹配损失分支和群体预测；在非线性MLP中，GD和SGD在匹配损失下保持接近，而Adam则明显偏离该分支，但固定探测集的时间形状保持高度相似。

Conclusion: 结果支持Fisher宽度采用分支式（branchwise）而非普适的（universal）损失参数化。训练损失并非在所有优化器中都能作为Fisher宽度的统一有效坐标，其有效性取决于优化器所选择的分支。

Abstract: Fisher width measures the Gaussian width of a probe set after deformation by the local Fisher geometry. We study its evolution along learning trajectories and ask when training loss can serve as an effective coordinate for this quantity.
  We first derive an exact trace--shape factorization and a deterministic stability bound for fixed compact probes. In a population Gaussian-teacher logistic model, the teacher-aligned state is extremal on every loss level below $\log 2$: it has minimal parameter norm and maximizes both Fisher trace and Euclidean-ball Fisher width. We then show that population gradient flow asymptotically selects this branch, with explicit rates for the aligned and orthogonal coordinates. This yields, for $d\geq2$, \[
  \frac{w_F(B_2^d;θ(t))}
  {\sqrt{L(θ(t))}}
  \longrightarrow
  \frac{\sqrt6}π\mathbb E[χ_{d-1}]. \] Controlled full-Fisher experiments support the matched-loss branch and the population predictions. In a nonlinear MLP with a diagonal model-Fisher approximation, GD and SGD remain close at matched loss, whereas Adam follows a substantially displaced branch; the fixed probes tested retain highly similar temporal shapes. These results support a branchwise, rather than universal, loss parametrization of Fisher width.

</details>


### [37] [Anchoring Bias: A Persistent Fairness Backdoor Attack against MLLMs under Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21577)
*Yuyang Luo,Kai Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出持续公平性后门攻击（PFBA），针对多模态大语言模型（MLLM），通过潜在空间公平性强化和持续学习模拟两种机制，注入在持续学习后仍能存活的群体特异性歧视后门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM在高风险领域部署中对公平性要求日益重要，且模型需通过持续学习不断更新。已有工作表明后门攻击会因后续持续学习更新而退化，但针对公平性的后门能否在持续学习中存活这一问题尚未被探索。两个关键问题待回答：后门能否可靠地诱导MLLM产生公平性违规，以及此类公平性定向后门能否在持续学习中持久存在。

Method: PFBA通过两种新颖机制实现：1）潜在空间公平性强化（Latent Space Fairness Reinforcement）——重塑模型深层特征几何，锚定特权群体表征以保留效用，同时排斥和聚拢目标群体表征以维持歧视；2）持续学习模拟（Continual Learning Simulation）——迭代优化触发器以应对模拟参数漂移，确保后门在未来更新中持续存在。

Result: 大量实验表明PFBA能诱导严重的公平性差异，且这些差异在持续学习多轮后依然存在，并能规避标准后门防御手段。

Conclusion: PFBA首次揭示了公平性定向后门攻击可以在持续学习条件下持久存活于MLLM中，且难以被常规防御检测，为MLLM安全部署中的公平性风险提供了新的警示。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes domains where fairness is a critical safety requirement. In practice, these models are continually updated through continual learning (CL) to adapt to evolving tasks and data distributions. Prior work has shown that backdoor attacks can manipulate MLLM responses through hidden triggers, but naively implanted backdoors degrade as models undergo subsequent updates of CL. Although fairness has emerged as a central concern for MLLM deployment, whether backdoor-induced fairness violations can survive CL remains unexplored, leaving two critical questions unanswered: (1) whether a backdoor can reliably induce fairness violations in MLLMs, and (2) whether such fairness-targeted backdoors can persist through continual learning. We bridge this gap by proposing Persistent Fairness Backdoor Attack (PFBA) to inject persistent and group-specific discrimination into MLLMs. Specifically, PFBA achieves this through two novel mechanisms. The Latent Space Fairness Reinforcement reshapes the model's deep feature geometry by anchoring privileged-group representations to preserve utility while repelling and clustering targeted-group representations to sustain discrimination, and the Continual Learning Simulation iteratively optimizes the trigger against simulated parameter drift to ensure backdoor persistence across future updates. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PFBA induces severe fairness disparities that persist across continual learning rounds, evading standard backdoor defenses. The data and code are publicly available at https://github.com/lyygua/PFBA.

</details>


### [38] [Sorting from Counterexamples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21579)
*Noga Alon,Shay Moran,Shlomo Moran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带噪声的线性序学习问题：学习者每次猜测一个完整的物品排序，系统返回正确确认或一对逆序反例，其中最多k个反例可能是不可信的（k未知）。作者确定了最优查询复杂度为Θ(n log n + nk)，即无噪声时与经典排序复杂度匹配，每个不可信反例额外带来O(n)成本。上界基于排列的几何表示和Grünbaum定理，下界结合排序论证与Condorcet式构造。此外，对于低维几何表示（点投影排序）的排名类别，给出O(d² log n + dk)的上界和Ω(d log n + dk)的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在反例可能包含不可信（对抗性噪声）信息时，如何高效学习未知的线性排序；特别关注噪声数量k未知的情况，目标是确定最优查询复杂度，并探索具有低维几何结构的排名类别能否获得更优的复杂度。

Method: 上界采用排列的几何表示，将每个排列对应到某个几何对象，利用Grünbaum定理来处理带噪声的反例；下界结合了传统排序的必要性论证与Condorcet型的构造来证明噪声项nk的必要性。对低维情形，利用d维空间中的几何表示分别给出上下界。

Result: 确定了带最多k个不可信反例的线性序学习问题的最优查询复杂度为Θ(n log n + nk)。对低维几何表示类别，给出上界O(d² log n + dk)和下界Ω(d log n + dk)，在无噪声项上存在因子d的差距。

Conclusion: 无噪声时复杂度与经典排序一致（n log n），而每个不可信反例在最优情形下需要额外花费O(n)次查询。低维几何表示类别能显著降低复杂度（从O(nk)降至O(dk)），但无噪声部分仍存在因子d的开区间待填补。

Abstract: Consider the following problem of learning an unknown linear order on $n$ items. In each round, the learner guesses a complete ordering of the items and receives either confirmation that the guess is correct or a counterexample: a pair of items in the wrong order. The goal is to identify the unknown order using as few queries as possible. We study this problem when up to $k$ of the returned counterexamples may be untruthful, where $k$ is not known in advance. We determine the optimal query complexity up to constant factors: \[
  Θ(n\log n + nk). \] Thus, while the noiseless complexity matches the classical complexity of sorting, each untruthful counterexample incurs an additional cost of order $n$. The upper bound is based on a geometric representation of permutations and Grünbaum's theorem, while the lower bound combines sorting arguments with a Condorcet-type construction. We also study the case where the target ranking has a low-dimensional geometric representation: each item is represented by a point in $\mathbb{R}^d$, and the ranking is obtained by projecting the points onto an unknown direction. For these classes we give an upper bound of $O(d^2\log n+dk)$ and a lower bound of $Ω(d\log n+dk)$, leaving a factor of $d$ gap in the noiseless term.

</details>


### [39] [Reading the Room: Implicit Confusion Encoding in Recurrent World Model States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21582)
*Donald Aadithiyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现基于RSSM架构的世界模型（如DreamerV3）中，循环隐藏状态h_t不仅编码预测信息，还隐式跟踪自身的"困惑度"（confusion）。该信号几乎与h_t最大方差方向正交，无法被基于方差的方法检测到，且与集成分歧（标记新输入）和重建误差（标记当前坏预测）功能不同。在线性探针上AUROC达0.72，且该信号是可被因果使用的（编辑h_t可改变行为）。该发现及几何/闭式解在三个控制任务中泛化，但关键的区分性测试仅在一个任务中严格成立，实际应用（决定何时检查现实而非依赖想象）仅在两个任务中泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RSSM架构的世界模型（如DreamerV3）中，隐藏状态h_t仅以降低预测误差为目标训练。先前方法（方差、集成分歧、重建误差）均未揭示h_t隐含的"困惑度"信号，作者希望发现并验证这一隐藏信号的存在、功能性及因果性。

Method: 使用线性探针检测h_t中正交于高方差方向的困惑信号；设计固定预测误差、变化困惑度的分离测试；用打折的高误差步计数解释探针输出（R²=0.80）；通过直接编辑h_t（包括用其他轨迹的真实值替代合成编辑）验证因果性；跨三个控制任务检验泛化性。

Result: 线性探针AUROC达0.72（5次运行），集成基线低于偶然水平；打折计数解释探针输出的80%方差（R²=0.80）；确认信号是因果使用的而非仅存在；几何结构和闭式解在三个控制任务中泛化，但严格分离测试仅在一个任务成立，实际使用（判断何时检查现实而非想象）仅在两个任务成立。

Conclusion: h_t中隐藏着一个可泛化、可因果使用的"困惑度"信号，与现有信号（方差、集成分歧、重建误差）在功能上分离。但该信号及其实用性跨任务的鲁棒性有限——核心分离测试和实际应用并非在所有任务上稳定泛化，提示其可靠性存在边界。

Abstract: World models built on the RSSM architecture, such as DreamerV3, keep a recurrent hidden state $h_t$ trained only to reduce prediction error. We show this state also tracks its own confusion, hiding in plain sight: nearly orthogonal to $h_t$'s directions of greatest variance, invisible to any variance-based method. It is functionally distinct from ensemble disagreement, which flags new inputs, and reconstruction error, which flags bad predictions right now. On a test holding prediction error fixed while confusion varies, a linear probe on $h_t$ finds the signal (AUROC 0.72, 5 runs), while an ensemble baseline scores below chance. A discounted count of recent high-error steps explains 80% of the probe's output ($R^2=0.80$). We confirm the signal is causally used, not merely present, by editing $h_t$ directly and watching behaviour change, including a check using real values from other trajectories instead of synthetic edits. Its geometry and closed form generalize across three control tasks; the decisive dissociation test itself holds cleanly on only one, and its practical use, deciding when to check reality instead of trusting imagination, generalizes to only two of the three tasks.

</details>


### [40] [Predicting Early Functional Decline from Longitudinal Laboratory and Vital Sign Trajectories: A Large-Scale Study Using the All of Us Research Program](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21589)
*Rashmita Kudamala,Aravind V. Kuruvikkattil,Lalitha Pranathi Pulavarthy,Saptarshi Purkayastha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用All of Us研究计划中297,861名参与者（11.1%为功能衰退病例）的常规生物标志物纵向轨迹特征，在功能衰退前3年窗口内预测其临床前阶段。LightGBM模型结合轨迹特征显著优于静态实验室摘要（AUROC 0.797 vs. 0.755），并在年龄性别匹配分析及3-12个月时间窗口分析中均展现出稳健的独立预测信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人功能衰退通常在跌倒或步态障碍显现后才被识别，错过了预防干预的关键窗口。常规诊疗中已记录但很少被纵向分析的生物标志物数据，可能蕴含临床前阶段的可识别信号，为早期发现和预防提供机会。

Method: 研究采用回顾性队列设计，基于All of Us研究计划数据（N=297,861，11.1%为病例），对十二种生物标志物在3年前期窗口内提取轨迹特征（斜率、变异度、差值、均值）。构建LightGBM机器学习模型，并与静态实验室摘要模型对比；同时进行1:1年龄和性别匹配分析以及时间视野分析验证模型稳健性。

Result: 轨迹增强模型显著优于静态模型（AUROC 0.797 vs. 0.755，DeLong p<0.001；AUPRC 0.380 vs. 0.304）；年龄性别匹配分析确认独立轨迹信号（AUROC 0.727 vs. 仅人口学特征0.680）；时间视野分析显示在功能衰退前3-12个月均能持续预测（AUROC 0.768-0.740）。

Conclusion: 仅利用常规诊疗中已采集的实验室测量数据，即可被动、零负担地整合到电子健康记录系统中，实现对临床前功能衰退的早期检测，为预防干预争取宝贵时间窗口。

Abstract: Functional decline in older adults is typically recognized only after falls or observable gait impairment, closing the window for prevention. We investigated whether temporal trajectories of routine biomarkers, already recorded but rarely analyzed longitudinally, can identify patients in the pre-clinical phase of mobility decline. Using the All of Us Research Program (N = 297,861; 11.1% cases), we derived trajectory features (slope, variability, delta, mean) for twelve biomarkers over a three-year pre-index window. LightGBM models incorporating trajectories significantly outperformed static laboratory summaries (AUROC 0.797 vs. 0.755; DeLong p < 0.001; AUPRC 0.380 vs. 0.304). A 1:1 age- and sex-matched analysis confirmed an independent trajectory signal (AUROC 0.727 vs. demographics-only 0.680). A horizon analysis demonstrated sustained prediction 3-12 months before decline onset (AUROC 0.768-0.740). Because the model uses only measurements already ordered in routine care, it supports passive, zero-burden EHR integration for early detection of pre-clinical functional decline.

</details>


### [41] [Reaching the Tail: Calibration Diversity Drives Conformal Coverage under Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21591)
*Donald Aadithiyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究宏观经济长序列下的多视界稀有事件预测问题，发现自适应共形推断（ACI）的表观稀有事件阈值实为校准集大小所致。在200个随机校准集上，非一致性分数分布的支持宽度解释了高达85%的覆盖率方差（稀有事件数量仅解释2%），并在合成条件与五国数据中复现。基于此构建的多样性最大化选择器是唯一能在长视界下提升覆盖率（六个月从67.8%提升至81.4%）的策略，而Mondrian、shift-robust和极值方法均失败。作者提出紧凑命题解释：覆盖率缺口反映校准集上分位数与测试分布的接近程度，多样性是必要而非充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宏观经济长序列中多视界稀有事件（如衰退）预测面临双重挑战：标注稀有事件数量极少，且标准的共形不确定性量化假设可交换性，而自相关数据违反该假设。这导致不确定性量化在长视界下覆盖率严重不足，缺乏可靠的校准策略。

Method: 通过受控消融实验分离了稀有事件数量与校准集大小的混淆效应；在200个随机校准集上分析非一致性分数分布特性（支持宽度vs稀有事件计数）对覆盖率方差的可解释比例；构造多样性最大化选择器，并与Mondrian、shift-robust和极值方法对比；提出理论命题解释覆盖率缺口与校准集上分位数-测试分布接近度的关系；在两阶段美国衰退预测框架（RegressorChain）上验证。

Result: ①支持宽度解释85%的覆盖率方差vs稀有事件计数仅解释2%，在合成条件和五国数据中一致复现（五国Spearman ρ 0.45-0.66 vs 0.02-0.23）；②多样性最大化选择器是唯一提升六个月覆盖率的方法（67.8%→81.4%）；③Mondrian在oracle标签下甚至恶化了覆盖率；④六个月覆盖率能否在诚实评分下达到90%仍是一个开放问题。

Conclusion: 稀有事件预测的覆盖缺口根源在于校准集上分位数与测试分布之间的接近程度，而非稀有事件本身的稀缺性。多样性是改善覆盖率的必要而非充分条件。该方法为宏观稀有事件的不确定性量化提供了新的校准思路，但95%置信水平下的诚实覆盖目标仍未达成。

Abstract: Multi-horizon rare-event forecasting is hard under long macroeconomic series' data constraints: labeled events are scarce, and standard uncertainty quantification assumes an exchangeability that autocorrelation violates. A controlled ablation shows an apparent rare-event threshold for Adaptive Conformal Inference instead reflects calibration-set size. Across 200 random calibration sets, support width of the nonconformity-score distribution explains up to 85% of coverage variance versus 2% for rare-event count; the same, not the same magnitude, replicates across synthetic conditions and five countries (five-country Spearman $ρ$ 0.45-0.66 vs. 0.02-0.23). A diversity-maximizing selector built on this is the only strategy tested that improves long-horizon coverage (67.8% to 81.4% at six months); Mondrian, shift-robust, and extreme-value alternatives fail to close it. Mondrian even worsens coverage under oracle labels. A compact proposition explains why: coverage deficit reflects how closely the calibration set's upper quantile reaches the test distribution's. Diversity is necessary, not sufficient. Demonstrated on a two-stage U.S. recession-forecasting framework with RegressorChain, whether six-month coverage reaches 90% under honest scoring remains open, a question this paper quantifies rather than resolves.

</details>


### [42] [Subzero matrix completion for sparse data analysis: large-scale learning of latent low-rank structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21607)
*Lawrence K. Saul,Ningyuan Huang,Dennis Bollweg,Jeff Soules,Diana C. Halikias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何从低秩实值矩阵中通过将负元素置零来恢复稀疏非负矩阵，提出了一种基于随机交替最小二乘的算法，该算法在稠密矩阵的小块上操作，避免了存储与乘积等大的稠密矩阵，并利用稀疏优化和自定义CUDA内核加速。作者将该算法应用于果蝇神经连接组（139,255×139,255的突触权重矩阵），揭示了其潜在的低秩结构能预测不同特异度水平下的细胞类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者希望探索稀疏非负矩阵与低秩结构之间的数学联系。此前算法虽然能通过因子上的交替最小化发现这类分解，但需要计算和存储一个与乘积等大的稠密矩阵，既非稀疏也非低秩，这严重限制了算法的可扩展性，无法处理大规模的稀疏矩阵。

Method: 开发了一种随机的交替最小二乘算法，它在稠密矩阵的较小数据块上操作，从而避免了存储完整的大规模稠密矩阵。此外，还结合了稀疏优化技术并编写了自定义的CUDA内核来进一步加速计算，使算法能够扩展到更大的问题规模。

Result: 将该算法应用于果蝇连接组的突触权重稀疏矩阵（139,255行×139,255列）。尽管该矩阵的奇异值频谱缓慢衰减，但其仍表现出潜在的低秩结构，且该结构在不同特异度水平下都能有效预测细胞类别。

Conclusion: 稀疏非负矩阵可以通过将低秩实值矩阵的负元素置零来恢复，这种分解在稀疏性与秩之间建立了数学联系。新提出的随机块式交替最小二乘算法显著提高了此类分解的计算可扩展性，并在真实的果蝇神经连接组数据上验证了其有效性与生物学意义。

Abstract: We investigate when a sparse nonnegative matrix can be recovered from a real-valued matrix of much lower rank by zeroing out its negative elements. The potential for such decompositions suggests a mathematical connection between sparsity and rank; we analyze a number of sparse matrices with this latent low-rank structure and use them to illustrate the geometric origins of this connection. Previous algorithms have discovered these decompositions via an alternating minimization over the factors of a low-rank matrix, but to do so, they have also needed to compute and store another matrix, neither sparse nor low-rank, that is the size of their product. We develop a stochastic, alternating least-squares algorithm that operates on smaller blocks of this dense matrix and scales as a result to much larger problems. We also show how to further accelerate this algorithm with sparse optimizations and customized CUDA kernels. As one example, we use the algorithm to analyze the sparse matrix of synaptic weights for the recently published $\textit{Drosphilia}$ connectome. The nonzero elements of this matrix, with 139,255 rows and columns, record the number of synapses between cells in the nervous system of a female fruit fly. Despite a slowly decaying spectrum of singular values, this matrix exhibits a latent low-rank structure that is predictive of cell categories across multiple levels of specificity.

</details>


### [43] [FrugalSOT - Frugal Search Over the Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21621)
*Pradheep P,Yuvanesh S,Harish KB,Keerthan Saai Reddy S,Joshva Devadas T,Naveenkumar J,Hemalatha K*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FrugalSOT是一种面向端侧设备（如树莓派5）的资源感知模型选择架构，通过估计请求复杂度并采用级联式模型路由策略，在保证输出相关性的同时显著降低推理时间和计算资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧NLP任务受限于嵌入式硬件的有限资源（如树莓派5），需要高效的推理策略来平衡推理效率与输出质量。

Method: FrugalSOT从请求中提取提示长度、命名实体密度和句法复杂度等特征来估计复杂度。请求先发送给最可能通过相关性阈值的最简单模型；若输出未达阈值，则升级到更复杂的模型。相关性阈值通过低通滤波机制在后台基于历史验证结果持续自适应更新，以适应变化的输入模式。

Result: 在树莓派5上的实验表明，相比单一模型基线方法，FrugalSOT显著降低了平均推理时间和总体计算资源使用，同时输出相关性与最复杂模型相当。

Conclusion: 自适应模型选择能够在资源受限的设备上实现高效且高质量的NLP推理。

Abstract: In on-device NLP tasks, limited resources of embedded hardware, such as the Raspberry Pi 5, require efficient inference strategies. This paper introduces FrugalSOT (Frugal Search Over The Models), a resource-aware model selection architecture for on-device NLP inference. FrugalSOT estimates each request's complexity by extracting features such as prompt length, named entity density, and syntactic complexity. The request is first made to the least complex model that is likely to pass a relevance threshold. If the output of that model falls short of the threshold, the request is made to a more complex model. It is important to note that the relevance threshold undergoes continuous updates in the background. using past validation outcomes in an adaptation process using a low-pass filtering mechanism, thus imparting adaptation to changing input patterns. Experimental results achieved on a Raspberry Pi 5 show that FrugalSOT reduces average inference time and overall computational resource use to a significant extent compared to a single-model baseline approach, without compromising output relevance to the same extent as the most sophisticated model. These results confirm that adaptive model selection can enable efficient, high-quality natural language processing inference on limited devices.

</details>


### [44] [Rethinking Communication Metrics: How Should We Measure Meaning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21626)
*Niloofar Tavakolian,Hakimeh Purmehdi,Jungyeon Baek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从统一的评估中心视角，系统综述了文本和图像语义通信系统的关键性能指标（KPI）。不同于以往按架构或应用组织的综述，本文聚焦于如何定义和测量语义成功，对现有KPI按通信目标、源模态、接收端输出、参考可用性、评估层级及信道约束等维度进行分类，并分析了开放问题和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义通信将通信系统的目标从精确符号重建转向语义保真、任务完成和高效信息交换，但其评估在电信、NLP、计算机视觉和机器学习等领域碎片化，缺乏能够跨模态、跨任务和跨信道条件统一刻画语义质量的单一度量标准，亟需一个统一的评估中心视角来系统梳理现有KPI。

Method: 该综述以评估为中心的组织方式，不同于以往按架构、应用或传输策略组织的综述。它依据通信目标、源模态、接收端输出、参考可用性、评估层次和信道/资源约束对现有KPI进行分类，系统回顾了基于重建、任务导向、无参考、表示级、感知和信道感知等各类指标，并进行了跨模态对比分析。

Result: 文章对各项KPI的角色、优势和局限性进行了跨模态比较，分析了未解决的语义KPI挑战如何影响监控、质量保证、资源优化、故障诊断和标准化，识别出多个关键开放问题：缺乏通用语义成功标准和标准化语义真值、语义漂移、无参考评估有限、机器学习指标与通信约束整合薄弱、缺少关系级和多模态KPI。

Conclusion: 未来应朝着标准化、可解释、自适应、任务感知和通信感知的统一评估框架方向发展，以填补当前语义通信评估体系的空白。

Abstract: Semantic communication shifts the objective of communication systems from accurate symbol reconstruction toward meaning preservation, task accomplishment, and efficient information exchange. However, its evaluation remains fragmented across telecommunications, natural language processing, computer vision, and machine learning, and no single metric can characterize semantic quality across modalities, tasks, and channel conditions. This article surveys key performance indicators (KPIs) for text- and image-based semantic communication systems from a unified, evaluation-centered perspective. Unlike prior surveys primarily organized around architectures, applications, or transmission strategies, this work focuses on how semantic success should be defined and measured. Existing KPIs are classified according to communication goal, source modality, receiver output, reference availability, evaluation level, and channel or resource constraints. The survey reviews reconstruction-based, task-oriented, reference-free, representation-level, perceptual, and channel-aware metrics, and presents a cross-modality comparison of their roles, strengths, and limitations. It further analyzes how unresolved semantic-KPI challenges affect monitoring, quality assurance, resource optimization, fault diagnosis, and standardization. Key open problems include the absence of universal semantic success criteria and standardized semantic ground truth, semantic drift, limited reference-free evaluation, weak integration of machine-learning metrics with communication constraints, and the lack of relation-level and multimodal KPIs. Finally, future research directions are outlined toward standardized, interpretable, adaptive, task-aware, and communication-aware evaluation frameworks.

</details>


### [45] [ChequeMark: An Ensemble Machine Learning Framework for After-Hours Business Deposit Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21629)
*Ann Youduo Xu,Emily Yu,Justin Leski,William Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种多视图集成机器学习框架用于支票欺诈检测，结合XGBoost、Isolation Forest和GraphSAGE三种模型的输出，生成客户级风险评分。在分布偏移场景下优于单一XGBoost模型（F1: 83.77%）且保持可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 支票欺诈在非工作时间存款业务中因其时间差（资金一个工作日内到账，但票据清算需数日）构成重大风险敞口。现有缓解措施依赖静态存款级检查，忽略了历史客户行为及演化模式，缺乏对分布偏移的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出多视图集成机器学习框架，具体结合三种模型：(1) XGBoost用于识别已知欺诈模式；(2) Isolation Forest用于无标签异常检测；(3) GraphSAGE用于捕捉与交易活动相关的关联模式。三个模型输出被组合为单一的客户级风险评分，并提供基于行为、异常和关联证据的通俗语言解释。

Result: 在稳定条件下，框架性能与XGBoost相当；在定向分布偏移条件下，框架表现最佳：F1达83.77%，FPR为0.69%，优于XGBoost（F1: 82.77%, FPR: 0.72%），表明对分布偏移具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该多视图集成框架在保持可解释性的同时，显著提升了欺诈检测对分布偏移的鲁棒性，为支票欺诈防护提供了一种兼顾性能与透明度的解决方案。

Abstract: Cheque fraud is a material risk in after-hours business deposit operations because funds may be released within one business day, while cheque clearing takes several days. This timing gap creates a fraud exposure window for financial institutions. Prior mitigation relies on static, deposit-level checks and therefore miss historical client behavior and evolving patterns. To address this gap, we propose a multi-view ensemble ML framework that combines: Extreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) for known fraud patterns, Isolation Forest for label-free anomaly detection, and Graph Sample and Aggregate (GraphSAGE) for relational patterns associated with transaction activities. We then combine the three outputs into a single client-level risk score. Under stable conditions, performance is comparable to XGBoost; under a targeted distribution shift, our framework performs best (F1: 83.77%, FPR: 0.69%) versus XGBoost (F1: 82.77%, FPR: 0.72%). These results indicate improved robustness to distribution shift while preserving interpretability through plain-language explanations grounded in behavioural, anomaly, and relational evidence.

</details>


### [46] [Large-Scale Evaluation of Advanced Imputation Methods for Missing Values in Smart Meter Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21638)
*Daniela Stojcheska,Marija Markovska,Dimitar Taskovski,Branislav Gerazov,Boris Nikolov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在大规模真实电力消费数据集（北马其顿17,428个商业智能电表，两年数据）上实证评估了三种数据填补算法：OWA、SoftImpute和Shape-Modeling Autoencoder，通过模拟1至168小时连续数据缺口来测试其鲁棒性。结果表明OWA在整体重建误差和稳定性方面表现最优，而自编码器方差较大，SoftImpute准确度较低但稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高级计量基础设施（AMI）中的电力消费数据对智能电网运行和非技术性损失（NTL）检测至关重要，但真实数据常因通信故障出现缺失值。现有缺失值填补研究缺乏在大型数据集上的验证，因此本文旨在大型真实数据集上对多种填补算法进行实证评估。

Method: 在北马其顿的17,428个商业智能电表、两年期的真实电力消费数据集上，对三种填补算法——最优加权平均（OWA）、低秩矩阵补全（SoftImpute）和形状建模自编码器——进行实证评估。通过模拟1至168小时（即连续缺口从1小时到一周）的连续数据缺失场景，检验各算法的鲁棒性和重建性能。

Result: 在所有被评估的缺口大小下，OWA算法提供了最低的整体重建误差，并且在最长一周缺口的极端情况下表现出很强的稳定性；自编码器则显示出较高的方差（即性能波动大）；SoftImpute虽然表现稳定但准确性较差。

Conclusion: 填补方法的选择应基于负荷曲线数据的特征，研究结果凸显了未来电网管理系统中采用混合算法架构的潜力，即结合不同方法的优势以提升填补性能。

Abstract: Accurate and reliable collection of electricity consumption data through Advanced Metering Infrastructure (AMI) is of great importance for the operation of smart grids, especially for the detection of non-technical losses (NTL). However, real-world datasets frequently suffer from missing values due to communication failures. This paper presents an empirical evaluation of three advanced algorithms for large-scale data imputation: the Optimally Weighted Average (OWA) method, Low-Rank Matrix Completion via SoftImpute, and a Shape-Modeling Autoencoder. Existing studies on missing value imputation in electricity consumption data often lack validation on larger datasets. Therefore, the goal of this paper is to validate the selected algorithms on a large-scale real-world electricity consumption dataset from North Macedonia that includes 17,428 commercial smart meters over two years. The robustness of each algorithm is evaluated by simulating continuous gaps in the data ranging from 1 to 168 hours. The results indicate that OWA provides the lowest overall reconstruction error across the evaluated gap sizes and strong stability in worst-case scenarios for gaps of up to one week. In contrast, the autoencoder exhibits higher variance, while SoftImpute has stable but inferior accuracy. These findings suggest that imputation methods should be selected based on the characteristics of load curve data and highlight the potential for hybrid algorithmic architectures in future grid management systems.

</details>

*Yujie Zhang,Dhananjaya Wijerathne,Zhaoying Li,Tulika Mitra*

TL;DR: 本文对微控制器（MCU）上的多种TinyML应用进行了系统性的性能和功耗特征分析，涵盖神经网络模型、软件库、操作系统和硬件架构，并提出一个模型来估算不同抽象层的开销成本，以帮助设计者最小化开销并优化边缘设备的CNN推理和神经架构搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TinyML系统正在推动边缘端的机器学习推理，但缺乏对这些系统的定量分析。本文的动机是填补这一空白，系统地量化和理解TinyML应用在MCU上的性能与功耗表现，特别是多层抽象（软件库、操作系统、硬件架构等）对性能和能效的影响。

Method: 本文对多种TinyML应用进行了系统性的性能和功耗特征化分析，覆盖了神经网络模型、软件库、操作系统和硬件架构等多个维度。研究者提出一个成本估算模型，用于量化不同抽象层的开销，并基于该模型提出减少开销的建议。

Result: 通过系统化的定量分析，本文揭示了不同抽象层（软件库、OS、硬件）在性能与功耗方面的开销影响，给出了抽象层成本的估算结果，并为降低这些开销提供了可操作的建议，可用于指导神经架构搜索和CNN推理优化。

Conclusion: 本文的发现能够帮助设计者更有效地进行神经架构搜索（NAS）和边缘设备上的CNN推理优化，通过理解并最小化抽象层的开销成本，提升TinyML系统在MCU上的性能与能效。

Abstract: TinyML systems are enabling machine learning (ML) inference at the edge. However, there is little quantitative analysis of such systems. This paper presents a systematic performance and power characterization of diverse TinyML applications on microcontrollers (MCUs), spanning neural network models, software libraries, operating systems, and hardware architectures. We focus on the impact of the multiple layers of abstraction that provide higher programmability at the expense of performance and energy efficiency. We propose a model to estimate the costs of different abstraction layers and make recommendations for minimizing those costs. Our findings can help designers with Neural Architecture Search (NAS) and CNN inference optimization on edge devices.

</details>


### [48] [GeoQ: Geometry-Aware Conditional Quantile Error Estimation for Scientific Surrogate Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21652)
*Khoa Nguyen,Daniel Serino,Aviral Prakash,Marc Klasky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoQ（Geometry-Aware Conditional Quantile Error Estimation），一种非侵入式校准框架，通过几何感知条件分位数建模来估计科学代理模型在单个查询点处的预测误差，在标量回归、混沌动力学、中期天气预报和Richtmyer-Meshkov不稳定性预测等任务上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络代理模型广泛应用于加速科学模拟，但在外推和自回归场景下需要输入相关的误差估计。现有方法缺乏对查询点处预测误差的个性化、有效的不确定性量化能力，因此需要一种能感知几何结构并估计条件误差的方法。

Method: GeoQ将查询点误差表示为锚点平均校准误差与学习的非负修正项之和。修正项建模为锚点相对误差增量的上条件分位数，使用基于表示空间位移和局部支持密度的几何特征。通过交叉拟合生成近似样本外校准元组，并利用特征空间k近邻支持分数识别学习误差模型受校准数据支持的区域。

Result: 在标量回归、混沌动力学、中期天气预报和Richtmyer-Meshkov不稳定性预测四个任务上评估GeoQ，结果显示几何感知条件分位数建模为科学代理模型的误差估计提供了一种实用且非侵入式的方案。

Conclusion: GeoQ证明了几何感知的条件分位数建模能够在科学代理模型中实现有效的、与输入相关的误差估计，且无需侵入模型内部结构，为外推和自回归等实际部署场景提供了可行的错误校准方案。

Abstract: Neural-network surrogate models are increasingly used to accelerate scientific simulations, but their deployment in extrapolative and autoregressive settings requires input-dependent estimates of prediction error. In this work, we introduce GeoQ (Geometry-Aware Conditional Quantile Error Estimation), a non-intrusive calibration framework for estimating surrogate error at individual query points. GeoQ represents the error at a query point as an anchor-averaged calibration error plus a learned nonnegative correction. This correction is modeled as an upper conditional quantile of the anchor-relative error increment, using geometry-based features that encode representation-space displacement and local support density. A cross-fitting procedure generates approximately out-of-sample calibration tuples, while a feature-space k-nearest-neighbor support score identifies regions \textcolor{black}{where the learned error model is supported by calibration data}. We evaluate GeoQ on scalar regression, chaotic dynamics, medium-range weather forecasting, and Richtmyer-Meshkov instability prediction. The results demonstrate that geometry-aware conditional quantile modeling provides a practical and non-intrusive approach for validity-aware error estimation in scientific surrogate models.

</details>


### [49] [Bounded Precision-Geometry Scaling for Robust Multi-Task Learning under Loss Scale Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21653)
*Krishna Subedi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BPGS（有界精度-几何缩放）方法，通过有界sigmoid参数化映射各任务的log-variance，并解耦网络优化与不确定性优化，解决多任务学习中异方差不确定性加权在损失量级跨越多个数量级时严重退化的问题。BPGS在合成压力测试和三个真实基准（NYUv2稠密预测、Yeast多标签分类、RF1多目标回归）上均表现优异，对纯损失缩放（×1到×1000）具有鲁棒性（macro分数仅0.777→0.778，而Kendall加权从0.780降至0.637），且运行时开销较Kendall低1%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务学习常将跨越多个数量级的损失结合，导致异方差不确定性加权（homoscedastic uncertainty weighting）严重退化。现有加权方法对损失量级差异敏感，需要在损失尺度失衡场景下提供更鲁棒的加权策略。

Method: BPGS将每个任务的log-variance通过有界sigmoid参数化映射，锚定到分离的batch损失统计量上，并将网络优化与不确定性优化解耦。其归一化任务权重在非退化损失尺度下可证明对均匀重缩放保持不变。

Result: 在纯损失重缩放（×1至×1000）下，BPGS的macro分数从0.777变为0.778，而Kendall加权从0.780降至0.637；在NYUv2上BPGS取得最低深度绝对相对误差（0.223）、深度RMSE（0.790）和总损失（1.891）；在Yeast上取得最高micro-F1（0.616）；在RF1上与PCGrad竞争（后者在RMSE和MAE上领先）。批大小和校准敏感性研究显示变化很小，运行时开销相对Kendall低于1%。

Conclusion: BPGS被确立为异方差不确定性加权的尺度鲁棒（scale-robust）替代方案，在损失尺度差异主导多任务优化时尤为有效。

Abstract: Multi-task learning often combines losses that span several orders of magnitude, causing homoscedastic uncertainty weighting to degrade severely. We propose Bounded Precision-Geometry Scaling (BPGS), a method that maps each task's log-variance through a bounded sigmoid parameterisation anchored to detached batch loss statistics, and decouples network optimisation from uncertainty optimisation. Its normalised task weights are provably invariant to uniform rescaling under non-degenerate loss scales. We evaluate BPGS on synthetic stress tests and three real-world benchmarks: NYUv2 dense prediction, Yeast multi-label classification, and RF1 multi-target regression. Under pure loss rescaling from $\times 1$ to $\times 1000$, its macro score changes from 0.777 to 0.778, whereas Kendall weighting drops from 0.780 to 0.637; $\ell_1$-normalising Kendall's weights does not close the gap. On NYUv2, BPGS records the lowest depth absolute relative error (0.223), depth RMSE (0.790), and total loss (1.891) among all compared methods, including Nash-MTL. Sensitivity studies on batch size and calibration show small variation across the tested ranges, and runtime overhead relative to Kendall is under 1%. BPGS posts the highest Yeast micro-F1 (0.616) and is competitive on RF1, though PCGrad leads RMSE and MAE there. These findings establish BPGS as a scale-robust alternative to homoscedastic uncertainty weighting, notably effective when loss-scale disparities dominate multi-task optimisation.

</details>


### [50] [Variational Structure at the Edge of Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21660)
*Eric Regis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了"edge coupling"函数到heavy-ball和Nesterov动量优化器，证明其临界点刻画不动点与二周期轨道，Hessian刻画稳定性，并将edge coupling与对称Verlet作用量建立联系，形式化了优化器不稳定边缘与离散力学之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散时间优化器在不稳定边缘运行时表现出近似二周期行为，这些振荡动力学类似于辛积分器生成的保守系统动力学。然而，离散时间优化器在不稳定边缘与离散力学之间的精确联系尚未被充分探索，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 本文将Litman引入的edge coupling函数从梯度下降扩展到heavy-ball和Nesterov动量优化器。通过分析该函数的临界点来表征不动点和二周期轨道，利用其Hessian矩阵分析这些点的稳定性，并将edge coupling识别为对称Verlet作用量以建立与离散力学的联结。

Result: 证明了扩展后的edge coupling的临界点能够刻画heavy-ball和Nesterov动量优化器的不动点与二周期轨道，其Hessian矩阵能够刻画这些轨道的稳定性。同时将edge coupling与对称Verlet作用量等同起来，形式化了优化器不稳定边缘行为与离散力学之间的联系。

Conclusion: 本文成功将edge coupling框架推广到带动量的优化器，并建立了优化器不稳定边缘与离散力学（尤其是对称Verlet积分）之间的严谨理论联系，为理解现代优化器的振荡行为提供了新的数学视角。

Abstract: When discrete-time optimizers operate at the edge of stability, they exhibit near-two-periodic behavior. These oscillatory dynamics are reminiscent of conservative systems, such as the dynamics generated by symplectic integrators. However, a precise formulation of the connection between discrete-time optimizers at the edge of stability and discrete mechanics remains underexplored. Recently, Litman introduced the "edge coupling": a functional on consecutive gradient descent iterates whose critical points encode the fixed points and two-point orbits of the gradient descent dynamics. Here we extend the edge coupling to heavy-ball and Nesterov momentum. We show that its critical points characterize the fixed points and two-point orbits, with its Hessian characterizing their stability. We also show that the edge coupling can be identified with the symmetric Verlet action, formalizing the connection between the edge of stability and discrete mechanics.

</details>


### [51] [SynEHR: Joint Modeling Inter-visit Temporal Evolution and Intra-visit Clinical Structure for Longitudinal EHR Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21673)
*Ximiao Li,Lin Jiang,Rongchao Xu,Dahai Yu,Zhe He,Guang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SynEHR，一种基于LLM的轻量级纵向EHR合成框架，通过时间状态条件模块和时间关系适配模块，结合LoRA微调的语言模型生成器，生成具有临床一致性和时间真实性的纵向电子健康记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实纵向EHR数据难以访问，而现有生成模型未能显式整合EHR中访视间的不规则时间演化和访视内的临床事件结构，导致生成的访视序列在临床上不一致、时间上不真实。

Method: 提出SynEHR框架，包含两个创新设计：(1) 时间状态条件模块（Temporal State Conditioning Module）捕捉跨访视的不规则时间状态；(2) 时间关系适配模块（Temporal-Relational Adaptation Module）将时间状态与患者历史结合，动态构建患者特定的关系表示。框架基于参数高效的LoRA适配语言模型生成器，具备下一访视生成能力，用于训练两个模块进行时间和临床信息引导的生成。

Result: 在真实世界EHR数据集上的保真度、隐私和下游效用评估中，SynEHR在生成更临床连贯且时间忠实的纵向EHR数据方面优于多个最先进模型。

Conclusion: SynEHR通过显式建模访视间不规则时间演化和访视内临床事件结构，有效提升了合成纵向EHR的临床一致性和时间真实性，为有限真实记录条件下进行更广泛的建模和分析提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Longitudinal electronic health records (EHRs) document patients' sequences of clinical visits over time, preserving the temporal evolution of disease progression and care delivery. However, real longitudinal EHRs are difficult to access because they contain large amounts of fine-grained, patient-specific information. Synthetic EHR generation therefore provides a valuable approach for preserving the statistical patterns and clinical structure of patient visit trajectories, enabling broader modeling and analysis when real records are limited. Although recent generative models have made progress in producing future visit sequences, they remain limited in explicitly integrating inter-visit irregular temporal evolution and intra-visit clinical event structures in EHRs, leading to clinically inconsistent and temporally unrealistic visit sequences. In this work, we propose SynEHR, a lightweight adaptive LLM-based framework for longitudinal EHR synthesis. There are two novel designs in SynEHR, i.e., a Temporal State Conditioning Module captures irregular temporal states across visits and a Temporal-Relational Adaptation Module combines these states with patient history to dynamically construct patient-specific relational representations. SynEHR then builds on a parameter-efficient LoRA-adapted language-model generator with next-visit generation capability to train the two modules for temporally and clinically informed generation. Extensive experiments on real-world EHR datasets across fidelity, privacy, and downstream utility evaluations demonstrate that SynEHR outperforms state-of-the-art models by generating more clinically coherent and temporally faithful longitudinal EHR data.

</details>


### [52] [Read, Write, Relax: Why Neural PDE Surrogates Need Both Global and Local Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21677)
*Anuj Kumar,Heiko Zimmermann,Josiah Bjorgaard,Jacan Chaplais,Nikolaos Bouklas,Matteo Salvador,Alexander Lavin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Read-Write-Relax (RWR)框架，统一了全局（潜token注意力）与局部（边消息传递）两类网格仿真神经代理模型。作者指出全局模型如同空间低通滤波器，局部模型缺乏全局传播能力，二者恰似多重网格循环的两半。RWR通过交错潜注意力和消息传递松弛，在工业与大尺度网格基准上实现了全频谱误差校正，在数据稀缺场景下具有高数据效率，并在多项对比中取得最佳精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网格仿真神经代理模型分为两类：基于潜token注意力的全局模型和基于边消息传递的局部模型。两类模型都难以应对工业场景中的高维问题和大规模/复杂网格，作者希望揭示这一局限的根源并提出统一的解决方案。

Method: 作者首先从理论上阐明：全局方法的潜token注意力在空间上等价于低通滤波器，而局部消息传递在大型网格中缺乏足够的全局传播范围。从误差谱角度看，这两个算子分别对应多重网格循环中的不同半部（一个校正低频误差，另一个校正高频误差），相互无法替代。基于此，作者提出RWR，在统一公式下交错执行潜注意力与消息传递松弛，从而覆盖整个误差频谱。

Result: RWR在工业基准和公开基准的几乎所有对比中取得最高精度；在数据稀缺场景下表现出显著的数据效率优势；能准确预测工程关注的物理量，并将全场预测扩展到具有挑战性的大规模问题上。

Conclusion: 全局潜注意力和局部消息传递是互补的误差校正算子，二者各司其职但不能互相替代。RWR通过交错融合两者，实现了全频谱误差降低，成为工业级网格仿真中更具精度和数据效率的神经代理模型。

Abstract: Recent mesh-based simulation advances have, in no small part, relied on neural surrogates of two distinct families: global models that route information through a small set of latent tokens, and local models that perform message passing across mesh edges. Consistent with both classes is the inability to perform beyond low-dimensional problems and small-scale or oversimplified meshes, the simulation regimes where industrial problems reside. Our work shows this explicitly and presents a unified formulation. In global approaches, latent-token attention acts as a spatial low-pass filter, while local message passing lacks the global reach necessary to propagate information across large mesh spaces. Viewed through the error, the two operators are the halves of a multigrid cycle: one corrects errors at the lower end of the spectrum, the other at the higher end, and neither can do the other's job. We introduce Read-Write-Relax (RWR), which interleaves latent attention with message-passing relaxation under a unified formulation. The interleaved processor lowers error across the entire spectrum, making RWR the most accurate model in nearly every comparison across our industrial and public benchmarks. It is also markedly data-efficient in the scarce-data regimes, accurate on the engineering quantities of interest, and scales full-field predictions to challenging, large-scale problems.

</details>


### [53] [Benchmarking Composable Compression Techniques in Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21693)
*Afsara Benazir,Chen Chen,Rongxiao Qu,Jiabo Huang,Jingtao Li,Lingjuan Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoEXBench是一个系统化基准，用于评估MoE大语言模型在实际部署中多种压缩技术（专家剪枝、权重量化、KV-cache压缩）组合使用时的端到端效果。该基准覆盖10个MoE模型（30B-235B参数）、三种注意力架构，评估20%-50%专家剪枝率、1-16比特量化和多种KV-cache精度设置。研究发现压缩技术之间存在非平凡交互，组合压缩效果无法从单独技术预测，专家剪枝是质量下降的主要来源，并发布了标准化模块评分和可复现脚本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE LLM通过稀疏激活扩展模型容量，但其庞大的专家参数、路由不均衡和长上下文KV-cache增长使得在商用硬件上部署困难。实际部署通常需要叠加多种压缩技术（专家剪枝、权重量化、KV-cache压缩），但现有研究仅单独评估这些技术，缺乏对它们组合使用时交互效应的系统研究。

Method: MoEXBench设计了一个八模块评估套件，联合衡量组合压缩质量、工作负载与架构鲁棒性、剪枝/量化/KV-cache敏感性以及商用硬件上的部署效率。基准涵盖10个MoE模型（30B-235B参数），覆盖标准注意力、混合线性注意力和滑动窗口注意力三种架构，系统评估20%-50%专家剪枝率、1-16比特量化方案及多种KV-cache精度设置的单独和组合应用。

Result: 实验揭示了压缩方法间非平凡的交互：组合压缩效果无法从单独技术表现预测；压缩率本身不能可靠预测质量损失或运行时间增益；专家剪枝是质量下降的主导因素；平均质量指标可能掩盖特定工作负载和架构的失效情况。

Conclusion: MoEXBench通过发布标准化模块评分、压缩产物和可复现脚本，为MoE模型家族和硬件后端提供了实际的精度-内存-延迟对比基准，支持实际部署场景下多压缩技术组合的可行性评估和优化决策。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) LLMs scale model capacity efficiently through sparse activation, but their large expert parameter footprint, routing imbalance, and long-context KV-cache growth make deployment difficult on commodity hardware. Practical deployment often requires stacking multiple compression techniques: expert pruning removes redundant experts, weight quantization lowers model memory footprint, and KV-cache compression reduces long-context memory pressure. However, these techniques are typically evaluated in isolation, leaving open how they interact when applied together in realistic deployment pipelines.
  In this work, we present MoEXBench, a systematic benchmark for evaluating composable MoE compression as an end-to-end deployment workflow. MoEXBench studies 10 MoE models ranging from 30B to 235B total parameters across standard-attention, hybrid linear-attention, and sliding window attention architectures. It evaluates 20%-50% expert pruning rates, 1 to 16 bit weight-quantization schemes, and multiple KV-cache precision settings, applied both individually and in combination.
  MoEXBench introduces an eight-module evaluation suite that jointly measures composable-compression quality, workload and architecture robustness, pruning/quantization/KV cache sensitivity, and deployment efficiency on commodity hardware. Our results reveal non-trivial interactions among compression methods: composable compression cannot be predicted from standalone techniques, compression rate alone does not reliably predict quality loss or runtime gain, expert pruning is the dominant degradation source, and average quality can hide workload and architecture-specific failures. By releasing normalized module scores, compressed artifacts, and reproducible scripts, MoEXBench enables practical accuracy-memory-latency comparison across MoE families and hardware backends.

</details>


### [54] [Posterior Information Dynamics of Diffusion Models for Linear Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21709)
*Xiangming Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过"平滑似然力"（精确后验分数与先验分数之差）研究扩散模型在解决线性逆问题时测量信息的注入时程与分配机制，推导出I-MMSE恒等式与二次衰减规律，并通过可解模型和实验验证相关预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型广泛用作线性逆问题的先验，但人们尚不清楚测量信息在反向去噪过程中何时进入、以及如何沿信号方向分配。端点质量无法揭示这一内部机制，因此需要从理论层面刻画信息注入的动态过程。

Method: 定义平滑似然力（精确后验与先验分数之差），分析其在各噪声水平下的性态；推导其期望平方范数与相对熵耗散/增长的关系；建立I-MMSE恒等式联系信息增益与去噪误差降低；在有限二阶矩假设下证明高噪声区力能量与先验分数能量之比按核信号系数的二次方衰减；并通过可解模型与实验验证理论预测。

Result: 可解模型显示：条件化能移除测量已解释的类间分离，将n个经验样本上的均匀索引熵从logn降至H(I|r)，且信息同化效率即使在奇异值相同的情况下也依赖算子与先验的对齐程度。FFHQ实验中，共享相同谱的掩码在先验归一化零空间轨迹统计上呈现不同表现。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了扩散模型条件采样中测量信息注入的定量规律，证明信息同化不仅取决于测量算子的奇异值谱，还依赖于算子与先验的结构对齐，为理解和设计基于扩散先验的逆问题求解提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Diffusion models are widely used as priors for linear inverse problems, yet endpoint quality does not reveal when measurement information enters reverse denoising or how it is allocated across signal directions. We study this process through the smoothed likelihood force, the difference between exact posterior and prior scores at each noise level. For a fixed measurement, its expected squared norm gives both posterior--prior relative-entropy dissipation and reverse-path relative-entropy growth. Averaging over measurements yields an information--minimum mean-square error (I-MMSE) identity linking information gain to denoising-error reduction. Under finite second moments, the force energy and its ratio to prior-score energy decay quadratically in the noising kernel's signal coefficient at high noise. Solvable models show that conditioning removes class separation already explained by the measurement, reduces a uniform index entropy over \(n\) empirical samples from \(\log n\) to \(H(I\mid r)\), and makes assimilation depend on operator--prior alignment even for identical singular values. Experiments in models with tractable posteriors evaluate these predictions. In a separate illustration with a frozen FFHQ model, masks sharing the same spectrum yield different prior-normalized null-space trajectory statistics.

</details>


### [55] [Reinforcement Learning on Benign Facts Amplifies Leakage of Memorized Private Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21727)
*Renfei Zhang,Niloofar Mireshghallah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文发现，在良性事实数据上应用带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）会显著增强指令模型对已记忆的个人身份信息（PII）的提取能力，即使训练数据中完全不包含任何隐私信息。在DeepSeek-V3.1上，逐字回忆命中率从0.155提升至0.370（2.4倍），且效果随模型规模增大而增强，而推理能力和拒答率保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习已成为提升模型推理能力的主流方法，但其对模型信息泄露行为的副作用尚未被充分研究。作者希望揭示RLVR在良性数据上训练是否会意外解锁模型已记忆的隐私信息，从而为隐私安全评估提供新的视角。

Method: 作者首先确认指令模型已记忆PII但处于潜伏状态（很少主动泄露），然后在完全不含任何PII的良性事实数据上应用RLVR，随后通过两种探针重新测试：（1）针对性的name->email配对探测；（2）无针对性的自由回忆提示（直接要求模型列出已知地址）。实验跨三个模型（8B到671B参数）验证效果，并同时评估推理能力和拒答率是否保持不变。

Result: PII提取在两种探测方式下均显著上升：DeepSeek-V3.1上逐字recall@k从0.155提升至0.370（2.4倍提升）。效果随模型规模扩大而增强，671B参数的最大模型绝对泄露量最大。同时，模型的推理能力和拒答率均保持不变，表明RL是选择性改变可访问的记忆信息，而非广泛改变模型行为。

Conclusion: 已记忆的私有数据可以通过完全不接触该数据的训练被显著提高可提取性。这为攻击者提供了一条获取记忆数据的路径——不需要任何隐私相关的训练信号，不需要访问数据本身，只需在无害数据上进行微调即可。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is deployed to make models better at reasoning tasks, but its side effect on what models will divulge is under studied. Here we show that RLVR on facts increases extraction of personally identifiable information (PII) the instruct model had already memorized. We first confirm that instruct models have already memorized PII but leave them latent, rarely surfacing one when asked. We then apply RL on benign factual data that contains no PII of any kind, and re-probe: a targeted probe over name->email pairs, and an untargeted free-recall prompt that simply asks the model to list the addresses it knows. PII extraction rises sharply under both: on DeepSeek-V3.1, verbatim recall@k increases from 0.155 to 0.370, a 2.4x gain. The effect scales with model size: across three models spanning 8B to 671B parameters, absolute leakage is largest in the biggest model. Meanwhile model's reasoning abilities and refusal rates are retained, indicating that RL selectively changes which memorized information is accessible rather than broadly altering the model. In summary, memorized private data can be made markedly more extractable by training that never touches it. This gives an adversary a route to memorized data that requires no privacy-relevant training signal and no access to the data itself -- only the ability to fine-tune on something innocuous.

</details>


### [56] [Adaptive Multilevel Twisted Sequential Monte Carlo for Rare Events Estimation in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21736)
*Zixuan Liu,Fangzheng Wu,Brian Summa,Zizhan Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应多级扭曲序贯蒙特卡洛（Adaptive Multilevel Twisted SMC）方法，通过学习一系列逐渐变稀有的事件来引导LLM生成，从而更准确地估计罕见不安全行为的概率，为部署规模下的语言模型安全评估提供有效的工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模部署（百万或十亿次交互）下，LLM中极低概率的罕见不安全行为仍具有实际重要性。标准的扭曲序贯蒙特卡洛（Twisted SMC）在学习扭曲函数时需要目标稀有事件的正样本，但在学习到有效的扭曲之前这些正样本几乎不存在，导致稀有事件概率估计不可靠。

Method: 提出Adaptive Multilevel Twisted SMC方法：通过学习一系列逐步变稀有的中间事件来逐步学习目标稀有事件的扭曲函数。每一层学习到的扭曲为其下一层提供更丰富、更具信息量的正样本，从而逐步获得更精确的最终扭曲函数，用于目标事件的稀有概率估计。

Result: 在多种任务和不同模型规模上的实验表明，所提出的方法能够产生更准确的稀有事件概率估计，相比标准方法有显著提升。

Conclusion: 该方法能够更可靠地发现难以观测的不安全行为，为部署的大语言模型的安全评估和安全性对齐提供了实用的工具。

Abstract: Rare unsafe behaviors in large language models can remain practically significant even when their probability is extremely small, particularly at deployment scales involving millions or billions of interactions. Twisted Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) provides a principled framework for rare-event probability estimation by learning twist functions that guide generation toward a target event. However, the standard twist learning framework relies on positive samples from the rare-event target distribution, which may be nearly absent before an informative twist has been learned, resulting in unreliable rare-event estimation. We propose Adaptive Multilevel Twisted SMC, which learns the rare-event twist through a sequence of progressively rarer intermediate events. At each level, the learned twist provides more informative positive examples for learning the next twist, ultimately leading to a more accurate final twist for the target rare event. Experiments across diverse tasks and model scales show that the proposed method produces more accurate rare-event probability estimates. By enabling more reliable discovery of hard-to-observe unsafe behaviors, our method provides a practical tool for strengthening the evaluation and safety alignment of deployed language models.

</details>
