### [89] [Loss Landscape Features That Make Adam Stall: Definitions, Estimators, and the Preconditioned Hessian View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22145)
*Rodion Podorozhny*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了隐式神经表示（INR）架构中Adam优化器在病态损失地形上的表现，定义了用于判断Adam能否缓解病态化的量化指标（Hessian条件数、Adam预处理Hessian条件数、对角质量、负谱质量、梯度能量分数等），并通过2×2示例、FINER图像拟合案例全面验证了诊断框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然经过充分调参的Adam（特别是学习率）在病态损失地形上也可能达到很低损失，但有时会收敛到远高于二阶方法所能达到损失的平台。现有研究缺乏系统性的量化指标来判断Adam在何种病态地形下能够缓解病态化、何种情况下会失效（如停滞在鞍点附近），因此需要一套可测量的诊断框架来指导优化器的选择和使用。

Method: 首先定义了用于判断Adam能否缓解病态性的核心指标，包括：原始Hessian与Adam预处理Hessian D^{-1/2}HD^{-1/2}的条件数（后者由Adam更新规则推导而来）、区分轴向对齐与交叉耦合病态化的对角质量ρ、通过随机Lanczos求积估计的负谱质量、以及曲率带上的梯度能量分数（其中平坦分数用于指示Adam停滞）。然后用2×2算例和图示说明对角预处理可消除轴向对齐病态化但无法处理交叉耦合病态化的原因。最后以FINER图像拟合架构为案例，完整走通整个损失地形分析流程，包括架构描述、鞍点停滞原因、调参基线PSNR与分块二阶方法120-134dB结果的对比及误差图分析。

Result: 实验表明所定义的指标可以有效判定Adam在特定损失地形上的表现；FINER案例中Adam在鞍点处停滞而二阶方法达到120-134dB的高精度，验证了诊断框架在实际INR架构中的实用性，并展示了高精度图像拟合在实际应用中的价值。

Conclusion: 提出的损失地形分析框架为判断Adam在病态优化问题中的适用性提供了可操作的量化标准，揭示了Adam对角预处理能力的边界（仅能处理轴向对齐病态化而不能处理交叉耦合病态化），并展示了结合诊断指标选择二阶方法以突破Adam性能瓶颈的可行路径。

Abstract: Across implicit-neural-representation (INR) architectures and analytic benchmarks we observe that a thoroughly tuned Adam (especially its learning rate (lr), e.g. in a hyperparameter sweep from $lr = 0.05$ to $10^{-8}$) can potentially reach a very low loss even on ill-conditioned loss landscape or converge at a plateau far above the loss attained by second-order methods. This report defines the measured metrics that help determine if Adam can mitigate the ill-conditioning on a given loss landscape. We provide the indicators by which each outcome is determined, that are: the condition number of the Hessian and of the Adam-preconditioned Hessian $D^{-1/2}HD^{-1/2}$ (with the derivation from Adam's update rule), the diagonal mass $ρ$ that distinguishes axis-aligned from cross-coupled ill-conditioning, the negative spectral mass estimated by stochastic Lanczos quadrature, and the gradient energy fractions over curvature bands, including the flat fraction that indicates the Adam stall. A worked out $2\times 2$ example and an illustration show the reasons why a diagonal preconditioning by Adam can remove axis-aligned ill-conditioning by rescaling and why it cannot do the same if the ill-conditioning is cross coupled. In addition, we present a case study of FINER image fitting architecture that goes over the whole loss landscape analysis framework: the fitting architecture description, reasons due to which its landscape stalls Adam at saddles, the measured PSNR values through our tuned baselines to the $120$--$134$\,dB results of the blockwise second order methods, the error maps behind those numbers, and description of the benefits such image fitting accuracy gives in practice.

</details>


### [90] [More accurate behavioral predictions with hybrid Bayesian-connectionist models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22154)
*Brenden M. Lake,Akshay K. Jagadish,Guangyuan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"贝叶斯蒸馏与行为微调"(BBT)方法，将贝叶斯模型和神经网络两种范式结合：先用合成数据训练神经网络模仿贝叶斯模型，再用人类行为数据微调网络以捕捉额外结构。在人类概念学习的四个案例研究中，BBT在预测人类行为方面优于传统方法，同时能揭示心理学洞见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者在建模人类行为时需在贝叶斯模型和神经网络之间做选择，两者各有优劣。贝叶斯模型易于测试多种表征和归纳偏好，但容易过度简化；神经网络能捕捉复杂结构，但难以测试不同的归纳偏好。作者希望获得两种范式的优点。

Method: BBT采用两阶段流程：(1) 通过合成数据训练神经网络使其模仿贝叶斯模型的行为（贝叶斯蒸馏）；(2) 在人类行为数据上对网络进行微调（行为微调），以捕捉超出贝叶斯模型假设的额外结构和细微差别。

Result: 在人类概念学习的四个案例研究中，BBT在预测人类行为方面优于传统方法，同时还能揭示心理学的洞见，产出的模型既能模仿贝叶斯先验，又能捕捉违反简单建模假设的启发式和偏差。

Conclusion: BBT提供了一种简单有效的模型构建配方，成功融合了贝叶斯模型的可解释性和神经网络的表达力，使得同一模型既能测试不同归纳偏好，又不会过度简化人类行为的复杂性。

Abstract: Researchers must often choose between Bayesian or neural network models of behavior, two paradigms with complementary strengths and weaknesses. An ideal paradigm would facilitate testing many kinds of representations and inductive biases; Bayesian models make this easy, while neural networks do not. Similarly, an ideal paradigm would avoid over-simplifications; neural networks make this easy, while Bayesian models do not. Here, we introduce Bayesian distillation with Behavioral Tuning (BBT) as an approach to getting the best of both traditions. BBT offers a simple recipe for model building: first, a neural network is trained to mimic a Bayesian model through synthetic data, and second, the network is fine-tuned on human behavior to capture additional structure and nuance. Across four case studies in human concept learning, we find that BBT outperforms traditional approaches at predicting human behavior while also revealing psychological insights, resulting in models that can both mimic Bayesian priors and capture heuristics and biases that violate simple modeling assumptions.

</details>


### [91] [Why Does Robustness Reduce Superposition?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22155)
*Adam Elimadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于特征分类学，通过因果链解释对抗训练为何减少叠加现象：对抗训练放弃了非鲁棒特征，导致需要表示的特征总数减少，从而降低了叠加程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗样本的起源仍是开放问题。虽然Gorton & Lewis (2025)已证明对抗样本源于叠加现象，并实证发现对抗训练能减少叠加，但缺乏对这些现象背后的机制性解释。

Method: 借鉴Ilyas et al. (2019)的鲁棒/非鲁棒特征分类学，提出一条经验性因果链：对抗训练 → 放弃非鲁棒特征 → 总特征数减少 → 叠加减少。

Result: 提供了一个经验性的机制解释，将对抗训练减少叠加现象归因于其对非鲁棒特征的弃用所引发的特征总量缩减。

Conclusion: 该工作通过将特征分类学与机械可解释性中的叠加理论相衔接，为理解对抗训练如何抑制叠加效应提供了初步的经验性因果解释。

Abstract: The study of adversarial examples and their origins remains an open area of research. Mechanistic interpretability, and superposition in particular, offers new avenues for approaching this problem. Gorton & Lewis (2025) demonstrate that adversarial examples arise from superposition and show empirically that adversarial training reduces superposition, yet provide no mechanistic account of why this occurs. We present an empirical explanation inspired by the feature taxonomy of Ilyas et al. (2019), tracing the following chain of causalities: adversarial training abandons non-robust features, leading to fewer total features to represent, resulting in less superposition.

</details>


### [92] [Unveiling the Depth-Performance Dilemma in Split-Federated Fine-tuning of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22188)
*Hariharan Ramesh,Someshwaran Murugaiyan,Jyotikrishna Dass*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Split Federated Fine-tuning（SFF）中的"深度-性能困境"（Depth-Performance Dilemma）：最大化系统效率的深层划分配置恰恰会导致微调质量崩溃。通过对四种模型规模和多基准的综合审计，发现深层划分在提升吞吐量和隐私的同时导致灾难性的性能平台期，现有的联邦适配器聚合方法（AVG, STACK, SVD, FREEZE）无法缓解分割架构特有的伪影。机制诊断表明，崩溃源于Transformer的近等距拓扑结构，使聚合噪声无衰减传播直至触发服务器分区的"注意力崩溃"（Attention Collapse）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多关注SFF在系统层面的优势（吞吐量、隐私），但很少系统评估不同划分深度对模型效用（fine-tuning质量）的影响。作者识别出深层划分是系统效率最优的选择，但恰恰在该配置下模型效用会遭遇严重退化，且此"深度-性能困境"缺乏理论解释和有效的缓解策略，因此需要全面探索。

Method: 在四种模型规模（GPT-2到Llama-3-8B）和多个基准上进行了系统的审计实验，评估深层和浅层划分在不同联邦适配器聚合方法（AVG、STACK、SVD、FREEZE）下的表现；同时提出一个机制诊断方法，通过分析Transformer的近等距拓扑特性，追踪聚合噪声如何在网络中传播并最终在服务器分区触发"注意力崩溃"（Attention Collapse）。

Result: 更深的分区在吞吐量和隐私方面带来单调增益，但伴随着微调性能的崩塌（平台期）；所有四种SOTA联邦适配器聚合方法在标准联邦学习中有效，但在分割架构中无法缓解崩溃；机制层面证明崩溃根源是Transformer近等距拓扑使聚合噪声无衰减传播，最终导致服务器分区中的注意力崩溃。

Conclusion: 研究挑战了"划分深度是效用中性的调参旋钮"这一主流假设，明确了系统效率与模型效用之间的根本矛盾，并为稳定开展分布式LLM微调提供了结构化的理论基础——即分割架构的稳定性需要超越现有适配器聚合方法的新设计。

Abstract: Split Federated Fine-tuning (SFF) is a promising paradigm for scaling Large Language Models (LLMs) by partitioning model depth between resource-constrained clients and a centralized server. While system incentives for throughput and privacy favor deep partitions, the impact of such configurations on model utility remains poorly understood. In this work, we identify and characterize the Depth-Performance Dilemma: the regime that maximizes system efficiency is precisely where fine-tuning quality collapses. Through a comprehensive audit across four model scales (GPT-2 to Llama-3-8B) and diverse benchmarks, we demonstrate that deeper partitions provide monotonic gains in throughput and privacy at the cost of catastrophic performance plateaus. We evaluate a suite of state-of-the-art federated adapter aggregation methods including AVG, STACK, SVD, and FREEZE, revealing that while these techniques are effective in standard Federated Learning, they fail to mitigate the artifacts unique to split architectures. Finally, we provide a mechanistic diagnosis for this failure, tracing the collapse to the near-isometric topology of Transformers, which allows aggregation noise to propagate without attenuation until it triggers Attention Collapse in the server partition. Our findings challenge the prevailing assumption that partition depth is a utility-neutral tuning knob and provide a structural foundation for stable distributed LLM fine-tuning.

</details>


### [93] [On the Capability Separation Between World-Model Policy Learning and Imitated World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22197)
*Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论和经验角度系统比较了"世界-动作模型"（先预测未来结果再推断动作）与直接行为克隆两种范式的控制能力差异。核心发现是：在观测演示数据上，世界-动作模仿学习在外部策略类和理想模仿目标上与直接行为克隆等价；但动作条件的世界模型学习（用于策略优化）与它们本质不同，因为观测数据无法识别动作效应，且可能导致最坏情况的后悔值，而一次信息性干预即可改变这一局面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型被认为可以通过预测未来结果来改善表征学习和数据效率，但尚不清楚当两者都从相同的观测演示数据中训练时，它是否比直接行为克隆提供更强的控制能力。本文旨在厘清这两类方法在控制能力上的本质区别。

Method: 本文采用理论分析框架，从三个层面进行比较：（1）控制器类层面：证明任何世界-动作策略都可以展平为具有相同闭环轨迹分布的随机策略；（2）种群层面：在可实现性、精确优化、共享部署信息和保分布部署条件下，证明直接行为克隆和世界-动作模仿都能恢复观测行为策略；（3）世界模型学习层面：刻画不条件化于候选动作的未来模型的不可约动作特定预测误差，识别世界-动作联合模型恢复干预性前向模型的条件，并构造环境族证明观测学习者的最坏情况后悔值为正，而一次信息性干预可达到零后悔。

Result: 直接行为克隆与世界-动作模仿在不受限外部策略类和理想模仿目标上是等价的；两者的关键区别不在于是否预测未来，而在于是否基于指定动作进行条件化预测并用控制目标进行比较。观测演示数据在一般情况下无法识别动作效应，会导致正的最坏情况后悔值，而一个信息性干预就足以实现零后悔。

Conclusion: 单纯预测与观测行为相关联的未来（世界-动作模仿）不会比直接行为克隆提供更强的控制能力；真正的关键区别在于"预测观测行为对应的未来"与"预测指定动作的后果以供策略优化"之间的鸿沟，后者需要干预性数据或额外的识别条件。

Abstract: World-action models predict a future outcome and then infer an associated action. Although this factorization can improve representation learning and data efficiency, it is unclear whether it provides stronger control capability than direct behavior cloning when both are trained from the same observational demonstrations.
  We compare a direct behavior-cloning policy, an imitation-trained world-action policy, and a policy optimized with an action-conditioned world model. At the controller-class level, every world-action policy can be flattened into a direct stochastic policy with the same closed-loop trajectory distribution. At the population level, under realizability, exact optimization, common deployment information, and distribution-preserving deployment, direct behavior cloning and world-action imitation both recover the observational behavior policy. Thus, future prediction changes the learning factorization but not the unrestricted external policy class or ideal imitation target.
  Action-conditioned world-model learning differs by predicting outcomes under specified actions and comparing them through a control objective. We characterize the irreducible action-specific prediction error of future models that do not condition on the candidate action, identify conditions under which a world-action joint can recover an interventional forward model, and show that observational demonstrations do not identify action effects in general. Finally, we construct an environment family in which every observational learner has positive worst-case regret, whereas one informative intervention permits zero regret. The key distinction is therefore between predicting futures associated with observed behavior and predicting consequences of specified actions for policy optimization.

</details>


### [94] [Joint Causal Structure and Cluster Discovery Using Variational Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22212)
*Avni Rajpal,Anubhav Kumar,Rishabh Karnad,Mohammad Emtiyaz Khan,P. K. Srijith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于变分推断的新方法，能够同时推断潜在变量分组（聚类）和组间因果结构，而无需预先知晓分组信息。该方法通过分类分布和伯努利分布分别建模聚类和图结构的变分后验，推导变分下界并用于参数学习，在合成与真实数据上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果关系发现通常处理单个随机变量之间的关系，但在脑成像、气候建模等应用中，更有意义的是考虑变量组之间的交互。现有方法假设分组信息已知，然而实际中这些聚类以及聚类间的因果结构都是潜在的，需要同时推断。

Method: 基于变分推断的方法：分别用分类分布（categorical）和伯努利分布（Bernoulli）构建关于聚类和图结构的变分后验，推导变分下界（variational lower bound），并通过估计技术共同学习变分参数和模型参数，从而同时推断潜在聚类和因果结构。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集上验证了所提出方法在聚类发现和因果关系发现方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的变分推断框架能够在不预先知道分组的情况下，同时推断潜在变量聚类及其之间的因果结构，扩展了因果发现在组级层面的适用性。

Abstract: Causal discovery aims to understand the relationships between individual random variables. In many applications, such as brain imaging and climate modeling, it is more meaningful to consider interactions among groups of variables. Existing methods assume that knowledge of such groups or clusters is explicitly available when modeling interactions. However, in practice, these clusters as well as the causal relationships among them, are latent. In this paper, we present a novel approach based on variational inference to simultaneously infer both the latent clusters and causal structures. We learn an approximate posterior over clusters and graph-structure by considering variational distributions based on categorical and Bernoulli models respectively. We derive variational lower bounds and estimation techniques to learn variational and model parameters. The effectiveness of our proposed methods for cluster and causal discovery are demonstrated on both synthetic and real data sets.

</details>


### [95] [Counterfactual Evaluation of Temporal Observation Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22221)
*Xizhe Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究"反事实协议评估"问题：即利用已部署协议收集的数据，能否确定从未部署的替代协议的预测价值。作者证明即使拥有无限基准数据，也不一定能确定替代协议的价值，并发展了一套"价值特异性识别"理论，将可识别性问题聚焦于那些真正改变替代协议价值的潜在混淆上。针对线性目标，提出不可见协方差方向作为非可识别性的判据，并引入精准排列构造扩展到非线性聚合目标。在有限密集校准数据下给出均匀误差界，控制协议选择遗憾与可区分的价值差距，支持成本约束下的目标感知观测设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际观测系统设计中，人们常常需要评估那些从未实际部署过的替代测量方案的预测价值，但这类反事实评估能否在观测数据下成立是一个根本性的方法论问题。作者希望厘清：无限基准数据是否足以确定替代协议的价值？若不足，什么样的条件下可以识别该价值，以及如何设计校准测量来恢复识别。

Method: 作者将协议价值定义为在固定轨迹级目标下贝叶斯最优预测器的人群R^2。通过构造不同潜在协方差结构但诱导相同基准测量-目标联合分布的实例，证明非识别性。针对线性目标，提出"不可见协方差方向"作为非识别判据；对非线性聚合目标，采用精确排列构造。在有限密集校准数据下，推导均匀误差界来分析协议选择遗憾，并结合精确边际增益支持成本约束下的观测设计。

Result: 研究表明：即使无限基准数据也未必确定替代协议的价值——不同潜在协方差结构可以诱导相同的基准测量-目标分布但对应不同的替代协议价值。线性目标下，不可见协方差方向可以证明非可识别性，而定向测量无需恢复完整潜在协方差即可恢复识别。有限数据下可控制协议选择遗憾并区分可分辨的价值差距。在Sleep-EDF和Long-Term AF数据上的实验表明，粗粒度的时间布局差异比从有限数据中挑选的细粒度配置更容易被可靠地区分。

Conclusion: 该工作系统性地建立了"识别-校准分辨率-观测设计"三者的理论联系，为反事实协议评估提供了严格的识别理论与实用算法保障。它表明协议价值识别无需完全恢复潜在协方差，只需消除影响目标价值的混淆方向；同时给出了有限数据下可验证的条件，以及支持成本约束观测设计的理论依据。

Abstract: We study counterfactual protocol evaluation: whether data collected under a realised observation protocol determine the predictive value of alternatives that were never deployed. Protocol value is the population $R^2$ of the Bayes-optimal predictor of a fixed trajectory-level target from the measurements an alternative would collect. We show that even infinite benchmark data need not determine this value: distinct latent covariance structures can induce the same benchmark measurement--target law while assigning different values to the same alternative. We develop a value-specific identification theory in which only latent ambiguity that changes the alternative's value matters. For linear targets, invisible covariance directions certify non-identification, while targeted measurements can restore identification without recovering the full latent covariance; an exact permutation construction extends the result to nonlinear aggregate targets. With finite dense calibration data, uniform error bounds control protocol-selection regret and distinguishable value gaps. Exact marginal gains then support cost-constrained, target-aware observation design. Simulations and retrospective analyses of Sleep-EDF and Long-Term AF show that broad temporal-layout differences can be more reliably distinguished than fine placements selected from finite data. Together, these results connect identification, calibration resolution and observation design for undeployed protocols.

</details>


### [96] [FreKoo++: Learning Continuous Spectral Dynamics for Temporal Domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22224)
*En Yu,Xiaoyu Yang,Wei Duan,Guangquan Zhang,Jie Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FreKoo++，一种全新的连续谱动力学框架，用于时序域泛化（TDG）。它将连续Koopman模态动力学与自适应谱解耦统一，通过学习可学习的连续模态（复数特征值同时编码振荡频率与时间增长/衰减），自然适配不规则时间戳并支持任意预测视界的外推。还提出基于稳定性和谱正则化的自适应软谱加权机制，自动分离持久主导动态与瞬态噪声，无需人工频率阈值，并在离散和连续TDG基准上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TDG方法在复杂真实世界流式场景中表现不佳，难以处理多尺度漂移模式（如长期周期性交织短期增量变化）和局部不确定性，尤其在观测不规则到达的连续设定下。已有方法依赖刚性离散步进，无法自然处理不规则时间戳。

Method: FreKoo++将源域参数映射到紧凑潜在空间，将其演化建模为可学习连续模态的叠加；复数特征值联合编码振荡频率和时间增长/衰减。提出自适应软谱加权机制，辅以稳定性和谱正则化，自动分离持久主导动态与瞬态噪声。推导了模态逼近与泛化界，刻画幅度和特征值估计误差如何随预测视界传播。

Result: 在离散和连续TDG基准上的大量实验表明，FreKoo++在复杂多尺度漂移和不规则采样场景下达到最先进（SOTA）性能。

Conclusion: FreKoo++通过统一连续Koopman模态动力学与自适应谱解耦，有效解决了TDG中的多尺度漂移与不规则采样挑战，并为模态估计误差的传播提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Temporal Domain Generalization (TDG) aims to learn from historical domains and generalize to unseen future distributions under concept drift. Nevertheless, prevailing TDG methods struggle with complex real-world streaming scenarios involving both multi-scale drift patterns (e.g., long-term periodicity intertwined with short-term incremental changes) and local uncertainties, especially in continuous settings where observations arrive irregularly. To address this limitation, we propose FreKoo++, a novel continuous spectral-dynamical framework that pioneers the unification of continuous Koopman modal dynamics with adaptive spectral disentanglement. Specifically, FreKoo++ maps source-domain parameters into a compact latent space, modeling their evolution as a superposition of learnable continuous modes where complex eigenvalues jointly encode oscillatory frequency and temporal growth or decay. This formulation naturally accommodates irregular timestamps and supports arbitrary horizon extrapolation without rigid discrete stepping. Furthermore, we propose a new adaptive soft spectral weighting mechanism backed by stability and spectral regularization, which automatically isolates persistent dominant dynamics from transient noise without relying on manual frequency thresholds. We derive modal approximation and generalization bounds that characterize how amplitude and eigenvalue estimation errors propagate with the prediction horizon. Extensive experiments on both discrete and continuous TDG benchmarks demonstrate that FreKoo++ achieves state-of-the-art performance under complex multi-scale drifts and irregular sampling.

</details>


### [97] [Risk-Sensitive Reinforcement Learning with Smoothed Quantile Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22227)
*Mohammad Alipour-Vaezi,Huaiyang Zhong,Sajad Khodadadian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UCB-BQRL，一种基于模型优化的RL算法，通过下缓冲分位数准则来平滑精确分位数目标的不稳定性，并给出遗憾上界和计算复杂性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典强化学习不考虑风险敏感性，而精确分位数目标函数非光滑，在转移模型估计误差下会剧烈变化，难以可靠优化。

Method: 开发UCB-BQRL算法，为转移核维持置信集并采用下缓冲分位数准则规划；引入EVI-BQ精确动态规划过程来计算每幕的缓冲分位数策略。

Result: 建立高概率遗憾界O(e^{τ/ρ_τ}+H^2√(SAT))（对数因子忽略），提出信息论下界Ω(H/ρ_τ√(AT))，并证明精确点分位数和精确下缓冲分位数评估在多项式时间Turing归约下是PP-hard。

Conclusion: 缓冲分位数准则在保持风险敏感性的同时提高了稳定性，遗憾界匹配信息论下界（至多对数因子），且精确计算缓冲分位数即使在简单MDP中也具有计算复杂性挑战。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has achieved tremendous success in recent years. However, the classical foundations of RL do not account for the risk sensitivity of the objective function, which is critical in various fields, including healthcare, finance, etc. A popular approach to incorporate risk sensitivity is to optimize a specific quantile of the cumulative reward distribution. However, exact quantile objectives are non-smooth and can change abruptly under small perturbations of the return distribution, making them difficult to optimize reliably when the transition model must be learned from data. Motivated by this instability, we develop UCB-BQRL, a model-based optimistic learning algorithm that maintains confidence sets for the transition kernel and plans using a lower-buffered quantile criterion. The buffered criterion smooths the exact quantile objective by averaging nearby lower quantiles, thereby improving stability under transition-estimation error. To compute the buffered-quantile policy at each episode, we introduce EVI-BQ, an exact dynamic-programming procedure. We establish a high-probability regret bound for UCB-BQRL, which up to logarithmic factors scales as $\mathcal{O}(\mathrm{e}^{τ/ρ_τ}+H^2\sqrt{SAT})$, where $ρ_τ$ is denoted as the root-level left-plateau threshold, which is a problem-dependent constant. Further, we establish an information-theoretic lower bound of $Ω(H/ρ_τ\sqrt{AT})$ for the regret of any algorithm dealing with a quantile objective function. Finally, we prove that the exact point-quantile evaluation and exact lower-buffered quantile evaluation are PP-hard under polynomial-time Turing reductions, even for a fixed policy in a two-state, one-action finite-horizon MDP.

</details>


### [98] [When Test-Time Adaptation Helps, Harms, or Becomes Inactive: A Condition-Level Study on CIFAR-10-C](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22233)
*Sreeja Guha Majumdar,Aratrika Saha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对三种测试时自适应（TTA）策略在CIFAR-10-C完整基准上进行了受控比较。三种方法（BN-Adapt、TENT、EATA）在平均准确率上均显著优于源模型12.2–13.3个百分点，但每种方法在8.0–9.3%的条件下反而劣于源模型，这些失败集中在低严重度的亮度、雾、对比度和散焦模糊等条件。更重要的是，EATA的行为与梯度无关的BN-Adapt基线几乎一致（平均绝对差仅0.09个百分点），表明可靠性过滤可能过度限制了有效自适应，使TTA在部分条件下失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTA方法的评估主要依赖聚合准确率，这掩盖了自适应在何种条件下失败或几乎无效的细节。作者希望揭示聚合指标背后的系统性失败模式，并深入理解不同TTA策略的实际行为差异。

Method: 在完整CIFAR-10-C基准（15种腐蚀类型×5种严重度）上，对三种TTA策略（BN-Adapt、TENT、EATA的可靠性过滤简化版）与未自适应源模型进行受控比较，使用Wilcoxon符号秩检验评估显著性，并逐条件分析各方法的性能优势和劣势。

Result: 三种方法均比源模型平均提升12.2–13.3个百分点（p<10^-12）。但每种方法在8.0–9.3%的条件下低于源模型，失败集中在低严重度腐蚀（源模型已接近天花板），特别是亮度、雾、对比度和散焦模糊。EATA与BN-Adapt的平均绝对差仅0.09个百分点，而TENT与两者相差约1.08个百分点。

Conclusion: 聚合准确率会掩盖TTA的系统性失败模式。可靠性过滤机制可能使EATA在行为上更接近BatchNorm统计基线而非熵最小化方法，表明需要引入条件级别的评估来识别适应何时有帮助、何时有害、何时实际上未激活。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) aims to improve model robustness under distribution shift by adapting a source model using unlabeled test data. Although methods such as TENT and EATA have demonstrated gains on corrupted data, aggregate accuracy can obscure the conditions under which adaptation fails or provides little benefit. We present a controlled comparison of three TTA strategies---BatchNorm-statistics adaptation (BN-Adapt), entropy-minimization adaptation (TENT), and reliability-filtered adaptation (a scoped re-implementation of EATA)---against an unadapted source model on the full CIFAR-10-C benchmark, covering 15 corruption types and 5 severity levels. All three methods improve mean accuracy over the source model by 12.2--13.3 percentage points (Wilcoxon signed-rank $p < 10^{-12}$). However, each method underperforms the source model on 8.0--9.3\% of conditions, with failures concentrated in low-severity corruptions where the source model already performs near ceiling, particularly brightness, fog, contrast, and defocus blur. We further find that EATA closely tracks the gradient-free BN-Adapt baseline, with a mean absolute difference of 0.09 percentage points, compared with 1.08 percentage points relative to TENT. This suggests that reliability filtering can substantially restrict effective adaptation, causing EATA to behave more like a BatchNorm-statistics baseline than an entropy-minimization method. These results show that aggregate accuracy alone can mask systematic TTA failure modes and motivate condition-level evaluation of when adaptation helps, harms, or becomes effectively inactive.

</details>


### [99] [A Query-Time Framework for Transient 2D Pore-Scale Flow Prediction and Generative Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22235)
*Yiming Wang,Jiale Zhu,Zhichen Ye,Yandong Lv,Shiqi Wang,Jinlong Liu,Yucheng Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CT-PoreFlow，一种基于几何条件的时间连续孔隙尺度流动代理模型，并构建了包含7,606个二维多孔结构的QSGS-Transient-7606基准数据集。该模型通过拓扑感知几何编码、压缩谱混合和log时间条件化，结合末期通量校准目标，在未见测试几何上达到0.2248的流速相对L2误差和12.81%的终端渗透率误差。该框架还集成到逆向设计流程中，筛选9,216个生成候选，引导式GAN采样实现98.11%的通孔连接率和72.28%的条件设计成功率，统一了瞬态流动预测、输运感知筛选和LBM验证的逆向设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孔隙尺度流动控制着多孔介质工程应用中的输运和渗透行为，但传统的格子玻尔兹曼方法（LBM）在多几何和多设计查询下的重复模拟成本高昂，限制了其在反复设计部署中的实用性。

Method: 将瞬态孔隙尺度流动预测表述为几何条件下的查询时算子，构建含7,606个二维多孔结构（每个配30个对数采样LBM状态）的QSGS-Transient-7606基准。提出的CT-PoreFlow代理模型集成三种关键技术：拓扑感知几何编码、压缩谱混合（spectral mixing）以及log时间条件化，并采用晚期通量校准目标进行训练。代理模型嵌入逆向设计工作流，用于筛选9,216个GAN和扩散模型候选（覆盖18个属性目标），随后由LBM验证。

Result: 在未见测试几何上，CT-PoreFlow的流速相对L2误差为0.2248，终端渗透率误差为12.81%。冻结形态学和CT图像审计确认了无需微调即可保持合理的跨几何鲁棒性。在逆向设计中，引导式GAN采样达到98.11%的通过连通性和72.28%的条件设计成功率，优于扩散模型的生成效果。

Conclusion: 该框架将瞬态流动预测、输运感知筛选和LBM验证的逆向设计统一于多孔介质场景中，证明了基于几何条件的时间连续代理模型能够有效替代昂贵的逐次LBM模拟，并显著提升设计效率。

Abstract: Pore-scale flow governs transport and permeability behaviour in porous media engineering applications, yet repeated lattice Boltzmann method (LBM) simulation across many geometries and design queries remains costly for repeated deployment. This study formulates transient pore-scale flow prediction as a geometry-conditioned query-time operator and introduces QSGS-Transient-7606, a benchmark of 7,606 two-dimensional porous structures each paired with 30 logarithmically sampled LBM states. The proposed continuous-time pore-scale flow surrogate model (CT-PoreFlow) integrates topology-aware geometry encoding, compressed spectral mixing, and log-time conditioning with a late-time flux-calibration objective. On unseen test geometries, CT-PoreFlow achieves a velocity relative L2 of 0.2248 and a terminal permeability error of 12.81%. Frozen morphology and computed tomography image audits confirm reasonable cross-geometry robustness without fine-tuning. The surrogate is then embedded in an inverse design workflow, screening 9,216 generative adversarial network and diffusion candidates across 18 property targets prior to LBM verification. Guided GAN sampling attains 98.11% through-connectivity and 72.28% conditional design success, exceeding diffusion-based generation. The framework unifies transient flow prediction, transport-aware screening, and LBM-verified inverse design for porous media.

</details>


### [100] [Toward a First-Principles Update Geometry for the Language-Model Head](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22253)
*Aditya Somasundaram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将语言模型头与softmax视为单一模块，推导出参数更新在Hilbert投影距离下的更新几何性质，并提出一种基于行分离最大化的优化目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常孤立地分析权重矩阵的几何性质，忽略了语言模型头与softmax组合后的整体更新行为。作者希望从两者组合的角度理解更新的几何特性。

Method: 将语言模型头与softmax作为一个整体模块分析，使用Hilbert投影距离度量更新对输出分布的最大影响，推导出该影响等于H乘以词元行向量的欧氏直径。借鉴Muon优化器的奇异值条件化思想，提出在约束直径的同时最大化最小的行分离距离，当V远大于d时该问题近似为等距问题。

Result: 推导出更新S在Hilbert投影距离下的最大影响为H·max_{i<j}||s_i-s_j||_2，即H乘以词元行向量的欧氏直径；并提出约束直径条件下最大化最小行分离的优化目标。

Conclusion: 语言模型头与softmax组合的更新几何可以用Hilbert投影距离和词元行向量的欧氏直径来刻画，在此基础上可构建近似等距的优化问题来改善更新质量。

Abstract: We study the language-model head and softmax as a single module, deriving an update geometry from their composition rather than from the weight matrix in isolation. Under Hilbert's projective distance, the maximum change caused by an update $S$ over $\left|\left|{h}\right|\right|_2\le H$ is $H\max_{i<j}\left|\left|{s_i-s_j}\right|\right|_2$, which is $H$ times the Euclidean diameter of its token rows. Motivated by Muon's singular-value conditioning, we propose maximizing the smallest row separation while constraining this diameter, producing an approximate-equidistance problem when $V\gg d$.

</details>


### [101] [DAW: Dynamics-Aware Weighting for Deep Learning Forecasts of Chaotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22277)
*Zhou Fang,Gianmarco Mengaldo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动力学感知加权（DAW），利用动力系统理论中的局部维度d作为状态活跃自由度的先验度量，对深度学习代理模型的损失函数进行重加权，以优先分配模型容量到高维、动态复杂的暂态区域，从而缓解混沌时空系统（如Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程）在长期自回归预测中的灾难性误差累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混沌时空系统的深度学习代理模型在长期自回归预测中存在灾难性误差累积。其根本原因是这类系统（如KS方程）在相空间中的访问不均匀：大部分时间停留在低维、准静止的层流状态，而罕见的高维拓扑跃迁（如波合并事件）虽然出现频率低，却引发不成比率的局部误差。标准均匀目标的样本级损失函数将有限模型容量浪费在统计上占多数的静止状态，忽视了高影响但稀疏的动态暂态。现有的不均衡回归方法按目标空间密度重加权，但统计稀有性并不等同于动力学内在稀有性（吸引子的回归几何）。因此需要一种基于动力学本质而非统计频率的加权策略。

Method: 提出DAW（Dynamics-Aware Weighting）框架，一种数据驱动的目标重加权方法。核心思想是利用动力系统理论中的局部维度d作为每个状态活跃自由度的先验度量，以此重新塑形损失景观，将表示容量分配给高d值的稀疏区域——即预测误差系统性偏大的动力学复杂区域。该方法与纯统计密度加权（按目标空间密度重加权）形成对比，更关注吸引子的递归几何而非目标空间的统计分布。

Result: 在混沌KS方程上，DAW在长期自回归误差上一致优于均匀训练、纯统计密度加权及其随机置换消融实验基线。事件级分析表明，DAW通过抑制d值急剧跳变期间（即伴随波合并等复杂物理过程）的局部误差放大来实现这一改进。

Conclusion: 统计上的稀有性不等于动力学上的稀有性；基于吸引子递归几何（局部维度d）的加权策略比基于目标空间统计密度的重加权更有效地提升混沌系统深度代理模型的长程预测能力。DAW通过将容量聚焦于高维复杂暂态，抑制了d跳变引发的误差放大。

Abstract: Deep learning surrogates for forecasting chaotic dynamical systems suffer from catastrophic error accumulation over long-term autoregressive rollouts. This behavior is partly tied to the underlying systems: chaotic spatiotemporal systems, such as the Kuramoto-Sivashinsky (KS) equation, visit phase space unevenly - dominated by recurrent, low-dimensional quiescent states (e.g., near-laminar flows) and punctuated by rare, dynamically complex topological transitions (e.g., wave-merging events). Under a sample-wise uniform objective, standard neural surrogates allocate their finite capacity to the statistically numerous quiescent states, under-representing the transient regimes that trigger disproportionate, localized errors. Existing imbalanced-regression methods reweight samples by target-space density. However, statistical target-space rarity need not coincide with the intrinsic dynamical rarity - the recurrence geometry of the attractor that is the source of the imbalance. To address this, we introduce Dynamics-Aware Weighting (DAW), a data-centric objective reweighting framework. Using the local dimension $d$ from dynamical systems theory as an a priori measure of a state's active degrees of freedom, DAW reshapes the loss landscape to allocate representational capacity toward the sparse, high-$d$ regimes where forecast errors are systematically large. On the chaotic KS equation, DAW consistently outperforms uniform training, purely statistical density weighting, and its randomly permuted ablation, reducing long-term autoregressive error relative to all baselines. Event-level analysis shows that DAW achieves this by suppressing the localized error amplifications incurred during sharp jumps in $d$, which accompany complex physical processes such as wave-merging in the KS system.

</details>


### [102] [StocBench: A Benchmark for Generative Modeling of Stochastic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22309)
*Sebastian Pfister,Benjamin Holzschuh,Nils Thuerey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对基于输运的生成模型和基于蒸馏的少步方法在随机流体概率预测中的性能进行了基准测试，重点关注有限推理预算下的表现。在二维Kolmogorov流随机强迫任务上，flow matching在高推理预算下取得最准确的一步条件分布，而二阶指数积分器DPM-2在极低NFE下最强。少步蒸馏方法具有竞争力且能很好地保持涡量谱。在确定性控制任务中，蒸馏模型表现最差，说明模型性能无法跨设置迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机流体流动的概率预测在有限推理预算下需要高效的生成模型，但目前缺乏对输运类生成模型与蒸馏类少步方法在受限推理资源下的系统性比较。需要厘清不同方法在分布准确性、不变测度保持以及不同任务设置（随机vs确定性）下的性能差异。

Method: 在二维Kolmogorov流（带随机强迫）上对所有方法进行基准测试。测量一步分布准确性（对比大规模模拟参考系综），并通过涡量谱评估自回归滚动中不变测度是否被保持。同时设计了一个确定性控制任务（预测区间内的强迫可观测），用以分离偶然不确定性与认知不确定性。

Result: 高推理预算下flow matching的一步条件分布最准确；极低NFE下DPM-2（二阶指数积分器）最强；少步蒸馏方法与多步方法竞争力相当且涡量谱保持尤佳。蒸馏模型在随机任务上竞争力强但在控制任务上最不准确。随机扩散采样器（DDPM）在随机设置下更好保持涡量谱，而确定性采样器（DDIM、DPM-2）在确定性设置下谱保持更好。

Conclusion: 模型性能在随机预测与确定性控制两种设置之间不能相互迁移，选择最优方法需结合具体任务场景和推理预算约束。蒸馏方法适合随机任务，但在确定性控制任务上存在明显短板。

Abstract: We benchmark transport-based generative models as well as distillation-based few-step methods for the probabilistic forecasting of stochastic fluid flows, with a particular focus on performance under limited inference budgets. All methods are evaluated on a two-dimensional Kolmogorov flow with stochastic forcing. We measure one-step distributional accuracy against large simulated reference ensembles and assess whether the invariant measure is preserved during autoregressive rollouts via the enstrophy spectrum. On the stochastic task, flow matching achieves the most accurate one-step conditional distribution at high inference budgets, while the second-order exponential integrator DPM-2 is strongest at very low NFE. Few-step distillation methods are competitive with the multi-step methods and preserve the enstrophy spectrum particularly well. A deterministic control task, in which the forcing over the prediction interval is observed, separates aleatoric from epistemic uncertainty. Model performance does not translate between the two settings: the distilled models are competitive on the stochastic task but least accurate on the control task. While stochastic diffusion samplers such as DDPM better preserve the enstrophy spectrum during rollouts in the stochastic setting, deterministic samplers such as DDIM and DPM-2 show better spectral preservation in the deterministic setting.

</details>


### [103] [Does a Modern-Handwriting Warm-Up Help Historical Arabic OCR? A Reproducible, Compute-Matched Evaluation on Muharaf and KHATT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22316)
*Sumaih Almarshad,Maram Alamri,Dona Aloraini,Fares Altuwaim,AlJawharh AlOtaibi,Reem Alyabis,Rayah Aldawsari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑了"现代阿拉伯语手写体中间训练有助于历史阿拉伯语手写文本识别(HTR)"这一结论的稳健性。作者通过四次名义相同的消融实验发现效果从-17.64到+14.52 CER点之间摆动甚至反向；剔除含混杂因素(run)后，两次干净实验显示无显著效果。计算匹配实验显示KHATT热身与同域对照相比差+2.42 CER点，其中手写域特有损失仅约0.6点，为小幅负效应而非普适结论。同时发布了SaudiHeritage-OCR工具包供独立验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常仅基于单一实现和单一比较就断言中间训练阶段对历史阿拉伯语HTR有益或有害，这种依据过于单薄。作者旨在通过重复多次消融实验来检验这种结论的稳定性，并揭示单一实现得出的紧置信区间无法推广到其他实现的问题。

Method: 作者执行了四次名义相同的消融实验，让基础检查点、编码器冻结策略、epoch预算、精度和学习率调度自然变化（如同实际开发中的情况），同时固定归一化、评分器和区间估计。每次实验比较"KHATT现代手写热身+ Muharaf历史手稿微调"与"直接对Muharaf微调"。随后进行计算匹配实验，在相同预算下对三个种子比较KHATT热身与同域控制，并量化手写域特有的效应部分。

Result: 四次运行中估计效果在-17.64到+14.52 CER点之间摆动并反转符号；两个极端恰好是带可识别混杂因素的两组（一个是低五倍的学习率，一个是来源不明的检查点），两次干净运行得到-0.25与+0.94，即无效果。计算匹配实验中，KHATT热身比匹配的同域对照差+2.42 CER点(95%区间[+0.60,+4.25])，但其中手写域特有部分仅约0.6点。

Conclusion: 单一实现得出的紧置信区间无法推广到其他实现，中间现代手写训练的效应在此配置下表现为小幅负效应而非普适结论。作者发布SaudiHeritage-OCR包（包含归一化器、区间评分器、验证过的KHATT解码器、实验清单、VLM基线及版本对齐协议），并严格将Al-Mahd铭文行排除、不作为基准，以确保结果可独立复现验证。

Abstract: Whether an intermediate stage of modern Arabic handwriting helps or hurts historical Arabic HTR is usually decided from one implementation and one comparison, too thin a basis for a claim either way. We test stability by running the same nominal ablation four times, letting the base checkpoint, encoder-freezing strategy, epoch budget, precision, and learning-rate schedule vary as they naturally did during development, while holding the normalization, scorer, and interval estimation fixed. Each run compares intermediate training on modern handwriting (KHATT) then fine-tuning on historical manuscripts (Muharaf) against fine-tuning on Muharaf directly. Across the four runs the estimated effect swings from -17.64 to +14.52 CER points and reverses sign. The two extremes are exactly the two runs with an identifiable confound (a fivefold lower learning rate in one; a checkpoint of undisclosed provenance in the other); the two clean runs land at -0.25 and +0.94, i.e. no effect. A tight interval from one implementation says nothing about the next. We then run a compute-matched experiment with identical budgets over three seeds: KHATT warm-up is +2.42 CER points worse than a matched same-domain control (95% interval [+0.60, +4.25]); the part of that gap specific to the handwriting domain is only about 0.6 points a small negative effect under this configuration, not a universal result. We release a SaudiHeritage-OCR package with the normalizer, interval scorer, a verified KHATT decoder, experimental manifests, VLM baselines, and an edition-alignment protocol, so the result can be checked independently. The Al-Mahd inscription line is held strictly out and is not offered as a benchmark.

</details>


### [104] [Beyond Dense Adam States: Adaptive Log-Space Quantization for Memory-Efficient Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22322)
*Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应对数空间（Adaptive Log-Space, AL）量化方法，针对低精度优化器状态（optimizer states）在非Adam类内存高效优化器（如Adafactor、CAME、APOLLO）中的异质性误差传播问题，通过逐块自适应非零范围并保留精确零来量化非负状态。在96次运行（总计214.7 GPU小时）中评估AdamW、Adafactor、CAME和APOLLO路径，AL8+8位动量在TinyLlama-1.1B基准上达到72.90困惑度（FP32为72.48，动态量化基线为73.54），并将优化器状态存储从8392.7降至2119.2 MiB；同时引入拓扑感知参数保护将量化Adafactor的后期损失差距从+0.1185降至+0.0159。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低精度优化器状态方法主要针对稠密的Adam风格矩设计，但内存高效优化器维护因子化、基于置信度或投影的状态，其量化误差传播方式与稠密Adam不同。这种异质性尚未被系统地表征，导致现有量化策略在这些优化器上性能不佳或精度不足。作者希望通过刻画不同优化器状态的量化误差异质性，提出更适配的量化方案。

Method: 1) 从语言模型预训练中表征不同优化器（AdamW、Adafactor、CAME、APOLLO）状态的量化误差特征；2) 提出自适应对数空间（AL）量化：一种逐块（block-wise）的表示方法，针对非负状态自适应调整每个块的非零范围，同时保留精确的零值；3) 将AL8和AL16与独立的符号动量编码及状态特定精度选择相结合；4) 引入拓扑感知的参数保护（topology-aware parameter protection）以减小量化Adafactor的晚期损失差距。

Result: 在96次运行共214.7 GPU小时的实验中：TinyLlama-1.1B 20K步基准上，AdamW采用AL8二阶矩+8位均匀动量达到72.90困惑度（FP32为72.48，8位动态量化基线为73.54），优化器状态存储从8392.7降至2119.2 MiB。CAME的非负状态需要更高精度：AL16达到86.16困惑度（FP32为86.68），而全AL8仅为90.19。GPT-2 100K步实验中，拓扑感知参数保护将量化Adafactor的后期损失差距从+0.1185降至+0.0159。

Conclusion: 研究支持状态感知和拓扑感知的优化器量化策略。不同优化器状态对量化的敏感度存在显著差异（如CAME的非负状态比AdamW需要更高精度），通过状态特定的精度选择和拓扑感知的参数保护可以显著降低量化带来的精度损失，同时大幅减少优化器状态的内存占用。

Abstract: Low-precision optimizer-state methods are commonly designed for dense Adam-style moments, but memory-efficient optimizers maintain factored, confidence-based, or projected states whose quantization errors propagate differently. We characterize this heterogeneity in optimizer-state traces from language-model pre-training and introduce Adaptive Log-Space (AL) quantization, a block-wise representation for non-negative states that adapts its nonzero range per block while preserving exact zeros. AL8 and AL16 are combined with independent signed-momentum encodings and state-specific precision choices.
  Across 96 runs totaling 214.7 GPU-hours, we evaluate AdamW, Adafactor, CAME, and APOLLO paths. On a 20K-step TinyLlama-1.1B benchmark, AdamW with AL8 second moments and 8-bit uniform momentum reaches 72.90 perplexity, versus 72.48 for FP32 and 73.54 for an 8-bit dynamic-quantization baseline, while reducing measured optimizer-state storage from 8392.7 to 2119.2 MiB. CAME requires higher precision for its non-negative states: AL16 reaches 86.16 perplexity versus 86.68 for FP32, while all-AL8 reaches 90.19. In a 100K-step GPT-2 experiment, topology-aware parameter protection reduces the late-loss gap of quantized Adafactor from +0.1185 to +0.0159. These results support state- and topology-aware optimizer quantization. End-to-end comparisons use a single training seed and are reported as empirical measurements.

</details>


### [105] [Gaussian process learning with flow map refinement for parameter estimation in dynamical systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22324)
*Yue Hao,Dongwei Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GPL-FMR，一个两阶段的参数估计框架，结合高斯过程学习与流映射细化，解决梯度/导数匹配方法在稀疏且有噪声观测下全局不一致的问题，通过多个动力系统实例验证其参数估计精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于高斯过程的梯度/导数匹配方法虽避免了重复数值积分并强制局部导数一致性，但在观测稀疏且有噪声时，局部匹配可能导致与全局流映射不一致，从而影响参数估计精度。作者旨在解决这一局限。

Method: 提出两阶段框架GPL-FMR：第一阶段采用高斯过程学习算法获得参数估计的后验分布，并将其作为信息先验传递到第二阶段；第二阶段基于流映射细化（flow-map refinement）进行优化，利用全局动力学约束进一步改进参数估计。

Result: 在Van der Pol振荡器、Lotka-Volterra模型和Lorenz-63系统等多个数值示例上验证了所提框架，结果显示该框架在稀疏且有噪声的观测条件下持续提高了参数估计精度。

Conclusion: GPL-FMR通过将高斯过程学习的局部信息与流映射的全局动力学约束相结合，有效地解决了参数估计中的全局一致性问题，特别是在数据稀缺和有噪声的场景下表现出显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Parameter estimation is a central task in data-driven learning of dynamical systems. It aims to recover the underlying physical parameters from observed time-series data, thereby providing interpretable insights into the physical mechanisms governing the system. Gradient/derivative matching methods based on Gaussian process provide an efficient way to perform parameter estimation. Those methods avoid repeated numerical integration and enforce local derivative consistency. However, such local matching may result in global inconsistency with the governing flow map, particularly under scarce and noisy observations. To address this limitation, we propose a framework based on Gaussian process learning with flow map refinement (GPL-FMR), a two-stage parameter estimation framework. The first stage is based on Gaussian process learning algorithm and the posterior obtained from which is transferred as an informative prior to the second stage based on flow-map refinement. The second stage further improves the parameter estimation via optimisation based on global dynamical constraints. We demonstrate and analyse its performance on multiple numerical examples, including the Van der Pol oscillator, the Lotka-Volterra model, and the Lorenz-63 system. The results show that the proposed framework consistently improves parameter estimation accuracy, particularly under scarce and noisy observations.

</details>


### [106] [SANE: State Anomaly Neutralization for Stable Extreme-Context Delta-Rule Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22354)
*Qingwen Lin,Boyan Xu,Xiao Liu,Zhifeng Hao,Ruichu Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文诊断了Delta-Rule递归模型（RWKV-7）在超长序列（1亿token）下的失效模式为"局部范数爆炸+稀疏基底"，而非全局状态饱和，并提出SANE（状态异常中和）方法，通过在块边界应用自适应tanh压缩，在100M token前缀下保持推理能力（33.46-35.56）而基线数值溢出，且不损害短上下文基准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Delta-Rule递归模型虽具有O(1)推理内存优势，但在极端上下文外推下可能变得不稳定。理解其失效的具体模式是设计有效稳定策略的前提。作者希望超越"全局状态饱和"这一笼统认知，精确定位故障形态，从而提出针对性的解决方案。

Method: 首先通过追踪RWKV-7在长达1亿token序列上的行为，实证识别出失效模式为局部化范数爆炸发生于相对稀疏的基底上。基于递归更新分析，发现持久衰减使弱更新条目保持小值，而不均匀注入使少数通道累积极端值。据此提出SANE方法，在块边界应用自适应tanh压缩，同时保留块内并行结构。

Result: 在安全阈值范围（3≤α≤5）内，SANE在11个短上下文推理基准上与基线无统计显著差异；在超过训练长度24000倍的100M token前缀后，SANE保持功能推理（33.46-35.56）而基线数值溢出。过度宽松的阈值（α≥8）虽然数值稳定但完全丧失推理能力。

Conclusion: 数值稳定并不等同于功能推理，状态压缩存在容量-稳定性权衡。SANE展示了精确定位异常模式并针对性干预的有效性，既能在极端外推下维持推理能力，又不损害正常范围内的性能。

Abstract: Delta-Rule recurrent models maintain a fixed-size state, enabling $O(1)$ inference memory but potentially becoming unstable under extreme-context extrapolation. By tracking RWKV-7 over sequences of up to 100M tokens, we empirically identify a distinct failure pattern: \textbf{localized norm explosion atop a relatively sparse substrate}, rather than global state saturation. Analysis of the recurrent update suggests that persistent decay keeps weakly updated entries small, whereas uneven injections allow a few channels to accumulate extreme values. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose \textbf{State Anomaly Neutralization (SANE)}, which applies adaptive $\tanh$ compression at chunk boundaries while preserving the intra-chunk parallel structure. Within a safe threshold range ($3 \le α\le 5$), SANE matches the baseline on 11 short-context reasoning benchmarks with no statistically significant degradation. After a 100M-token prefix, which exceeds the training length by over $24{,}000\times$, SANE retains functional reasoning ($33.46$--$35.56$) while the baseline encounters numerical overflow. In contrast, overly permissive thresholds ($α\ge 8$) remain numerically stable but lose reasoning capability entirely, showing that numerical stabilization alone does not guarantee functional reasoning and revealing a capacity--stability trade-off in state compression.

</details>


### [107] [Tracing the Unlabeled Storm: Cross-Variable Transfer in a Lagrangian Atmospheric JEPA Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22358)
*K M Anirudh,S Sandeep,Hariprasad Kodamana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出M-JEPA，一种多尺度季风联合嵌入预测架构，通过跨变量代理学习（cross-variable proxy learning）在大尺度连续大气代理场（如射出长波辐射OLR）上预训练，完全无需降雨监督，再冻结表征迁移到日降水预报任务。相比直接在降雨上训练，代理预训练使CRPS误差降低36%，且以1540万参数在单消费级GPU上超越51成员ECMWF运行集合的部分指标，特别是在强降雨阈值和精细空间尺度上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度大气对流支配南亚季风变率，但直接从零膨胀、重尾分布的降水数据学习潜在世界模型会产生次优预测表征。连续大气代理场（如OLR）能更连贯地表达对流组织，因此有必要通过跨变量代理学习来规避降水数据的分布特性带来的学习困难。

Method: M-JEPA在多尺度上对五个连续代理场进行JEPA（Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture）预训练，训练数据为追踪移动对流系统的拉格朗日补丁，全程无降雨监督。冻结后的表征通过共享解码器主干（含并行概率分支和确定性分支）迁移到日降水预报。采用冻结骨干探针框架，设置两个对照：完全在降雨上训练的相同架构和随机初始化骨干，以归因代理预训练带来的迁移增益。

Result: 代理预训练的表征在下游降水预报中CRPS误差为5.54 mm/day，相比直接在降雨上训练的7.52 mm/day降低36%。与51成员ECMWF运行集合相比，迁移模型在CRPS（6.81 vs 6.89 mm/day）和Brier技能（+0.05 vs -0.04）上取得统计显著的解析优势，优势集中在重雨阈值和细空间尺度；但集合在邻域技能和点尺度确定性参考上仍保有优势。

Conclusion: 基于季风内尺度（intraseasonal）动力学的代理预训练表征能够提供具有竞争力的季风降水预报，并建立了一套评估跨变量迁移后大气表征的诊断框架，为从连续代理场学习可迁移对流世界模型提供了有力证据。

Abstract: Deep atmospheric convection governs South Asian monsoon variability, yet attempting to learn its latent world model directly from zero-inflated, heavy-tailed precipitation yields suboptimal predictive representations. Continuous atmospheric proxies, such as outgoing longwave radiation (OLR), express this convective organization far more coherently. We address this mismatch with \emph{cross-variable proxy learning}: M-JEPA, a multiscale Monsoon Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture, is pretrained on five continuous proxy fields over Lagrangian patches tracking moving convective systems---without rainfall supervision at any point. The resulting frozen representation is transferred to daily precipitation forecasts through a shared decoder trunk featuring parallel probabilistic and deterministic branches. Because rainfall is strictly unobserved during pretraining, downstream skill directly measures the predictive information captured in the latent rollout. A frozen-backbone probing framework with two controls (an identical architecture trained on rainfall alone, and a randomly initialized backbone) attributes the transfer specifically to proxy pretraining: direct rainfall training exhibits $36\%$ higher CRPS error ($7.52$ vs.\ $5.54$\,mm/day). Against the 51-member operational ECMWF ensemble, the transferred model attains a statistically resolved CRPS advantage ($6.81$ vs.\ $6.89$\,mm/day) and higher Brier skill ($+0.05$ vs.\ $-0.04$) using $15.4$M parameters on a single consumer GPU, concentrated at heavy-rain thresholds and fine spatial scales, while the ensemble retains an advantage in neighborhood skill and deterministic references on point metrics. The result provides a competitive monsoon precipitation forecast grounded in intraseasonal dynamics and a diagnostic framework for evaluating transferred atmospheric representations.

</details>


### [108] [Self-Supervised Graph Representation Learning for In-The-Wild Wearable and Smartphone based Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22387)
*Ioannis N. Ziogas,Leontios J. Hadjileontiadis,Ahsan H. Khandoker,Aamna Al Shehhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自监督图掩码增强的多任务归纳图神经网络，用于可穿戴与智能手机情绪识别（WER）中的图节点分类，通过子图采样结合监督、半监督和SSL机制，在仅使用20%和25%标签的情况下，于二分类唤醒度和效价任务上分别获得4.3%和7.8%的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景下的情绪标签采集困难且存在偏差，个体间及个体内的情绪变异性高，传统的全资源监督学习方案难以适应有限标签的WER设置。

Method: 采用子图采样策略结合已标注和未标注数据，构建多任务归纳图神经网络，融合监督学习、半监督学习与自监督学习（SSL）图掩码增强机制，在有限的标签资源下通过图节点分类建模情绪。

Result: 在K-EmoPhone数据集上通过留一组交叉验证，在二分类唤醒度任务上使用20%标签获得4.3%准确率提升，在效价任务上使用25%标签获得7.8%准确率提升，均优于全资源设置。

Conclusion: 模型分析揭示了SSL图增强与情绪唤醒度、效价之间的关联，验证了SSL驱动的子图训练方法在真实环境WER任务中的有效性与合理性。

Abstract: Wearable and smartphone-based emotion recognition (WER) remains a challenging setting in affective computing, due to the notorious difficulty and bias associated with in-the-wild label collection. The high inter-and intra-subject emotional variability motivates us to explore WER modeling through graph node classification in a limited resources learning scheme powered by Self-Supervised Learning (SSL) graph masking augmentation tasks. We employ a subgraph sampling approach during training, utilizing labeled and unlabeled data, along with supervised, semi-supervised, and SSL mechanisms in a multi-task inductive graph neural network architecture. Our evaluations on K-EmoPhone through leave-one-group-out cross-validation in the binary arousal and valence tasks yield average accuracy gains of 4.3% and 7.8%, compared to the full resource setting, utilizing only 20% and 25% of the labels, respectively. Our model analysis sheds light on the relation of SSL graph augmentations to emotional arousal and valence and justifies the approach of SSL-driven subgraph training for in-the-wild WER.

</details>


### [109] [Dual-Scale State-Space Modeling with Speaker-Wise Dynamic CRF for Speech Emotion Recognition in Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22399)
*Guan-Hua Wen,Kuan-Yu Chen,Hou-Chiang Tseng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DSSM-CRF音频情感识别架构，通过双向状态空间模型融合多尺度自监督语音表征，并用独立的动态条件随机场链对每位说话者的情感演化进行建模，在IEMOCAP和MELD数据集上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话语音情感识别需同时处理跨时间尺度的声学证据、说话者间的上下文影响以及说话者自身的情感演化，但现有方法未能明确分离这两种交互过程。

Method: 1）双向状态空间模型在帧级和对话级两个尺度编码融合的自监督语音表征；2）解码器将每位说话者的话语排序成独立的动态条件随机场链，转移对得分融合语料库级转移矩阵与从两个上下文化话语预测的残差；3）辅助目标监督情感变化但对数不参与Viterbi推理，确保对话者轮次影响上下文情感得分但不被当作另一说话者情感轨迹中的转移。

Result: 在IEMOCAP上达到75.81% UA和74.90% WA，在MELD上达到54.72% WA和49.31% WF1；匹配对照实验证明说话者因子分解与CRF建模带来的互补增益。

Conclusion: 明确分离说话者间上下文影响与说话者内情感演化两大过程，能有效提升对话语音情感识别性能，体现了序列结构建模在情感轨迹预测中的价值。

Abstract: Conversational speech emotion recognition must reconcile acoustic evidence across temporal scales with two interaction processes: cross-speaker contextual influence and within-speaker emotion evolution. We propose DSSM-CRF, an audio-only architecture that explicitly separates these processes. Bidirectional state-space models encode fused self-supervised speech representations at frame and dialogue scales, so each utterance representation captures local prosody and context from all speakers. The decoder then orders each speaker's utterances into an independent dynamic conditional random field chain. Consecutive utterances in a speaker's chain form a transition pair whose score combines a corpus-level transition matrix with a residual predicted from the two contextualized utterances. An auxiliary objective supervises whether each pair changes emotion but does not participate in Viterbi inference. Thus, interlocutor turns affect contextual emotion scores without being treated as transitions in another speaker's emotion trajectory. DSSM-CRF achieves 75.81% UA and 74.90% WA on IEMOCAP, and 54.72% WA and 49.31% WF1 on MELD. Matched controls demonstrate complementary gains from speaker-wise factorization and CRF modeling.

</details>


### [110] [Geometric Structures on Graphs: a Holonomy-Based Discretization of Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22453)
*Hao Li,Yuhan Peng,Junwen Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于和乐的图曲率离散化框架，在带局部对称正定度量（SPD）的图上定义曲率，通过三角回路上的和乐观测值来驱动度量的指数更新，从而在图上实现黎曼式的曲率-度量动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图上的曲率离散化方法往往将和乐视为启发式特征，没有遵循微分几何的基本原理——无穷小和乐受曲率控制。作者希望在图论环境中忠实离散化这一几何原理，使图上的曲率观测能够驱动对局部SPD度量的更新，同时保持度量的正定性和规范等变性。

Method: 1) 每个顶点携带纤维度量g_i，每条有向边携带可逆且度量相容的传输F_{ij}；2) 沿有向三角回路的有序乘积给出和乐H_C，其归一化对数Ω_C=-s_C^{-1}Log(H_C)作为有限回路曲率观测；3) 由于Ω_C位于g_i-正交李代数中，不是SPD度量的速度场，故引入两种聚合机制：与对称响应矩阵的对易子产生对称的Ricci型度量响应，以及基于关联的曲率诱导边通量的协变散度；4) 响应具有局部正交规范等变性，可驱动保持正定性的指数更新；5) 给出可逆度量相容的边传输参数化，使正交边因子、回路尺度、权重和响应矩阵可在尊重图几何的同时被学习。

Result: 在单位球面上进行已知几何的校准实验，检验了：和乐-曲率关系的正确性、在非平凡局部度量表示下曲率保持性、以及从局部观测中经验性地恢复边传输的能力。

Conclusion: 该框架将微分几何中"无穷小和乐由曲率控制"的原理以原理性（而非启发式）的方式离散化到图上，提供了规范等变的Ricci型度量更新机制，并支持端到端学习边传输参数化，在已知几何上验证了框架的有效性。

Abstract: We propose a holonomy-based framework for discretizing curvature on graphs equipped with local symmetric positive-definite metrics. Each vertex carries a fibre metric \(g_i\), and each directed edge carries a reversible metric-compatible transport \(F_{ij}\). The ordered product around an oriented triangular loop \(\mathcal C\) gives a holonomy \(H_{\mathcal C}\), whose normalized logarithm \(Ω_{\mathcal C}=-s_{\mathcal C}^{-1}\operatorname{Log}(H_{\mathcal C})\) is used as a finite-loop curvature observation. Thus the construction discretizes the geometric principle that infinitesimal holonomy is controlled by curvature, rather than treating holonomy as a heuristic feature. Since \(Ω_{\mathcal C}\) lies in the \(g_i\)-orthogonal Lie algebra, it is not itself a velocity of an SPD metric. We therefore introduce two aggregation mechanisms: a commutator with a symmetric response matrix, producing symmetric Ricci-type metric responses, and an incidence-aware covariant divergence of curvature-induced edge fluxes, reflecting the relation between trace and covariant divergence. The resulting responses are locally orthogonal-gauge equivariant and can drive exponential updates that preserve positive definiteness. We also give a reversible metric-compatible parametrization of edge transports, allowing orthogonal edge factors, loop scales, weights, and response matrices to be learned while respecting the graph geometry. Known-geometry calibrations on the unit sphere test the holonomy--curvature relation, curvature preservation under nontrivial local metric representations, and the empirical recovery of edge transports from local observations.

</details>


### [111] [KPI-Conditioned Generative Design of Automotive Hood Inner Panels: A Two-Stage Retrieval-Generation Pipeline with Surrogate-Based Performance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22457)
*Sudeep Chavare*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段逆向设计流水线，用于在离散拓扑族组织的工业零件（如内引擎盖面板）设计空间中，从给定的性能需求生成几何形状。第一阶段通过可达性分析确定哪些拓扑族能满足需求向量，第二阶段使用条件变分自编码器（CVAE）在选定族内生成点云几何，并由神经算子代理估计性能。流水线完全基于公开数据和免费计算资源构建，并部署为交互工具。核心结果显示流水线有效，但代理误差与所需区分的性能差异相当，这一比例被论证为决定此类流水线能否工作的关键量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前向（几何→性能）的机器学习代理已成熟且常规化，但逆向（需求→几何）对于设计空间按离散拓扑族而非连续参数化组织的工业零件仍未被充分解决。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 两阶段流水线：1) 可达性阶段——判断哪些拓扑族能满足给定的需求向量；2) 生成阶段——CVAE在选定族内生成点云几何候选，神经算子代理评估每个候选的性能。全流程基于公开数据与免费算力，以交互工具形式部署。

Result: 流水线有效运行，但带有限制条件。代理在整体上准确，但其误差与需要区分的类内性能差异相当，这限制了单个生成设计可声明的性能精度。作者将“代理误差与类内信号之比”论证为决定此类流水线可行性的关键指标。

Conclusion: 该逆向设计流水线在工业零件的离散拓扑族设计空间中证明了可行性，但其有效性受限于代理误差相对于类内性能差异的比例。这一比率为后续此类流水线的设计与评估提供了理论判据，限制条件被视为主要发现而非缺陷。

Abstract: An inner hood panel must meet a deflection target, stay below a stress limit, and hit a mass target. Machine-learned surrogates have made the forward direction, geometry to performance, fast and routine. The inverse direction, producing geometry from a stated requirement, remains largely unaddressed for industrial parts whose design space is organized into discrete topology families rather than a continuous parameterization. This work presents a two-stage pipeline for that inverse problem. A reachability stage determines which topology families can satisfy a given requirement vector. A conditional variational autoencoder then generates point-cloud geometry within a selected family, and a neural-operator surrogate estimates the performance of each candidate. The pipeline is built entirely from public data and freely available compute, and is deployed as an interactive tool. The pipeline works, with qualifications that are reported as primary findings rather than caveats. The surrogate is accurate in aggregate, but its error is comparable to the performance differences it is asked to discriminate, which bounds what can be claimed for any individual generated design. That ratio of surrogate error to within-class signal is argued to be the quantity that determines whether a pipeline of this kind can work at all.

</details>


### [112] [MASH-Bench: Diagnosing Cross-Source Failure in Mass-Shooting Risk Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22460)
*Neha Sharma,Ritesh Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MASH-Bench基准，整合了来自四个美国枪击事件数据库的6,968起事件。通过留一数据集（LODO）评估发现，在精选数据源上表现良好的机器学习模型（RF/XGBoost/LightGBM）在泛化到GVA时性能急剧下降（VeryHigh风险召回率降至0.20，精确率降至0.0004）。通过特征掩蔽消融实验证明，特征完整性缺失是跨源泛化失败的主要因素；域适应方法（DANN）能显著提升召回率但精确率仍低，而标签侧校正（先验偏移重校准）无法弥补特征缺陷。研究还发现与媒体归因的精神健康标签相关的显著群体差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开的大规模枪击事件数据库在覆盖范围、特征可用性和报告实践上存在显著差异，这给需要跨数据源泛化的机器学习模型带来了严峻挑战。现有研究缺乏一个统一的基准来系统性地诊断和评估跨源风险分类中的泛化问题，因此需要构建一个基准来探究跨源泛化失败的根本原因。

Method: 1) 构建MASH-Bench基准，整合四个美国数据库（Kaggle、Mother Jones、Stanford MSA、GVA）共6,968起事件；2) 采用留一数据集（LODO）评估框架进行跨源风险分类；3) 对比Random Forest、XGBoost、LightGBM三种基学习器；4) 设计控制特征掩蔽消融实验，移除GVA中不可用的五个特征；5) 评估三种域适应方法（DANN、CORAL、重要性加权）和Oracle先验偏移重校准；6) 进行逐组审计以识别特定群体的偏差。

Result: 基学习器在精选数据源上取得良好性能（VeryHigh风险召回率0.68-0.89），但在GVA上泛化差（召回率0.20，精确率0.0004）。特征掩蔽导致召回率归零，确认特征完整性是主要因素。DANN将GVA的VeryHigh风险召回率提升0.282（95% CI [0.11,0.47], p=0.003），但精确率仍低；CORAL和重要性加权召回率为零；先验偏移重校准也失败。模型识别的结果显示了特征完整性和标签流行率比分类器选择更能制约跨源泛化。

Conclusion: 在MASH-Bench中，跨源泛化受限于特征完整性和标签流行率而非分类器选择。特征缺陷使得仅依靠标签侧的校正方法（如先验偏移重校准）不足以解决问题。MASH-Bench为诊断跨源风险分类中的这些效应提供了一个受控的实验设置，同时研究揭示了与媒体归因的精神健康标签相关的群体差异问题，需要进一步关注和解决。

Abstract: Public mass-shooting databases differ substantially in coverage, feature availability, and reporting practices, creating challenges for machine-learning models that must generalize across data sources. We introduce MASH-Bench, a harmonized benchmark of 6,968 incidents from four U.S. databases: Kaggle, Mother Jones, Stanford MSA, and the Gun Violence Archive (GVA). We evaluate cross-source risk classification using leave-one-dataset-out (LODO) evaluation. Random Forest, XGBoost, and LightGBM achieve VeryHigh-risk recall of 0.68-0.89 on the curated sources but generalize poorly to GVA, where mean recall drops to 0.20 and precision to 0.0004. To investigate the source of this degradation, we conduct a controlled feature-masking ablation that removes the five features unavailable in GVA from the curated sources. The resulting recall collapse to zero provides evidence that feature completeness is a major contributor to the observed cross-source failure. We further evaluate three domain-adaptation approaches: DANN, CORAL, and importance weighting. DANN improves VeryHigh-risk recall on GVA by 0.282 (95% CI [0.11, 0.47], p = 0.003), although precision remains low, whereas CORAL and importance weighting yield zero recall. Oracle prior-shift recalibration likewise fails to recover VeryHigh-risk predictions, indicating that label-side correction alone is insufficient under the observed feature deficiencies. A per-group audit further identifies substantial disparities associated with media-attributed mental-health labels. Overall, these results indicate that, in MASH-Bench, cross-source generalization is constrained more by feature completeness and label prevalence than by classifier choice. The benchmark provides a controlled setting for diagnosing these effects in cross-source risk classification.

</details>


### [113] [Functional compatibility as a determinant of persistent neural learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22462)
*Hossein Javidnia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次通过受控实验证明功能相容性（功能相容性，即新学习与需保留行为共存的程度的因果决定作用）是持久学习的因果决定因素。研究在多种架构（卷积/Transformer）、模态（视觉/文本）和多种种子下验证了这一效应，并揭示了学习规则对相容性的利用效率差异及非线性几何对学习机会的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有重放、正则化和约束更新等方法应对稳定性-可塑性困境，但尚不清楚输入学习本身的性质是否决定哪些内容能在不破坏受保护行为的情况下被保留。作者希望建立功能相容性作为一个可实验控制的学习持久性原理。

Method: 通过从匹配神经状态出发，刻意操纵新学习与需保留行为之间的功能相容性，在统一的保留要求下测量持久学习效果。采用受控因果实验设计，跨不同学习方向、卷积与Transformer架构、视觉与文本任务以及多种随机种子进行验证，并比较不同学习规则对相容性的利用效率。

Result: 功能相容性确实因果性地决定持久学习；该效应在多种架构、模态和种子上泛化；不同学习规则对可用相容性的利用效率不同，而保留约束限制了可存储的信息量；在较大有限更新下，非线性几何改变可用学习机会，最终使匹配相容性连续体无法实现。

Conclusion: 功能相容性是可实验控制的持久神经学习原理。这一发现将稳定性-可塑性问题从"如何防止遗忘"转向"识别哪些新学习成分可以被安全地永久化"。

Abstract: Artificial neural networks can acquire new capabilities but often damage existing ones when they continue to learn. This stability-plasticity problem has motivated replay, regularization and constrained-update methods, yet it remains unclear whether a property of incoming learning itself determines what can be retained without disrupting protected behaviour. Here we show that functional compatibility, the extent to which new learning can coexist with behaviour that must be preserved, is a causal determinant of persistent learning. To our knowledge, this is the first controlled causal demonstration in which compatibility is deliberately changed from matched neural states and persistent learning is measured under a common retention requirement. The effect generalizes across independent learning directions, convolutional and transformer architectures, vision and text, and additional seeds. Learning rules differ in how efficiently they exploit available compatibility, while retention constraints limit how much can be stored. At larger finite updates, nonlinear geometry changes the available learning opportunity and ultimately prevents the matched compatibility continuum from being realized. These results establish functional compatibility as an experimentally controllable principle of persistent neural learning, shifting the problem from preventing forgetting towards identifying which components of new learning can safely become permanent.

</details>


### [114] [The Variance of Thought: Policy Variance, Critical Forks, and Local Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22467)
*Yingru Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过策略方差σ²_π(s)分析长程语言模型任务（多步推理和智能体）中的信用分配问题。在确定性MDP中，该方差是回报方差的唯一来源，在状态（关键分叉）处以离散脉冲形式注入。作者得出三个结论：策略方差是发现预算（观察优势为c的动作需要Ω(c²/σ²)次采样）；策略方差被Gini离散度上界约束，可从logits无rollout地计算；剩余视界决定估计成本，MC优势估计的信噪比为√P，采样成本为1/P，bootstrap通过将生存概率乘积转化为和来消除这一成本，但要求价值表示具有乘法准确性，支持log-value参数化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程语言模型任务受困于信用分配问题，即如何将延迟的回报正确归因到导致该结果的关键决策上。作者希望从理论上理解这一问题的本质，并找到可操作的判定与优化方法。

Method: 作者从策略方差σ²_π(s)这一核心量出发进行分析。在确定性MDP中，该方差是回报方差的唯一来源，在关键分叉状态以离散脉冲形式注入。作者推导了三个结果：(i) 使用优势估计的下界Ω(c²/σ²)刻画采样的发现预算，并在双点分叉上证明紧致性；(ii) 用Gini离散度上界1-‖π(·|s)‖²_2约束策略方差，提供无需rollout的可计算必要性判据；(iii) 分析MC优势估计的信噪比（√P）与采样成本（1/P），论证bootstrap通过将生存概率乘积转为求和来缓解，并指出log-value参数化是乘性准确价值表示的关键。

Result: 三个理论结果：策略方差作为发现预算的精确下界；Gini离散度提供无rollout的关键性判据；MC估计的采样成本与1/P成正比，bootstrap可将其消除但需要乘性准确的价值表示，支持log-value参数化。

Conclusion: 策略方差σ²_π(s)为长程信用分配提供了统一的理论框架。作者给出了可计算的关键分叉判据（无需rollout）、精确的采样发现预算下界，以及bootstrap配合log-value参数化的估计成本优化方案。这些结果为设计更高效的训练算法和信用分配机制奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Long-horizon language-model tasks --- multi-step reasoning and tool-using agents alike --- are limited by credit assignment. We analyze it through the policy variance $σ_π^2(s)=\operatorname{Var}_{a\simπ}[Q_π(s,a)]$, which in a deterministic MDP is the sole source of return variance and is injected in discrete pulses at states we call critical forks. Three results follow. (i) Policy variance is a discovery budget: observing an action of advantage $c$ requires $Ω(c^2/σ_π^2(s))$ draws, a bound that is exact on the canonical two-point fork. (ii) Policy variance is bounded by the policy's Gini dispersion, $σ_π^2(s)\le 1-\|π(\cdot|s)\|_2^2$, a rollout-free necessary condition for criticality computable from logits alone. (iii) The remaining horizon sets the estimation cost: at a fork whose downstream success probability is $P$, the Monte Carlo advantage estimate has signal-to-noise ratio of order $\sqrt{P}$, so its sample cost scales as $1/P$ --- a cost that branched sampling shares. Bootstrapping removes it by converting a product of survival probabilities into a sum, provided the value representation is multiplicatively accurate, which argues for log-value parameterization.

</details>


### [115] [Quantum-Inspired Hybrid Neural Networks for Neural Decoding: A Controlled Ablation Study of Learnable Quantum Sidecar Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22475)
*Diana Legziel Levy,Menachem Finkelstein,Peter Chin,Eilon Vaadia,Sarel Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了将参数化量子电路（PQC）作为残差侧车模块集成到ResNet-50骨干网络中，用于31类神经群体解码（想象手写分类）。在严格控制条件下比较了四种模型变体，发现骨干梯度训练变体在3/4个种子中提升准确率（+0.19%均值），并一致降低与基线特征的线性CKA相似度，表明产生了真实的结构性表征重组。消融实验表明简单的浅层架构最有效且可复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索混合量子-经典架构在神经解码任务中的潜力，特别是参数化量子电路作为残差模块嵌入经典深度网络时，能否带来表征层面的结构性变化而非简单的性能提升，从而为量子-经典混合学习提供新的设计洞见。

Method: 在ResNet-50骨干中集成PQC残差侧车模块，设计四种变体：基线、冻结输入投影的量子侧车、骨干梯度训练的投影侧车、以及测量引导变体。所有实验在固定数据划分、种子和优化器下进行，使用4量子比特无噪声态矢量模拟，并通过九种变体的消融研究及Linear CKA相似度指标评估表征重组。

Result: 骨干梯度变体在3/4种子中提升准确率（均值+0.19%，95% CI [-1.10%, +1.48%]），同时在4/4种子中一致降低Linear CKA相似度（Δ=-0.025），表明表征结构发生真实重组。消融实验显示简单浅层架构最有效且可复现；测量引导训练持续改善表征几何而不降低准确率。

Conclusion: 参数化量子侧车模块虽未带来显著的量子计算优势或大幅准确率提升，但能一致地诱导表征结构重组，且测量引导训练可改善表征几何。研究不声称量子优势，而是在当前近端超导硬件约束下探索混合架构的可行性与表征层面价值。

Abstract: We study parameterized quantum circuits (PQCs) integrated as residual sidecar modules within a ResNet-50 backbone for 31-class neural population decoding---imagined handwriting classification from multi-neuron spike rasters. Under strictly controlled conditions (fixed data splits, seeds, and optimizer), we compare four model variants: baseline, quantum sidecar with frozen input projection, quantum sidecar with backbone-gradient-trained projection, and a measurement-guided variant that aligns angle encodings with circuit measurement outcomes. The backbone-gradient variant improves accuracy in 3/4 seeds (+0.19% mean, 95% CI [-1.10%, +1.48%]) and consistently reduces Linear CKA similarity to baseline features ($Δ=-0.025$, 4/4 seeds), indicating genuine structural reorganization of representations. A nine-variant ablation identifies simple shallow architectures as the most effective and reproducible configuration. Measurement-guided training consistently improves representation geometry without reducing accuracy. All results use noiseless statevector simulation on 4 qubits, a regime chosen to reflect the practical constraints of current near-term superconducting hardware; no quantum computational advantage over classical methods is claimed.

</details>


### [116] [From Symmetry to Invariance: Learning Galois Equivalent Representations in Finite Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22513)
*Zheng Zhang,Na Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究神经网络在有限域乘法中，能否将学习到的代数运算从训练基表示迁移到未训练的等价数学表示。通过伽罗瓦作用组织基表示为轨道，提出用模型预测伽罗瓦作用来构造每个轨道的规范代表，从而支持从未见基上通过精确规范匹配进行乘法迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络能从有限例子中学习代数运算，但尚不清楚这种能力是否能在同一运算的不同数学等价表示之间迁移。理解这种迁移能力对深度学习如何利用数学结构进行泛化至关重要。

Method: 以有限域乘法为研究对象，在基变换下考察问题。利用伽罗瓦作用将基表示划分为轨道，同一轨道的基诱导相同的坐标乘法映射。作者探索了多种获取轨道结构的途径（不变标签、基矩阵、轨道识别、代数分解）。核心方法是用模型预测基表示之间的伽罗瓦作用，通过反复应用学习到的变换构造每个轨道的规范代表，再通过精确规范匹配在未参加训练的基上实现乘法。

Result: 所提出的机制能够将学习到的代數对称性转化为可用于迁移的不变表示，在held-out基上支持乘法运算，实现了从训练基到未见基的乘法转移。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种具体机制，将学习到的代数对称性转化为不变表示并用于迁移，表明通过伽罗瓦作用学习并构造轨道规范代表可以有效实现代数运算在不同等价表示间的迁移。

Abstract: Neural networks can learn algebraic operations from finite examples, but it remains unclear whether this ability transfers across mathematically equivalent representations of the same operation. We study this question through multiplication in finite fields under changes of basis. The Galois action organizes basis representations into orbits, and bases in the same orbit induce the same coordinate multiplication map. This structure allows us to separate learning multiplication from transferring it to basis representations that are not used for training. We examine several ways of providing or recovering the relevant orbit structure, including invariant labels, basis matrices, orbit recognition, and algebraic decomposition. Our main approach trains a model to predict the Galois action between basis representations. Repeated applications of the learned transformation are then used to construct a canonical representative for each orbit, which supports multiplication on held-out bases through exact canonical matching. This provides a concrete mechanism for converting a learned algebraic symmetry into an invariant representation that can be used for transfer.

</details>


### [117] [From Detrimental to Beneficial: Dynamic Influence-based Valuation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22522)
*Adrian Nyakairu,Hongfu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DIVE是一个新颖的高效框架，在批次级别动态估计样本价值，通过策略性地逆转有害样本的梯度方向（而非丢弃或降权），在优化层面将有害数据转化为有益贡献，从而提升分类性能、数据效率和优化稳定性，并可推广到LLM微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据估值方法主要聚焦于将训练样本分类为有益或有害，但对估值结果的下游干预利用不足——常规做法是丢弃或降权有害样本，未能充分利用现有数据资源。

Method: DIVE框架在批次级别动态估计样本价值，不修改原始数据，而是在优化层面通过策略性地逆转有害样本的梯度方向将其转化为有益贡献，与标准学习流程无缝集成且开销极低。

Result: 大量实证评估表明，DIVE持续提升分类性能、最大化数据效率、稳定优化过程，并能有效泛化到大型语言模型的微调任务中。

Conclusion: DIVE证明了通过优化层面的梯度逆转策略，有害样本可以被转化为有价值的训练贡献，从而突破传统丢弃/降权范式的局限性，为数据干预提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Data valuation is a cornerstone of data-centric learning, where prior efforts primarily focus on designing algorithms to classify training samples as either beneficial or detrimental for the learning task. However, leveraging these valuation estimates for subsequent data intervention remains underexplored; conventional approaches typically discard or downweight harmful samples, thereby underutilizing available data resources. In this paper, we present Dynamic Influence-based Valuation and Editing (DIVE), a novel and efficient framework that dynamically estimates sample values at the batch level and transforms detrimental data into beneficial contributions. Rather than altering the raw data, DIVE operates at the optimization level by strategically reversing the gradient directions of harmful samples during training, ensuring seamless integration with standard learning procedures with minimal overhead. Extensive empirical evaluations demonstrate that DIVE consistently improves classification performance, maximizes data efficiency, stabilizes optimization, and effectively generalizes to large language model fine-tuning.

</details>


### [118] [Stress Testing Unlearning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22527)
*Noam Diamant,Ethan Fetaya,Neta Glazer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了WMDP++，一个扩展的机器学习遗忘（unlearning）基准测试框架，针对现有基准的两大缺陷：（1）未能主动测试被遗忘信息是否仍可被强制提取；（2）未评估模型在边界问题上（与被遗忘内容语义相近的正常查询）的性能保持。WMDP++提供了更严格、更具信息量的LLM遗忘评估基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）中的机器遗忘面临输入输出模糊性的挑战，因此需要严格的评估来同时衡量安全性和实用性。现有遗忘基准存在两个关键缺陷：一是没有主动测试被遗忘信息是否仍可被强制提取；二是没有评估模型在边界问题上（与被遗忘内容语义相近的正常/良性查询）的性能保持情况。这两个缺陷导致现有基准无法充分反映遗忘方法的真实效果。

Method: WMDP++作为WMDP（一个已有的有害知识基准）的扩展，通过以下方式解决上述缺陷：（1）引入对被遗忘信息的针对性提取测试，主动探测模型是否仍可通过特定方式恢复已遗忘的信息；（2）系统性地构建和评估边界问题，即与被遗忘内容在语义上接近但属于良性范畴的查询，以检验遗忘过程是否损害了模型在邻近领域的能力保持。

Result: WMDP++提供了一个比现有基准更严格、更具信息量的评估框架，能够更全面地揭示LLM遗忘方法的实际效果，包括其安全性（信息是否真正无法被提取）和实用性（边界问题上的性能是否保持）。

Conclusion: WMDP++弥补了现有LLM遗忘评估基准的关键不足，通过对遗忘信息的强制提取测试和边界问题评估，为LLM遗忘方法的发展提供了更严格、更有指导意义的基准工具。

Abstract: Recently, machine unlearning, the removal of specific training data influence from a model, has gained increasing attention. In large language models (LLMs), unlearning is particularly challenging due to the ambiguity of inputs and outputs. Con- sequently, rigorous evaluation is critical for assessing both safety and utility, and for driving progress in unlearning meth- ods. We identify two key shortcomings in existing unlearning benchmarks: (1) they do not actively test whether unlearned information can still be forcibly extracted, and (2) they fail to evaluate performance preservation on boundary questions, be- nign queries that are semantically close to the unlearned con- tent. Here we introduce WMDP++, an extension of WMDP that addresses these gaps by incorporating targeted extrac- tion of unlearned information and systematic evaluation on boundary questions. WMDP++ provides a more stringent and informative benchmark for evaluating unlearning in LLMs.

</details>


### [119] [BLADE: Bilevel Low-rank Augmented-Lagrangian Erasure for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22557)
*Md Toufikuzzaman,Ahmad Mousavi,Dongwon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BLADE，一个受约束的双层（bilevel）框架，通过三个机制（截断熵遗忘损失、非对称增广拉格朗日乘子、限定于LoRA适配器的双层结构）实现对LLM unlearning的平滑可控调节，在TOFU、MUSE、KnowUndo三个基准系列上超越最强基线，并在4倍规模扩展和4次顺序unlearning下保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法缺乏鲁棒性：无界的遗忘损失会破坏模型连贯性；固定权重平衡无法适应训练过程中保留难度变化；在某一基准上有效的方法在规模扩展或重复应用时失效。

Method: BLADE包含三个关键机制：(1) 截断熵遗忘损失——一旦token达到足够的熵/不确定性，其梯度精确归零，防止过度遗忘；(2) 非对称增广拉格朗日——在保留约束被违反后永久性地加强保留保护；(3) 限定在LoRA适配器上的双层优化结构——在每次遗忘步骤前修复保留损伤。

Result: BLADE在三个基准系列上全面占优：在TOFU上相较最强基线平均综合分数提升6%，MUSE Books提升9%，KnowUndo提升7%；在MUSE News上4倍规模扩展和4次顺序unlearning后仍保持稳定，而此时最强竞争方法完全崩溃。

Conclusion: BLADE通过结构性约束而非启发式调参，实现了对遗忘优化过程平滑、可预测的控制，显著提升了LLM unlearning在规模扩展和多次应用下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Existing LLM unlearning methods struggle with robustness: unbounded forget losses degrade model coherence, fixed-weight balancing cannot adapt as retain difficulty shifts mid-training, and methods that work on one benchmark falter under scaling or repeated application. We propose BLADE, a constrained bilevel framework whose three mechanisms give smooth, predictable control over the optimization landscape: a clamped-entropy forget loss whose gradient is exactly zero once a token reaches sufficient uncertainty; an asymmetric augmented Lagrangian that permanently ratchets retain protection after any violation; and a bilevel structure confined to LoRA adapters that repairs retain damage before each forgetting step. BLADE dominates across three benchmark families, improving average composite scores over the strongest baselines by $6$% on TOFU, $9$% on MUSE Books, and $7$% on KnowUndo, and it remains stable under $4\times$ scaling and $4$ sequential unlearning steps on MUSE News where the best competing method collapses entirely.

</details>


### [120] [Clinical Graph-JEPA: Predictive Patient-State Knowledge Graphs for Cognitive Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22583)
*Kushagra Yadav,Nalin Prabhath,Amit Lamba,Goeun Han,Yining Mao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Clinical records contain rich evidence about patient state, but converting that evidence into reliable, structured knowledge graphs remains difficult because extraction errors, ontology mismatch, missing relations, and temporal ambiguity can propagate into downstream systems. We propose a clinical knowledge graph construction and refinement framework that combines multi-agent relation proposal, ontology-aware normalization, deterministic evidence scoring, and JEPA-based latent refinement. Rather than treating a clinical knowledge graph as a static extraction artifact, we treat it as a predictive patient-state representation. For each admission, the system constructs an evidence-scored graph from structured MIMIC-IV records and inferred clinical cross-links, then learns to recover held-out clinical relations from the observed graph context. We evaluate the refiner with leakage-free leave-one-out edge recovery (MRR and Hits@k) and held-out batch-mask evaluation (AUC and MRR). To isolate the contribution of discharge-note context, we compare a note-embedding-free configuration with a note-augmented configuration that injects real discharge-note representations only into note-grounded entities. Under the same cohort and evaluation protocol, entity-grounded note injection improves overall leave-one-out MRR by 31% relative improvement.

</details>


### [121] [GCA: Global Centroid Alignment in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22593)
*Jong-Ik Park,Harry Jiang,Logan Blakely,Georgios Fragkos,Shamina Hossain-McKenzie,Carlee Joe-Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出全局质心对齐（GCA），一种基于潜在编码中介的联邦学习协议，用于自动编码器异常检测。GCA不传输AE参数或梯度，而是通过上传潜码子集、服务器聚类并广播全局质心与支持计数、客户端按逆计数加权对齐最近质心来协调训练。在五个表格和两个视觉基准上，GCA在21项比较中全部取得更高的重建误差（防御提取攻击），20/21项中余弦相似度更低，通信开销降低最高99.15%，且测试准确率最高提升5.76%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于自动编码器的联邦学习（FL）异常检测需要交换AE参数或梯度，导致大量通信开销，且由于AE显式优化输入重建，存在暴露客户端训练数据的隐私风险。在客户端本地数据有限的情况下，需要一种既能保护数据隐私又能降低通信成本的联邦协调协议。

Method: GCA协议每轮包含三步：(1)客户端用重建损失训练本地AE，仅上传一小部分编码器潜码到服务器；(2)服务器聚合这些编码、拟合聚类模型，只广播全局潜质心及其支持计数；(3)客户端通过逆计数加权对齐本地潜码与最近质心，强调全局欠代表的模式。该过程跨通信轮重复，通信成本仅取决于潜在维度、上传编码数和质心返回数，与AE模型大小无关。

Result: 在五个表格和两个视觉基准上，GCA在所有21项对比中取得比FedAvg、FedProx和FedNova更高的重建误差（服务器端数据提取攻击下），并在20/21项中余弦相似度更低。测试准确率最高提升5.76%，提取防御效果与DP-FedAvg相当，在DP-FedAvg失效时仍有效，每轮通信开销降低最高99.15%。

Conclusion: GCA通过仅交换潜码和质心统计量，在不传输AE参数/梯度的前提下实现了有效的联邦异常检测训练，显著提升通信效率和训练数据隐私保护，为资源受限场景下的联邦自编码器学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Autoencoder (AE)-based federated learning (FL) is attractive for anomaly detection when clients have limited local data. However, conventional FL exchanges AE parameters or gradients, incurring substantial communication overhead and potentially exposing input training data information, since AEs are explicitly optimized to reconstruct their inputs. We introduce \emph{Global Centroid Alignment (GCA)}, a latent-code-mediated FL protocol that coordinates clients without transmitting AE parameters or gradients. In each round, (1) clients first train their local AEs using a \emph{reconstruction} update and upload a small subset of encoder latent codes to the FL server. (2) The server pools these codes, fits a clustering model, and broadcasts only \emph{global latent centroids and their support counts}. (3) Each client then updates its encoder by aligning its local latent codes with the \emph{nearest} centroid using \emph{inverse-count} weighting to emphasize globally underrepresented patterns. Steps (1)--(3) repeat over communication rounds. Because GCA exchanges only sampled latent codes and centroid statistics, its communication cost depends on latent dimensionality and the numbers of uploaded codes and returned centroids rather than on AE model size. Across five tabular and two vision benchmarks, GCA yields higher reconstruction error under a server-side client data extraction attack in all 21 comparisons and clearly lower cosine similarity in 20 of 21 comparisons with FedAvg, FedProx, and FedNova, showing its ability to protect training data. It even improves test accuracy over FedAvg by up to $5.76\%$. GCA achieves extraction defense comparable to DP-FedAvg, remains effective when DP-FedAvg does not reduce target resemblance, and lowers per-round communication by up to $99.15\%$.

</details>


### [122] [Tabular foundation models for non-tabular tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22594)
*Goran Nakerst,John Brennan,Wouter Beugeling,Masudul Haque*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究表格基础模型（TFM）TabPFN v3是否具备超越传统表格任务的能力，将其应用于三个非表格分类问题（MNIST手写数字识别、法语/德语单词语言识别、Tiny ImageNet图像分类），将原始数据表示为表格行、分类任务形式化为缺失标签预测。结果显示，尽管没有显式访问空间或序列结构，TabPFN v3在某些情况下能达到与专门针对这些任务的模型相当的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型作为表格数据机器学习的新范式具有跨数据集泛化能力，但由于许多机器学习数据集可以表示为表格，作者希望探究TFM的能力是否超出传统表格任务范畴。

Method: 将三个非表格分类问题（MNIST、法语/德语单词分类、Tiny ImageNet）的数据表示为表格行，将分类形式化为缺失标签预测，使用TabPFN v3零样本推理（无额外训练或微调），评估性能随上下文样本数量的变化。

Result: TabPFN v3在没有显式访问空间或序列结构的情况下，在某些任务上达到了与专门针对对应任务设计的模型或方法相当的准确率。

Conclusion: TabPFN v3的能力可以扩展到传统表格任务之外，暗示表格基础模型在更广泛的机器学习问题上具有潜在的应用价值。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for machine learning on tabular data, offering the ability to generalize across datasets without task-specific training. Since many machine learning datasets can be represented as tables, this raises the question: does TFM capability extend beyond tasks traditionally regarded as tabular? We address this question by using TabPFN v3 on three non-tabular classification problems: handwritten digit recognition on MNIST, language identification of French and German words, and image classification on Tiny ImageNet. In each case, the original data are represented as rows of a table and classification is formulated as prediction of a missing label. We evaluate performance as a function of the number of context samples provided to the pretrained model, with no additional training or fine-tuning. Despite having no explicit access to the spatial or sequential structure characterizing the data, TabPFN v3 in some cases achieves accuracies comparable with that of models or methods geared specifically toward the corresponding tasks.

</details>


### [123] [Adversarial Agents on Topology Optimization: Understanding the Fragility and Robustness of Deep Learning-based and Physics-Based Design Models under Adversarial Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22606)
*Hoang Anh Nguyen,Yuan Hong,Hongyi Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于力学原理的可靠性评估框架，针对拓扑优化中的深度学习代理模型构建对抗性攻击代理。研究发现，仅在初始密度通道引入有界扰动即可导致灾难性的机械失效（合规性增加多个数量级），且更丰富的物理梯度条件并不能保证跨模型家族的单调鲁棒性。通过物理在环恢复（用扰动拓扑初始化经典SIMP优化器）可以有效缓解性能退化，表明学习代理应作为物理验证的初始化器而非替代物理求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习代理模型因其在线设计生成速度快而被广泛应用于网络物理制造系统中的生成式设计，但它们对输入扰动的脆弱性尚未被充分研究。现有工作缺乏一个力学上有依据的可靠性评估框架来系统性地验证这些代理模型在对抗性攻击下的表现。

Method: 作者提出一个力学接地（mechanics-grounded）的可靠性评估框架，构造一个非侵入式威胁模型的对抗性代理：仅在初始密度通道引入有界扰动，保持物理边界条件、合规性梯度通道、网络架构和求解器例程不变。对U-Net、卷积和生成式架构，以及不同物理梯度条件深度的代理模型进行评估。

Result: 有界的初始化噪声可以导致灾难性的机械失效，通过切断载荷路径和断开支撑使合规性增加多个数量级。更丰富的物理梯度条件并不保证跨代理家族的单调鲁棒性。物理在环恢复（用扰动拓扑初始化SIMP优化器）能够以高概率将合规性恢复到接近基线水平。

Conclusion: 学习代理应作为物理验证的初始化器（physics-verified initializers）而非完全替代基于物理的求解器，以构建有韧性的网络物理制造系统。所提出的对抗性代理为未来训练对噪声和目标扰动具有鲁棒性的生成式设计代理提供了基础。
