Unlike prior methods that model full categorical distributions over all feasible $N$:$M$ patterns, RoI introduces a compact-logit parameterization for sparsity mask learning and performs sampling without replacement to select masks, thereby reducing trainable parameters from combinatorial complexity to $\mathcal{O}({M})$. As a result, RoI requires 1.5-8.75$\times$ fewer learnable parameters and significantly lower memory cost, while remaining fully aligned with hardware-friendly sparsity patterns. Extensive evaluations across multiple scales of the Qwen2.5 LLM family (0.5-7B parameters) demonstrate that RoI achieves competitive performance with strong memory efficiency, stability, and scalability to more aggressive $N$:$M$ sparsity patterns, offering a practical path toward efficient LLM deployment.

</details>


### [158] [Graph Representation Learning of Lightweight IoT Ciphers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23054)
*Jonathan Cook,Sabih ur Rehman,M. Arif Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于机器学习引导的图表示学习框架，通过从部分差分分布表（pDDT）中提取四种差分特征，为SIMON32和SIMECK32两种轻量级密码算法构建有向图，首次实现了差分聚类效应的图可视化。三种模型（KNN、决策树、随机森林）均以1.0的精度识别高概率差分，其中KNN在聚类分离度、F1分数和构建时间（约2.3秒）上表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管SIMON和SIMECK等Feistel结构轻量级密码算法容易受到差分密码分析攻击，需要严格的抗攻击性评估，但现有的启发式与采样技术效率有限，且几乎没有研究将机器学习引导的图表示学习应用于高效识别和可视化高概率差分簇。

Method: 提出了一种高效的特征工程策略，从部分差分分布表（pDDT）中提取四种差分属性以揭示原始差分数据中隐含的结构信息；利用这些增强特征，分别使用K近邻（KNN）、决策树（DT）和随机森林（RF）为SIMON32和SIMECK32构建并比较三种机器学习引导的有向图。

Result: 该框架首次实现了差分聚类效应的图可视化，高概率单比特差分在学习的嵌入空间中形成几何上紧邻的簇。三种模型在识别高概率差分时均达到1.0的精度（零假阳性）；KNN在聚类分离度、F1分数和图构建时间（约2.3秒）上表现最佳，DT和RF实现了接近完美的回归最优路径。

Conclusion: 实验结果在两种轻量级密码算法上一致，证明了该框架对其他AND-rotation类轻量级密码算法家族的普适适用性。

Abstract: SIMON and SIMECK belong to a family of Lightweight Cryptographic Algorithms (LCAs) based on the Feistel block cipher, designed for Internet of Things (IoT) devices. As with all Feistel ciphers, they are susceptible to differential cryptanalysis, necessitating rigorous resilience evaluations. While state-of-the-art techniques leverage heuristics and sampling to improve efficiency, little work has applied Machine Learning (ML) guided Graph Representation Learning (GRL) to efficiently identify and visualise high-probability differential clusters. We address this gap by introducing an efficient feature engineering strategy that extracts four differential attributes from a partial Difference Distribution Table (pDDT), revealing structural information concealed in raw differential data. Utilising the enriched features, we construct and compare three ML-guided directed graphs for SIMON$32$ and SIMECK$32$ using K-Nearest Neighbour (KNN), Decision Trees (DT), and Random Forests (RF). To the best of our knowledge, our framework produces the first graph-based visualisation of the differential clustering effect, in which high-probability single-bit differentials form geometrically close clusters in the learned embedding. All three models achieve a precision of $1.0$ in identifying high-probability differentials, confirming zero false positives. KNN achieves the strongest cluster separation, the highest F1 score and the lowest graph construction time of approximately $2.3$ seconds, while DT and RF produce optimal paths with near-perfect regression. The results are consistent across both LCAs, demonstrating the applicability of the framework to other AND-rotation LCA families.

</details>


### [159] [Macro-Action Topological Navigation under Noisy Localization using Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23055)
*Simon Hakenes,Tobias Glasmachers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示了在照片级逼真的3D公寓环境中，基于纯像素输入的强化学习智能体仅通过摄像头估计自身姿态，在无需完整SLAM的情况下完成目标物体导航任务。方法用ORB特征的目标位姿估计配合简化扩展卡尔曼滤波器，构建局部一致的位姿，使其足以前往各目标物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为，在照片级真实的3D环境中，仅从原始像素进行强化学习导航是几乎不可行的。先前工作依赖模拟器提供的真实位姿和物体检测，而本文希望去除这一先验，实现仅从视觉估计位姿的自主导航，更贴近真实机器人和生物导航的约束条件。

Method: 在先前基于物体中心拓扑控制器的基础上，用自带物体中心位姿估计替代真实位姿。对每个物体维护一个ORB特征库，物体再次出现时通过特征匹配获得粗略位姿测量，再由一个小型扩展卡尔曼滤波器（EKF）结合运动模型进行融合。执行动作带有噪声，位姿会漂移，但智能体与附近物体一同漂移，因此局部一致的位姿足以沿每个短边移动并视觉锁定目标，从而用更小的模型替代全量SLAM。

Result: 在照片级逼真的Habitat模拟器中，智能体仅凭视觉即可依次到达各目标物体，且所需的位姿只需局部一致即可。

Conclusion: 局部一致的物体中心位姿估计足以取代完整SLAM实现基于像素的导航，这与生物导航的工作方式更接近，为在真实场景中部署视觉导航智能体提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Navigating large, photorealistic 3D apartments from raw pixels is widely considered infeasible for plain reinforcement learning. We build an agent that does it anyway, estimating its own pose from the camera alone. The agent has to reach several target objects in sequence, and their positions change between episodes, so it must explore to find them. It builds on our earlier object-centric topological controller, which still read the agent's true pose and its object detections from the simulator. Here we replace that true pose with an onboard, object-centric estimate. For each object we keep a bank of ORB features that, when the object is seen again, yield a rough pose measurement, which a minimal Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) fuses with a motion model. As on a real robot, the executed motions are noisy. The estimate drifts, but the agent and the nearby objects drift together, so a locally consistent pose is enough to follow each short edge and then home in visually on the target, which lets us replace full SLAM with a much smaller model, closer to how biological navigation appears to work. In the photorealistic Habitat simulator, the agent reaches its target objects from vision alone, with a pose that only needs to be locally consistent.

</details>


### [160] [PolyChirp: Multi-Species Birdsong Classification Using TinyML on Low-Power Acoustic Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23101)
*Nathan Duboisset,Zhaolan Huang,Felix Bießmann,Roudy Dagher,Antoine Lavandier,Emmanuel Baccelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PolyChirp是一种结合生物学领域知识、自动化数据集构建、神经架构优化和新型硬件(NPU加速)的方法，实现了在低功耗微控制器上进行多达10种鸟类的多类别野外实时监测，同时满足整个繁殖季节单次电池供电的资源约束，并在单物种二分类上超越了现有技术水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TinyML在低功耗微控制器上的鸟类声学监测仅限于单物种二分类，而真实野外动物监测通常需要同时监测多种物种，这构成了现有技术无法满足的实际部署需求。

Method: PolyChirp方法包含四个关键部分：生物领域专业知识指导、自动化数据集构建、神经架构优化，以及利用配备神经处理单元(NPU)的新一代微控制器和硬件加速技术，设计新型的微型多类别模型。

Result: PolyChirp在单物种二分类任务上超越了现有最优方法，同时实现了多达10种物种的稳健多类别分类，并在内存占用、延迟和能耗等计算性能指标上均满足整个季节单次电池供电的野外部署资源约束。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过软硬件协同设计，低功耗微控制器平台有能力从单物种监测扩展到多物种同步实时监测，为更大规模、更长时段的野外生物多样性声学监测提供了可行性验证。

Abstract: Recent progress in the field of TinyML has demonstrated that low-power hardware based on microcontrollers can achieve bird species monitoring in real time based on acoustic sensor data for an entire breeding period on a single battery charge. However, the state of the art on low-power microcontrollers was so far limited to binary classification of a single species. In contrast, real fauna monitoring deployments often target multiple species simultaneously. To address this challenge we develop PolyChirp, an approach combining biological domain expertise, automated dataset curation, neural architecture optimization and novel hardware to achieve multiclass bird species detection in the wild. PolyChirp is based on newly designed tiny multiclass models that leverage recent microcontrollers and hardware acceleration with a neural processing unit (NPU). We evaluate the predictive performance of these models, and we measure their computational performance -- memory footprint, latency, energy consumption -- on common microcontroller hardware. Our results demonstrate that PolyChirp not only outperforms state-of-the-art on single species binary classification, but also achieves robust classification of up to 10 species simultaneously, while still fitting with the resource envelope of a sensor that must remain operational in the field for a full season on a single battery charge.

</details>


### [161] [DeMixPert: Decomposed Response Modeling with Gaussian Mixtures for OOD Single-Cell Perturbation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23114)
*Jiawen Liu,Xuechenxiao Cao,Yutong Li,Bing Liu,Jiaming Liang,Tinghe Zhang,Xiaoqi Sheng,Hongmin Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeMixPert是一种用于未见遗传扰动下单细胞转录组响应预测的方法，将扰动诱导的基因表达变化分解为基础状态依赖的系统性响应、扰动特异性响应和群体水平变异三个组成部分，并通过高斯原型可逆网络对群体水平变异进行分布建模，实现异质性细胞响应的捕捉和未见过扰动靶点的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在预测未见遗传扰动的转录组响应时存在两大挑战：一是难以同时恢复扰动特异性的转录变化和异质性细胞响应；二是现有方法往往将确定性响应结构与随机群体水平变异纠缠在一起，导致占主导的共享模式掩盖较弱的扰动特异性信号，并损害分布建模能力。

Method: DeMixPert将扰动诱导的变化分解为三部分：1) 基础状态依赖的系统性响应（由对照细胞表达编码的基础状态推导）；2) 扰动特异性响应（从预训练靶点嵌入中推断，实现未见过靶点泛化）；3) 群体水平变异（通过高斯原型可逆网络建模）。该方法根据基础状态和扰动条件自适应组合可复用的高斯原型，生成条件特异性的变异分布，并将采样变异与系统性和扰动特异性分量集成，联合基础状态进行解码以重建扰动细胞的基因表达。

Result: 实验结果表明，DeMixPert能够有效捕捉异质性单细胞扰动响应，并在未见过扰动设置下取得优于现有方法的性能。

Conclusion: 通过显式分解确定性响应与随机群体变异，DeMixPert缓解了共享模式对扰动特异性信号的掩盖问题，提升了分布建模能力和未见过扰动的泛化性能，为单细胞扰动预测提供了一种有效的分解建模框架。源代码将在论文发表后公开。

Abstract: Predicting transcriptome-wide responses to unseen genetic perturbations remains a major computational challenge because accurate prediction requires recovering both perturbation-specific transcriptional shifts and heterogeneous cellular responses. Existing methods often entangle deterministic response structure with stochastic population-level variation, causing dominant shared patterns to mask weaker perturbation-specific signals and impair distributional modeling. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{DeMixPert}, an approach for Decomposed response Modeling with Gaussian Mixtures for Out-Of-Distribution (OOD) single-cell Perturbation prediction. DeMixPert decomposes perturbation-induced changes into a basal-state-dependent systematic response, a perturbation-specific response, and population-level variation. The systematic component is derived from the basal state encoded from control-cell expression, whereas the perturbation-specific component is inferred from pretrained target embeddings for unseen-target generalization. DeMixPert models population-level variation using a Gaussian prototype Invertible Network and adaptively combines reusable Gaussian prototypes according to the basal state and perturbation condition. The resulting mixture is mapped to a condition-specific variation distribution. Sampled variations are integrated with the systematic and perturbation-specific components, followed by joint decoding with the basal state to reconstruct perturbed-cell gene expression. Experimental results show that DeMixPert effectively captures heterogeneous single-cell perturbation responses and achieves superior performance across unseen-perturbation settings. The source code is made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [162] [Activation-Weighted Seeded Residual Coding for Low-Bit LLM Weight Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23144)
*Zehao Liu,Chuangchuang Fang,Yang Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AWSRC是一种紧凑的残差修复码编解码器，用于修复低比特权重量化造成的模型质量损失。它通过确定性种子生成基向量编码权重残差，仅存储种子选择器、低比特系数和缩放因子（而非显式码本），并以激活统计量优先修复影响层输出的误差。在Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct上，仅用0.162 scope-bits/weight的额外开销即可缩小INT4 RTN与BF16之间88.2%的PPL差距、78.9%的KL差距和71.3%的准确率差距，且优于稀疏、低秩和向量量化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特权重量化虽然节省存储，但引入的量化误差会显著降低大语言模型的质量。现有量化骨干虽高效但精度受损，需要一种紧凑的补充机制来修复残差误差，同时避免高额的边带存储开销。

Method: AWSRC在已有量化骨干的基础上，利用确定性种子生成的基向量对权重残差W-W0进行编码。边带存储仅包含种子选择器、低比特系数和缩放因子，取代了显式码本。同时利用激活统计信息对误差进行加权，优先修复对层输出影响最大的误差分量。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct上，AWSRC以每权重0.162 scope-bits的极低附加开销，对INT4 RTN骨干分别缩小了88.2%的PPL差距、78.9%的KL差距和71.3%的准确率差距至BF16。使用49.25MB的匹配边带（约为BF16模型权重载荷的0.8%）时，AWSRC在困惑度和平均任务准确率上均优于稀疏、低秩和向量量化码编解码器。

Conclusion: AWSRC展示了以极小边带开销高效修复量化残差的可能性，其激活加权策略和种子生成基设计使紧凑修复码编解码器在多类压缩方法中达到最优质量，为低比特量化模型提供了一条实用的精度恢复路径。

Abstract: Low-bit weight quantization saves storage but leaves errors that degrade language-model quality. We introduce Activation-Weighted Seeded Residual Coding (AWSRC), a compact repair codec for an existing quantization backbone. Given a reconstructed weight $W_0$, AWSRC encodes the residual $W-W_0$ using deterministic seed-generated bases. The sidecar stores seed selectors, low-bit coefficients, and scales rather than an explicit codebook. Activation statistics prioritize errors that affect layer outputs. On Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, adding 0.162 scope-bits/weight to an INT4 RTN backbone closes 88.2%, 78.9%, and 71.3% of the matched PPL, KL, and accuracy gaps to BF16. Repairing a matched strong low-bit backbone also improves all measured quality metrics. With a matched 49.25 MB sidecar, about 0.8% of the BF16 model-weight payload, AWSRC gives the best perplexity and mean task accuracy among sparse, low-rank, and vector-quantized codecs.

</details>


### [163] [Conformal Risk Minimization for Semi-Supervised Domain Adaptation via Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23153)
*Manos Giannopoulos,Yi Shen,Michael M. Zavlanos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端框架，将Conformal Risk Minimization (CRM)集成到Semi-Supervised Domain Adaptation (SSDA)训练目标中，利用Optimal Transport生成伪标签为CRM提供额外训练信号，从而在目标域标签稀缺的条件下联合优化域不变性和同形效率，生成紧凑、覆盖有效且支持领域特定约束的预测集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗等高危应用中，SSDA模型在分布偏移下缺乏原则性的不确定性量化能力。虽然可以事后应用Conformal Prediction提供覆盖保证，但会产生过大的预测集。CRM虽能解决此问题，却需要大量标注数据计算非一致性阈值，而这在SSDA场景中恰好稀缺。

Method: 将CRM目标融入SSDA训练目标，利用Optimal Transport (OT)为无标签目标域数据生成伪标签，为CRM提供训练所需的额外非一致性阈值计算信号。模型在训练中同时优化域不变性和同形效率，生成紧凑且覆盖有效的预测集。

Result: 生成的预测集紧凑、覆盖有效，并支持领域特定约束（如皮肤病变分类中排除互斥诊断），在SSDA有限标签设定下实现了有效的CRM训练。

Conclusion: 通过将CRM与SSDA和OT伪标签策略相结合，解决了SSDA场景下不确定性量化的核心挑战，为高风险的临床部署提供了既有精度也有可靠预测集保障的模型。

Abstract: In high-stakes healthcare applications, machine learning models are frequently trained on data from one patient population and deployed on another, creating a distribution shift that degrades both accuracy and reliability. Semi-Supervised Domain Adaptation (SSDA) addresses this by leveraging labeled data from some source domain to improve model performance on a target domain where labels are scarce. However, existing SSDA methods optimize primarily for point-prediction accuracy and offer no principled uncertainty quantification --- a prerequisite for clinical trust. Conformal Prediction (CP) can address this limitation by providing prediction sets with rigorous, distribution-free coverage guarantees. However, applying CP post-hoc to a pre-trained model can yield prohibitively large prediction sets, as SSDA pre-training methods do not account for the nonconformity score geometry that determines conformal set size. Conformal Risk Minimization (CRM) has been used to resolve this issue in the fully supervised setting by integrating the CP objective directly into model training, but it requires a large labeled dataset to compute nonconformity thresholds during training, precisely the data that is scarce in the SSDA regime. We propose an end-to-end framework that integrates CRM into the SSDA training objective, enabling effective CRM in the limited-labeled-target-data regime. The key idea is to utilize Optimal Transport (OT) to generate pseudolabels for unlabeled target instances, providing the additional training signal needed by CRM to operate using only a small labeled target set. This results in a model jointly optimized for domain invariance and conformal efficiency, producing prediction sets that are compact, coverage-valid, and support domain-specific constraints such as excluding mutually contradictory diagnoses in skin lesion classification.

</details>


### [164] [When More Modalities Hurt: Modality Dropout for Heavy-Duty Vehicle Engine Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23161)
*Adeel Zafar,Slawomir Nowaczyk,Hamid Sarmadi,Saeed Gholami Shahbandi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了重型车辆诊断中三种异构数据模态（非结构化多语言维修工单、高缺失率传感器遥测数据、诊断故障码DTC）的融合问题。在885个样本、5类发动机组件分类任务上，发现朴素融合仅略优于纯文本（65.3%），而训练期间随机丢弃整个模态的模态dropout方法在文本+DTC融合上达到68.8%准确率（加权F1: 0.67），比纯文本提升3.5个百分点，为所有方法中最佳。逐类分析显示不同故障类型的主导模态不同：进气/排气故障中传感器单独达93%，燃油系统故障中模态dropout融合将准确率从15%提升至38%。这是工业车辆诊断中首次实现文本、传感器与故障码的三模态融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重型车辆诊断中产生了三种互不关联的数据模态：非结构化的多语言维修工单、缺失率超过80%的高维传感器遥测数据、以及诊断故障码（DTCs）。作者关注的问题是能否通过融合这些模态来提升发动机组件分类的准确性，此前尚未有研究在工业车辆诊断中探索这三类数据的融合。

Method: 在来自某大型卡车制造商的专有数据集上，采用5折交叉验证，对比了多个模型家族的多种配置，包括逻辑回归、梯度提升树以及三个深度模型家族。测试了朴素融合与模态dropout（训练时按批次随机禁用整个模态）两种融合策略，共覆盖5类发动机组件、885个全部跨库匹配样本。

Result: 朴素融合仅带来小幅提升。模态dropout在文本+DTC融合上达到68.8%准确率（加权F1: 0.67），较纯文本（65.3%，加权F1: 0.64）提升3.5个百分点，为所有方法最佳。逐类分析表明：进气/排气故障上传感器单独达93%（文本仅80%）；燃油系统故障上模态dropout融合将准确率从15%提升至38%，接近三倍提升。

Conclusion: 模态dropout能够有效迫使网络利用较弱输入信号，从而在文本+DTC融合中取得最佳效果。不同故障类型由不同模态主导，表明多模态融合在工业车辆诊断中具有实际价值。这是首个将文本、传感器和故障码三模态融合应用于工业车辆诊断的研究。

Abstract: Heavy-duty vehicle diagnostics generate three disconnected data modalities: unstructured multi- lingual service complaints, high-dimensional sensor telemetry with over 80% missing values, and Diagnostic Trouble Codes (DTCs). We investigate whether fusing these modalities improves engine component classification on a proprietary dataset from a major truck manufacturer. Through 5-fold cross-validation across multiple model configurations spanning three model families on five engine component classes (885 samples, the full cross-database matched population for this manufacturer), we find that naive fusion provides modest gains over text alone (65.3%). However, modality dropout during training, which randomly disables entire modalities per batch, forces the network to exploit weaker inputs and achieves 68.8% accuracy on text+DTC fusion (weighted F1: 0.67), a 3.5-point improvement over text-only (65.3%, weighted F1: 0.64) and the best result across all methods including logistic regression and gradient-boosted trees. Per-class analysis shows that the dominant modality varies by fault type: text describes symptoms, DTCs encode structured fault signals, and sensors measure physical state. On intake/exhaust faults, sensors alone reach 93% where text achieves 80%. On fuel system faults, fusion with modality dropout nearly triples accuracy from 15% to 38% over text alone. To our knowledge, this is the first application of three-way modality fusion combining text, sensors, and fault codes in industrial vehicle diagnostics.

</details>


### [165] [Counterfactual Transition Graphs: Evaluating Cross-Class Transition Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23164)
*Syed Muhammad Hamza Zaidi,Szymon Bobek,Grzegorz J. Nalepa,Myra Spiliopoulou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于时间序列分类器可解释性的反事实转换图（CGT）框架。与逐样例评估反事实解释不同，CGT将每个类别作为节点、边权重表示从一个原型到另一个原型的反事实可靠性，从而揭示分类器内部类别间的结构性连接关系。在六类手部运动任务中，CGT展示了非平凡的拓扑结构，发现反事实可达性与分类器准确率不对齐甚至相反（Spearman ρ=-0.37），即分类器最能自信区分的边界恰恰是最难被在分布内的编辑跨越的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的反事实解释评估方法一次只评估一个样例，回答的是"哪次最小编辑能翻转这个时间窗口的预测"。但作者认为对诊断性可解释性更有信息量的问题是结构性的：分类器如何将自身的类别相互连接起来？换言之，需要从整体拓扑层面理解分类器内部的类别间关系，而非局限于单个样本。

Method: 提出反事实转换图（CGT）框架：每个节点代表一个类别，每条边的权重表示在邻近感知检索扫描下从一个原型到另一个原型的反事实可靠性。该框架是方法无关的（method-agnostic），任何反事实解释器（如基于替换的方法和基于梯度的方法）都可被接入使用。作者在六类手部运动任务上诱导出CGT，并采用Spearman相关系数评估反事实可达性与分类器准确率之间的一致性。

Result: 在六类手部运动任务中，CGT揭示了非平凡拓扑结构，这种结构无法由二分类混淆矩阵预测。关键发现是反事实可达性与分类器准确率不对齐甚至相反（Spearman ρ=-0.37，覆盖15个类别对），说明分类器分离最自信的边界恰是分布内编辑最难以跨越的。此外，基于替换的反事实方法能停留在数据流形上但无法跨越刚性边界，而基于梯度的反事实方法通过偏离数据流形可以到达几乎任何类别。

Conclusion: CGT提供了一种新的结构化视角来评估时间序列分类器的可解释性，揭示了逐样例解释无法发现的全局拓扑特征。该框架强调分类器置信度与反事实可达性之间的潜在矛盾，并揭示了不同反事实解释方法在数据流形上的行为差异，为诊断性可解释性分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: Counterfactual (CF) explanations for time-series classifiers are usually evaluated one example at a time: what minimal edit flips this single window's prediction? We argue that the more informative question for diagnostic interpretability is structural: how does the classifier connect its own classes to each other?
  We propose a counterfactual transition graph (CGT) in which each node is a class and each edge weight is the CF reliability of the transition from one prototype to another under a proximity aware retrieval sweep. On a six-class hand-movement task, we induce a CGT that reveals a non-trivial topology, which is not predicted by the binary confusion matrix: it shows that counterfactual reachability does not align with classifier accuracy and even runs counter to it (Spearman $ρ=-0.37$ over the 15 pairs), i.e. the boundaries the classifier separates most confidently are among those an in-distribution edit can least often cross.
  Our framework is method agnostic, i.e. any CF-explainers can be used. Presently, we use it to juxtapose replacement-based CFs with gradient-based CFs; gradient-based methods reach almost any class by stepping off the data manifold, while replacement-based methods stay on it and fail on precisely the rigid boundaries.

</details>


### [166] [A Comparative Study of Label-free Representation Quality Metrics in Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23182)
*Daniel Richards Arputharaj,Daniel Jönsson,Gabriel Eilertsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对深度神经网络表示质量的免标签评估指标进行了系统比较研究，将现有指标分为三类并建立同族指标间联系，通过合成实验刻画谱指标敏感性，并在260个视觉模型（六大数据集）上与下游任务精度对比评估。结果表明内在维度（ID）是最可靠的预测指标，但所有指标的可靠性均受架构类别和训练目标影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络中表示质量评估对模型理解与选择至关重要，但现有免标签指标（不依赖标签）的可靠性缺乏系统理解。研究者希望全面了解这些指标在不同配置（架构、目标、数据集等）下的适用性，以便为实践提供指导。

Method: 1）将现有免标签指标按构造方式分为三个家族，并在同一族内建立指标间的分析性联系；2）通过受控合成实验刻画谱类指标的敏感性特征；3）在260个视觉模型、六个数据集（涵盖通用分类、细粒度分类、场景识别和地理空间任务）上，将所有免标签指标与下游任务精度进行系统性对比评估，并按架构类别和训练目标进行分层分析。

Result: 内在维度（ID）是所考察指标中最可靠的表示质量预测因子；但包括ID在内的所有指标的可靠性都受架构类别和训练目标影响而发生变化。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解免标签表示质量指标的测量对象、可靠条件及实际解释方式提供了更清晰的认识，提示使用者在实践中需结合具体架构和训练设定来解读这些指标。

Abstract: We present a comparative study of label-free metrics for assessing the quality of representations in deep neural networks to understand their reliability under a wide variety of configurations. We group existing label-free metrics into three families based on their construction and analytically establish connections between metrics within the same family. We then characterise the sensitivity of spectral metrics through controlled synthetic experiments. Finally, all label-free metrics are evaluated against downstream task accuracy across a diverse set of 260 vision models on six datasets spanning generic object classification, fine-grained object classification, scene recognition and geospatial task, stratifying results by architecture class and training objective. We find that intrinsic dimensionality (ID) is the most reliable predictor among the metrics considered. However, the reliability of all metrics, including ID, is moderated by architecture class and training objective. Our results provide a clearer understanding of what label-free representation quality metrics measure, when they are reliable, and how to interpret them in practice.

</details>


### [167] [Leveraging Remote Traffic Data for Local Air Pollutant Estimation: A Scenario-Based Machine Learning Study Across London Monitoring Sites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23219)
*Valeria Legaria-Santiago,Amadeo Arguelles,Magdalena Saldana-Perez,Jocelyn Richardson,Marcella Bona*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了四种可解释的树基机器学习模型（随机森林、Extra Trees、LightGBM、XGBoost）在六种预测变量场景下估算伦敦多个站点NO₂、PM₁₀、PM₂.₅和O₃浓度的表现，探究远程获取的交通信息对空气质量建模的贡献。结果表明，在没有邻近站点数据时，交通信息的加入使NO₂的RMSE从9.73–11.66 μg/m³降至8.72–11.52 μg/m³，且SHAP分析显示在交通主导环境中交通相关变量对NO₂的贡献可与邻近监测站测量值相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通是空气污染的主要来源，但远程获取的交通信息在本地机器学习空气质量模型中的贡献尚未得到充分表征。本研究旨在量化交通信息对多污染物估算模型的增益，并探究其与邻近监测站数据的相对重要性。

Method: 采用四种可解释的树基ML模型（随机森林、Extra Trees、LightGBM、XGBoost），设计六种预测变量场景：从仅含远程交通、气象和时间变量，逐步扩展到加入1个和4个邻近监测站的测量数据。以岭线性回归为基线，并与空间插值方法及跨站点验证实验进行比较，同时使用SHAP进行变量贡献分析。

Result: 当不使用邻近站点数据建模时，加入交通信息使NO₂的RMSE从9.73–11.66 μg/m³降至8.72–11.52 μg/m³。SHAP分析表明，在交通主导的环境中，交通相关变量对NO₂的贡献可与邻近监测站的污染物测量值相当。

Conclusion: 远程获取的交通信息能够有效提升本地ML空气质量模型的估算精度，且在交通主导环境中其对NO₂预测的重要性可与邻近站点测量数据相媲美，表明交通信息可作为补充邻近站点数据的有价值预测变量。

Abstract: Vehicular traffic is a major source of air pollution; however, the contribution of remotely acquired traffic information to local machine-learning (ML) air-pollution models remains insufficiently characterised. This study evaluates four interpretable tree-based ML models (Random Forest, Extra Trees, LightGBM, and XGBoost) under six predictor scenarios combining progressively larger predictor sets, ranging from remotely acquired traffic, meteorological, and temporal variables alone to the inclusion of measurements from one and four neighbouring monitoring stations, to estimate NO$_2$, PM$_{10}$, PM$_{2.5}$, and O$_3$ concentrations across several sites in London. ML model performance was compared with a ridge linear regression model as a baseline, with spatial interpolation methods and with a cross-site validation experiment. When modelling without data from neighbouring stations, the RMSE for NO$_2$ ranged from 9.73 to 11.66 $μ$g/m$^3$ without traffic information, compared with 8.72 to 11.52 $μ$g/m$^3$ when traffic information was included. Additionally, for NO$_2$, SHAP analyses indicate that traffic-related variables can contribute at levels comparable to pollutant measurements from neighbouring monitoring stations in traffic-dominated~environments.

</details>


### [168] [The Laws of Context Allocation: Causal Measurement and Closed-Loop Orchestration in Generative Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23252)
*Peiyang Liu,Xi Wang,Di Liang,Wei Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对检索增强生成（RAG）中的两个关键瓶颈——证据利用的测量缺陷和上下文预算分配不合理——分别提出解决方案：用因果留一法探针取代传统相关性代理以准确度量LLM对证据的真实依赖；基于该探针的去混杂因子网格证明单调扩宽上下文是受相关性衰减惩罚的架构陷阱，而迭代式多轮顺序生成可带来16.7-20.5个绝对百分点的组合召回提升（最高扩展到32B模型）。最后将两者统一为可部署的闭环子模调度器，并辅以归因引导的对比解码器覆盖LLM注意力惯性，系统性强制新鲜证据整合，在经典开环基线上全面占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG正从单一检索走向多样化组合生成，但存在两个关键瓶颈：一是对证据利用的测量存在缺陷（标准相关性代理在硬负样本上严重失效，即"诊断幻觉"），二是上下文预算分配次优（当前主流策略是盲目加宽上下文窗口）。这两者共同制约了RAG在多样化组合场景下的性能上限。

Method: 首先提出一种高效的因果留一法探针（causal leave-one-out probe），通过逐一移除证据来精确隔离LLM的生成性依赖，并从形式上校准LLM注意力的结构性稀释。随后将该探针部署在去混杂的因子网格实验中，证明上下文加宽是受相关性衰减惩罚的架构陷阱；取而代之的是在多个顺序生成轮次间迭代分配计算资源。最终将这些发现统一为闭环子模调度架构，并增加归因引导的对比解码器（attribution-steered contrastive decoder）来克服LLM的注意力惯性，强制纳入新鲜证据。

Result: 迭代式多轮顺序生成相比传统方案在组合召回率上带来16.7-20.5个绝对百分点的提升，且该增益在最高32B参数的模型上稳健扩展。统一后的闭环子模调度架构在经典开环基线上全面占优，验证了顺序、反馈驱动的编排范式在生成式搜索中的有效性。

Conclusion: 作者证明顺序式、反馈驱动的编排（sequential, feedback-driven orchestration）是生成式搜索的确定性范式，推翻了"上下文越宽越好"的直觉认识。闭环架构加上因果测量工具为RAG系统提供了更准确的可归因性和更高效的计算分配方案，相关代码、数据和测量仪器均已开源。

Abstract: As Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) shifts toward diverse portfolio generation, it is stymied by two critical bottlenecks: flawed measurement of evidence utilization, and suboptimal context budget allocation. We resolve both sequentially.
  To resolve measurement, we expose a pervasive ``diagnostic illusion'': standard relevance proxies fail catastrophically on hard negatives. We replace them with an efficient causal leave-one-out probe that accurately isolates generative reliance and formally calibrates the structural dilution of LLM attention.
  To resolve allocation, we deploy this causal probe in a deconfounded factorial grid. We prove that the prevailing strategy of monolithic context widening is an architectural trap penalized by relevance decay. Instead, allocating compute iteratively across multiple sequential generations drives transformative portfolio recall gains of 16.7--20.5 absolute percentage points, scaling robustly up to 32B models.
  Finally, we unify these solutions into a deployable closed-loop submodular scheduler. Augmented by an attribution-steered contrastive decoder to override LLM attention inertia, our architecture systematically forces fresh evidence integration. By dominating classical open-loop baselines, we establish sequential, feedback-driven orchestration as the definitive paradigm for generative search. Our code, data, and causal measurement instruments are available at https://github.com/PeiYangLiu/ascp.

</details>


### [169] [From Multimodal Observation to Interpretable Suggestions: Counterfactual Time-Expanded Relational Modeling of Surgical Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23254)
*Vincenzo Marco De Luca,Antonio Longa,Giovanna Varni,Andrea Passerini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个基于时间展开图的节奏-关系框架，用于从多模态观测中建模外科团队动态，能够同时捕捉关系结构和时间演化，并在低数据量场景下保持鲁棒性。通过反事实程序生成可解释、可操作的建议，帮助临床医生改进团队协作技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有外科AI解决方案主要集中于视觉工作流和技术执行，忽略了团队交互的建模，未能主动支持临床医生提升团队协作技能。患者安全不仅受技术问题威胁，还受糟糕的团队合作影响，因此需要建模团队动态并生成可操作反馈。

Method: 提出一个节奏-关系（tempo-relational）框架，利用时间展开图（Time-Expanded graphs）建模外科团队动态，同时捕捉关系结构（relational structure）和时间演化（temporal evolution）。采用反事实（counterfactual）程序，通过识别个体行为和交互模式中最小的结构化变化来生成高效、可解释且可操作的建议。

Result: 在模拟外科手术的实验中，该框架在多种行为和交互目标上的预测性能均有提升，同时为团队动态提供了有意义的洞察。

Conclusion: 这项工作将外科AI从以结果为导向的预测范式推进到以社会为基础、以团队为中心、可操作的范式，更好地理解和支持外科环境中团队技能的培养。

Abstract: In surgery, patient safety is threatened not only by technical issues but also by poor teamwork. However, existing surgical AI-based solutions focus mainly on visual workflow and technical execution, neglecting the modeling of team interactions and missing opportunities to actively support clinicians in improving their teamwork skills. To address this gap, we propose a tempo-relational framework for modeling surgical team dynamics from multimodal observations. By leveraging Time-Expanded graphs, the approach captures both relational structure and temporal evolution, achieving strong expressivity while remaining robust in the low-data regime typical of surgical settings. Beyond prediction, such modeling enables the generation of efficient, interpretable, and actionable suggestions for clinicians. More specifically, we generate suggestions via a counterfactual procedure that identifies minimal yet structured changes in individual behaviors and interaction patterns associated with improvements in team performance. Experiments with simulated surgical procedures show that our approach improves predictive performance in diverse behavioral and interaction goals while offering meaningful insights into team dynamics. This work advances surgical AI beyond outcome-driven prediction towards a socially grounded, team-centric, and actionable paradigm to better understand and support the development of team skills in surgical settings.

</details>


### [170] [A Multidimensional Data-Driven Hybrid Transformer Framework for Non-invasive Continuous Blood Pressure Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23276)
*Yuexin Ma,Jingqi Hou,Yuxuan Kang,Zhaoying Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于混合Transformer框架的无袖带连续血压估计器，利用ECG/PPG导出的时序生理特征和人口统计学特征来估计舒张压和收缩压。在MIMIC-III数据集上，10步特征序列结合多源时间编码器和动态条件融合解码器，实现了DBP误差0.41±3.74 mmHg、SBP误差-1.60±5.95 mmHg的性能，符合AAMI和BHS Grade A标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无袖带连续血压监测有助于实现便捷、长期的血压跟踪，但现有方法在估计精度和泛化性上仍有不足。作者旨在利用生理时序特征与人口统计学特征的互补信息，通过混合模型架构提升血压估计的准确性和一致性。

Method: 提出混合Transformer框架：输入为ECG/PPG导出的10步序列（含6个生理描述符和2个人口统计学协变量）；采用多源时间编码器模块，融合Transformer（时序信息）、Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（非线性信息）和XGBoost（表格信息）三个分支；再通过动态条件融合解码器，结合差分多头注意力、token加权聚合和门控残差校正；最后用复合目标函数联合优化DBP和SBP。

Result: 基于MIMIC-III数据集的203名受试者28,486个波形段（保留166名受试者的53,621个观测），在2,431个段级测试窗口上，DBP均值误差±标准差为0.41±3.74 mmHg，SBP为-1.60±5.95 mmHg；95%一致性界限分别为[-6.93,7.74]和[-13.25,10.06] mmHg；10 mmHg内比例分别为98.48%和94.36%；在所有本地重训练基线中实现最低标准差和最窄一致性界限，满足AAMI和BHS Grade A数值阈值。

Conclusion: 特征序列融合框架改善了与参考血压的一致性，满足AAMI和BHS Grade A阈值。但本研究为回顾性分析，不等同于正式设备验证，临床使用前仍需进行受试者不相交及外部评估。

Abstract: Objective. To develop and evaluate a cuffless continuous blood pressure (BP) estimator using temporal physiological and demographic features. We propose a hybrid Transformer framework to estimate diastolic and systolic BP from ECG/PPG-derived feature sequences. Approach. Rather than raw waveforms, the framework models 10-step sequences of six physiological descriptors and two demographic covariates. A Multi-Source Temporal Encoder Module combines Transformer, Kolmogorov-Arnold Network, and XGBoost branches to capture complementary temporal, nonlinear, and tabular information. A Dynamic Conditional Fusion-Decoder applies differential multi-head attention, token-weighted aggregation, and gated residual correction. A robust composite objective jointly optimizes DBP and SBP. Main results. Using the MIMIC-III Waveform and Clinical Databases, the source pool comprised 28,486 waveform segments from 203 subjects, and feature generation retained 53,621 observations from 166 subjects. On 2,431 segment-level held-out test windows, mean error +/- standard deviation was 0.41 +/- 3.74 mmHg for diastolic BP and -1.60 +/- 5.95 mmHg for systolic BP, with 95% limits of agreement of [-6.93, 7.74] and [-13.25, 10.06] mmHg, respectively. The proportions within 10 mmHg were 98.48% and 94.36%. The framework achieved the lowest standard deviations and narrowest limits of agreement among the locally retrained baselines. Significance. The feature-sequence fusion framework improved agreement with reference BP and fell within numerical AAMI and BHS Grade A thresholds on this split. This retrospective analysis is not formal device validation; subject-disjoint and external evaluation remain necessary before clinical use.

</details>


### [171] [How Much Regularization Survives Averaging? Update Masking in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23286)
*Wenhao Yan,Fu Kuroda,Yucheng Jin,Zhenke Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了联邦学习中通过参数更新添加噪声（而非已有的锐度感知最小化）来达到平坦极小值的方法。作者证明在非IID联邦设置下，各客户端独立掩码会使联邦平均削弱噪声正则化（衰减因子恰为客户端数量），而采用相同掩码则可通过梯度多样性恢复部分效果。实验显示在CIFAR-10上恢复因子仅1.19（满分10），关闭小批量采样可提升至8.96，但这类配置训练效果过差无法实际使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中非IID数据需要平坦极小值以实现跨客户端泛化。现有方法借用集中训练中的锐度感知最小化（SAM），但未研究另一种通过参数更新添加噪声的天然正则化途径。作者希望弄清为何这种噪声正则化从未被引入联邦设置，并量化其衰减原因。

Method: 理论上证明：当每个客户端各自绘制掩码时，联邦平均会使噪声正则化对优化器的"惩罚"减弱恰好一个客户端群体规模（cohort size）的倍数；当所有客户端使用相同掩码时，惩罚恢复因子等于客户端群体梯度多样性的倒数。实验上在CIFAR-10数据集上验证该恢复因子，并测试关闭小批量采样及改变数据异质性程度的影响。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上，相同掩码带来的恢复因子仅为1.19（理论最大可能值为10）；关闭小批量采样可将该因子提升至8.96；而将数据异质性改变一百倍，恢复因子仅介于1.17到1.50之间。但保持正则化有效的配置其训练性能过差，无法实际部署使用。

Conclusion: 噪声正则化在联邦设置下的恢复因子受客户端数量及梯度多样性制约，理论上可行的配置在实际训练中表现不佳。这表明通过共享掩码恢复噪声正则化虽然数学上可行，但在实际联邦学习场景中缺乏实用性，为该方向的研究划定了限度。

Abstract: Federated learning on non-IID data seeks flat minima to generalize across clients, and existing methods borrow sharpness-aware minimization from centralized training. There is a second way to reach flat minima, in which the regularization comes for free from noise added to the parameter updates, and it has never been carried over to the federated setting. We show the reason. Masking charges the optimizer for moving in sharp directions. We prove that when each client draws its own mask, federated averaging weakens that charge by exactly the cohort size, and that giving every client the same mask brings it back by a factor equal to the inverse gradient diversity of the cohort. In our experiment setting on CIFAR-10, that factor is 1.19 out of a possible 10. Turning off minibatch sampling raises it to 8.96, while changing data heterogeneity a hundredfold leaves it between 1.17 and 1.50. The configurations keeping the regularization train far too poorly to use.

</details>


### [172] [Poisson Subspace Clustering: Focusing on the Essentials in Count Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23287)
*Collin Leiber,Kai Puolamäki,Heikki Mannila*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 3CPO是一种基于泊松和负二项分布等统计模型的聚类算法，专门处理计数数据。它不仅能给出聚类标签，还能识别相关列子集以增强可解释性，并通过最大化后验概率的迭代算法实现高效聚类，在基因表达、文本和经济等多个领域均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计数数据（如列联表）在多个领域普遍存在，但通用聚类算法往往忽略其分布特性，导致结果不可靠。因此需要基于泊松、负二项分布等成熟统计模型的专门聚类方法。

Method: 提出3CPO算法，基于对计数数据的统计建模（泊松/负二项分布），在输出聚类标签的同时识别相关特征子集以提升可解释性。采用一个简单的迭代算法最大化后验概率来搜索高质量聚类解，并讨论了该算法的性质。

Result: 大量实验表明，3CPO在多个数据领域（基因表达、文本、经济学）中均能在关联子空间内定义高质量聚类，展示出其作为统计可靠且可解释的计数数据聚类方案的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 3CPO是用于计数数据聚类的稳健解决方案，兼顾统计严谨性与结果可解释性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Count data represented as a matrix of non-negative integer values, such as contingency tables, are prevalent across diverse domains. When clustering such data sets, specific methods are required, as generic algorithms often fail to consider their unique distributional properties, leading to unreliable outputs. An effective strategy is to use well-established statistical models such as the Poisson and negative binomial distributions. We present 3CPO, a clustering algorithm based on statistically solid modeling of count data. In addition to the cluster labels, it identifies a subset of relevant columns, enhancing the interpretability of the results. We propose a simple iterative algorithm that maximizes the posterior probability to find good clustering solutions and discuss its properties. Extensive experiments demonstrate its ability to define high-quality clusters within associated subspaces for various data domains, ranging from gene expressions and texts to economics. Our findings suggest that 3CPO is a robust solution for clustering count data in a statistically sound and interpretable manner. Our code is available at https://github.com/collinleiber/3CPO.

</details>


### [173] [Sigmoid Attention as a Better Substrate for Learned KV Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23296)
*Isaac,Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究学习型KV缓存逐出中的"软-硬不匹配"问题，即训练时软门控仅衰减token贡献，而推理时需物理删除KV条目。通过在GPT-2规模模型上进行2×2×2对照实验，发现sigmoid注意力虽在密集语言建模上表现较差，但在学习型硬逐出场景下却能以极小的困惑度代价删除KV条目，且优于H₂O和KeyDiff等后处理方法；而softmax注意力下学习型门控并不统一优于后处理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型KV缓存逐出方法存在软到硬的不匹配：训练时使用可微分的软门控来衰减token贡献，但推理时只有物理移除KV条目才能节省内存。作者推测注意力子层（归一化方式）可能影响这种软到硬转换的有效性，因此设计对照实验进行验证。

Method: 在基于OpenWebText训练的GPT-2规模Transformer上，进行2×2×2受控对比实验，变量包括：注意力类型（sigmoid vs softmax）、学习型门控（有/无）、位置编码。在相同密集骨干网络上，采用匹配的实时缓存协议，将学习型sigmoid门控与H₂O和KeyDiff后处理方法进行比较。

Result: sigmoid门控模型能以相对于自身无逐出基线可忽略的困惑度变化删除KV条目；在相同的密集骨干和实时缓存协议下，学习型sigmoid门控的困惑度低于H₂O和KeyDiff实现，而softmax门控并不统一优于这些后处理方法。

Conclusion: 注意力归一化方式（sigmoid vs softmax）能显著影响训练期软门控是否能干净地迁移到推理期的硬KV删除任务，暗示选择sigmoid注意力可改善学习型KV缓存逐出的有效性。

Abstract: Learned KV-cache eviction often faces a soft-to-hard mismatch: during training, differentiable gates typically attenuate token contributions, whereas inference saves memory only when KV entries are physically removed. We ask whether the attention substrate affects this soft-to-hard transition. Using GPT-2-scale Transformers trained on OpenWebText, we run a controlled $2\times2\times2$ comparison over attention type, learned gating, and positional encoding. Although sigmoid attention is worse as a dense language model, learned hard eviction changes the useful operating points: sigmoid-gated models delete KV entries with negligible PPL change relative to their own no-eviction references. Under a matched live-cache protocol on the same dense backbones, learned sigmoid gates obtain lower PPL than our H$_2$O and KeyDiff implementations, whereas softmax gates do not uniformly beat these post-hoc methods. The results suggest that attention normalization can substantially affect whether a training-time soft gate transfers cleanly to hard KV deletion.

</details>


### [174] [Beyond Point Predictions: Uncertainty-Aware Satellite Poverty Mapping for Public Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23322)
*Markus B. Pettersson,James Bailie,Mohammad Kakooei,Eagon Meng,Adel Daoud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于不确定性感知的机器学习与地球观测影像相结合的方法，用于非洲高分辨率贫困制图。该方法结合同时分位数回归和一种新型保形预测，生成非洲城市邻里级国际财富指数的预测区间，并保证统计覆盖率。研究发现即使点预测性能（R²=0.75）达到先进水平，预测区间仍较宽，表明EO-ML不能天真地用于政策决策。为此，作者开发了一种结合实地调查与模型预测的援助分配程序，可在可证明的排他性风险约束下更高效地分配援助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲大部分地区缺乏高分辨率贫困数据，这对政策制定和研究至关重要。虽然机器学习结合地球观测影像可补充预测贫困数据，但决策者需要确保不会被预测误差误导，因此需要不确定性量化的可靠方法。

Method: 该方法基于同时分位数回归和一种新颖的保形预测形式，使用在Landsat和夜间灯光图像序列上训练的时空transformer模型，为非洲邻里级国际财富指数估计生成具有统计覆盖率保证的预测区间。此外，开发了一个高效援助分配程序，结合实地调查与模型预测，在可证明的排他风险约束下最大化援助效率。

Result: 该方法的点预测性能达到当前最优水平（R²=0.75），但其预测区间比预期更宽，表明即使模型解释力很高，EO-ML也不能天真地用于政策制定。然而，在模拟中，所提出的援助分配方法比其它策略向每位合格受益人交付了显著更多的援助，证明了EO-ML可以作为传统数据来源的可靠补充。

Conclusion: EO-ML中的不确定性是固有的且不可忽视的限制，即使模型性能卓越也需要谨慎对待。但通过不确定性感知的设计和合理的下游决策流程（如风险约束下的援助分配），EO-ML确实可以可靠地补充传统数据来源，关键在于方法需考虑预测的不确定性而不是仅依赖点预测。

Abstract: Despite their critical importance for policy and research, high-resolution poverty data remain limited across much of Africa. Machine learning (ML) with earth observation (EO) imagery has recently emerged as a way to supplement these data by predicting (i.e., estimating) poverty where it has not been directly measured. Yet to be used reliably, decision-makers and analysts need assurances that they will not be misled by the errors in these predictions. To meet this need, we develop an uncertainty-aware EO-ML method for poverty mapping based on simultaneous quantile regression and a novel form of conformal prediction. Using a spatiotemporal transformer trained on sequences of Landsat and nighttime-light images, we produce prediction intervals for neighborhood-level International Wealth Index estimates across Africa which are statistically guaranteed to achieve their desired coverage rates. While our method's point-prediction performance matches the state of the art, its prediction intervals are wider than might be expected given its high $R^2$ of $0.75$. However, other models of similar accuracy likely suffer from comparable uncertainty, pointing to an inherent limitation: even with its remarkably high explanatory power, EO-ML cannot naively be relied upon for policy-making, such as when designing poverty-targeting programs. To handle this challenge, we develop a procedure to efficiently allocate aid using both ground-truth surveys and model predictions while provably ensuring the risk of excluding eligible neighborhoods remains below a prespecified level. In simulations, this approach delivers substantially more aid per eligible recipient than other strategies, thereby demonstrating that EO-ML can indeed be a reliable supplement to traditional data sources---as long as methods

</details>


### [175] [Towards Actionable Surgical Team Dynamics: from Teamwork to Counterfactual Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23344)
*Vincenzo Marco De Luca,Antonio Longa,Andrea Passerini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个扩展的多模态手术团队交互分析数据集，基于真实手术室录音构建，提供了说话人分离、转录文本、多层次团队绩效与交互质量标注，以及反事实标注，用于支持手术场景中团队协作行为的计算建模与AI辅助系统设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险环境（如手术室）中的团队交互建模对理解协调、沟通和个体行为如何影响团队绩效与安全结果至关重要，但现有数据集在模态、标注方案和格式上高度碎片化，难以支撑对真实协作过程的集成分析。

Method: 在现有语料库基础上构建分析就绪版本，包含：(1) 说话人分离与转录文本；(2) 基于标准化手术团队评估协议的多层次团队绩效标注；(3) 覆盖协作、群体动态和非技术技能的交互质量与个体特征结构化评分；(4) 反事实标注，描述在观察到的交互失败情况下可能出现的替代性团队结果；(5) 面向计算建模的结构化时间与关系表示。

Result: 提供了一个统一且多层次标注的多模态数据集资源，涵盖团队绩效、交互过程、个体特征及反事实场景，支持对个体行为、交互模式和团队层面过程如何共同影响手术团队结果的联合分析。

Conclusion: 该数据集旨在成为高风险领域协作行为分析的统一资源，为团队工作过程的计算建模以及AI辅助协作系统的设计提供支撑。

Abstract: Modeling team interactions in high-stakes environments such as operating rooms is critical for understanding how coordination, communication, and individual behaviors shape team performance and safety outcomes. Existing datasets in this domain are often fragmented across modalities, annotation schemes, and formats, limiting their ability to support integrated analyses of real-world collaborative processes. We address this limitation by introducing an extended multimodal dataset for surgical team interaction analysis, built from real operating room recordings. Starting from an existing corpus, we construct an analysis-ready version of the data by providing speaker diarization, transcripts, and multi-level annotations capturing team performance, interaction processes, and individual characteristics. Team performance is assessed using a standardized surgical teamwork evaluation protocol, while interaction quality and individual attributes are annotated through structured rating schemes covering collaboration, group dynamics, and non-technical skills. To further support the study of coordination breakdowns and performance variability, we introduce counterfactual annotations that describe plausible alternative team outcomes in the presence of observed interaction failures, enabling analysis of how specific behavioral patterns may relate to different trajectories of team performance. In addition, we provide structured temporal and relational representations designed to support computational modeling of teamwork processes and the design of AI-assisted collaborative systems. The dataset is designed to support the study of how individual actions, interaction patterns, and team-level processes jointly contribute to team outcomes in surgical settings, providing a unified resource for analyzing collaborative behavior in high-stakes domains.

</details>


### [176] [Test-Time Adaptation for ECG Classification via SQI-Gated Self-Training and Beat-Rhythm Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23347)
*Wenhan Jiang,Zhipeng Deng,Jiale Zhou,Haolin Wang,Yafei Ou,Yefeng Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BeatRhythm-TTA，一个针对心电图的测试时自适应框架。通过SQI门控更新过滤低质量信号，并通过双层一致性约束保留心跳形态和节律动态，在三个适应协议上平均比最优基线提升+2.70% Macro-F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习ECG分类模型在域偏移下（如采集设备和患者群体变化）性能严重下降。现有的测试时自适应方法在ECG任务上表现不佳，因为朴素在线更新忽略了心脏周期的心跳-节律层次结构，且容易受到信号伪影干扰，导致适应不稳定和模型漂移。

Method: 提出BeatRhythm-TTA框架，包含两个核心组件：（1）Signal Quality Index（SQI）门控适应机制，选择性过滤低质量信号以防止有害的梯度更新；（2）双层一致性约束（dual-level consistency），在心跳形态（beat morphology）和节律动态（rhythm dynamics）两个层面强制模型在适应过程中保持语义一致性。

Result: 在PTB-XL作为源域、CPSC2018和Georgia作为目标域的多标签ECG诊断任务上，跨三种适应协议进行评估，平均比最优竞争方法提升+2.70%的Macro-F1相对改善。

Conclusion: 针对ECG领域定制的TTA方法BeatRhythm-TTA，通过显式建模ECG的噪声观测和心跳-节律结构语义，能够有效缓解域偏移下的性能退化，为ECG相关测试时自适应任务提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Deep learning models for electrocardiogram (ECG) classification often suffer from significant performance degradation when deployed in unseen domains due to shifts in acquisition devices and patient populations. Test-time adaptation (TTA) offers a practical solution by adapting models using only unlabeled data at inference time. However, existing TTA methods often underperform on ECG tasks, since naive online updates ignore the hierarchical beat-rhythm structure of cardiac cycles and are vulnerable to signal artifacts, which leads to unstable adaptation and model drift. We propose BeatRhythm-TTA, an ECG-tailored TTA framework that explicitly accounts for ECG's noisy observations and structured beat-rhythm semantics under domain shift. First, to handle pervasive ECG artifacts, we introduce a Signal Quality Index (SQI)-gated adaptation scheme that selectively filters out low-quality signals to prevent harmful updates. Second, to leverage ECG's beat-rhythm semantics, we enforce dual-level consistency so the model preserves beat morphology and rhythm dynamics while adapting to shifted acquisition conditions. Extensive experiments on multi-label ECG diagnosis across three adaptation protocols, using PTB-XL as the source domain and CPSC2018/Georgia as two target domains, demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, yielding an average +2.70% relative improvement in Macro-F1 over the best competing method.

</details>


### [177] [Spectrum-Aware Bounds on Invertibility for Privacy-Enhancing Instance Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23382)
*Seokjin Hwang,Yuting,Li,Kiwan Maeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一族新的实例编码隐私下界，相比现有MSE下界更紧、适用于确定性编码器，并能扩展到其他范数相似性度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实例编码是共享数据时的常用隐私增强技术，但大多数工作缺乏编码不可逆性的理论保证。现有MSE下界存在三个关键缺陷：过于松弛、仅适用于随机编码器（排除了许多实践中使用的确定性编码器）、且仅限于MSE度量。

Method: 作者通过恰当地考虑编码器的谱结构，引入了一族新的重构误差下界。这些下界在理论上：(1) 比现有下界更紧；(2) 适用于完全确定性的编码器；(3) 可扩展到MSE之外的其他基于范数的相似性度量。

Result: 在多种编码器、数据集和攻击方法上进行了评估，结果表明所提出的下界始终成立，且一致优于现有下界。

Conclusion: 通过利用编码器的谱结构，可以构建更通用、更紧致的隐私重构下界，从而为实例编码的隐私保证提供更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Instance encoding is a popular empirical technique for privacy enhancement when sharing data to an untrusted server. It transforms sensitive data through an encoding process before sharing, with the hope that the encoding process retains utility but makes it hard to reconstruct the original data. However, most work offers no theoretical guarantee that the encoding process is actually irreversible. A recent work derived a mean-squared error (MSE) bound limiting any adversary's reconstruction accuracy, offering one of the first theoretical results in this domain. This bound, however, has three critical limitations: it is often too loose, only works with randomized encoders (excluding many deterministic encoders practitioners use), and only bounds MSE. We introduce a family of new bounds that (1) are tighter, (2) applicable even to fully deterministic encoders, and (3) can extend beyond MSE to other norm-based similarity metrics, by properly accounting for the encoder's spectral structure. We evaluate our bounds across a range of encoders, datasets, and attacks, showing they hold consistently and improve upon the existing bound.

</details>


### [178] [The Axiomatic Trader: Latent Regularity, Information Budgets, and the Canonical Form of a Quantitative Investment System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23416)
*Jiayu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从系统化交易的核心理念——"过去发现的规律会持续存在"出发，将其形式化为一种由未观测潜在状态驱动的时间不变机制，并论证该机制仅需研究者声明五个常数（递归界Λ、块长b、表示的不变性缺陷ε₀、状态坐标的相干时间ℓᵢ、信号上限ρ及其中依赖体制的比例κ），即可近乎唯一地确定正确量化投资系统的架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统化交易的核心假设（历史规律具有持续性）缺乏严格的数学形式化，导致量化投资系统的设计高度依赖经验与随意性，缺少理论框架来约束系统架构的合理选择。

Method: 作者将"规律持久性"表述为由未观测潜在状态驱动的时间不变机制，通过对五个关键常数的声明来刻画该机制的完备约束条件，进而推导出正确量化投资系统架构的必然结构。

Result: 论文表明，一旦研究者明确这五个常数（递归界与块长、表示不变性缺陷、状态坐标相干时间、信号天花板及其对体制依赖的比例），正确的量化投资系统架构几乎被唯一确定，即架构选择空间被大幅压缩到近乎唯一的正确设计。

Conclusion: 量化投资系统的正确架构并非自由设计的结果，而由潜藏时间不变机制的结构性参数所决定；研究者只需诚实地声明五个基本常数，即可从第一性原理推导出所需的系统架构。

Abstract: Systematic trading rests on one article of faith: that regularities found in the past persist. We state it as a time-invariant mechanism driven by an unobserved latent state, and show that it leaves a researcher five constants to declare --- the recurrence bound $Lambda$ at a block length $b$, the invariance defect $epsilon_0$ of the representation it is declared of, the coherence times $ell_i$ of the state's coordinates, the signal ceiling $rho$ and the fraction $kappa$ of it contingent on the regime --- after which the architecture of a correct quantitative investment system is nearly forced.

</details>


### [179] [ChebBooster: A Training-Free Approach for Efficient Diffusion Transformer Inference via Chebyshev-Inspired Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23429)
*Chengjie Lu,Tianchi Deng,Zhengqi He,Chengwen Luo,Xueliang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ChebBooster，一个基于切比雪夫多项式理论的免训练外推框架，通过重心公式（Barycentric formulation）实现数值稳定高效的DiT加速，在多项模型和任务上达到最高3.68倍延迟加速和5.12倍FLOPs缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer（DiT）在图像生成中表现出色，但其采样过程在每个时间步都需执行完整模型，计算开销巨大。已有的基于缓存的加速方案在长间隔内简单复用导致精度低，而基于泰勒级数的外推方法因龙格振荡而数值不稳定，亟需一种既稳定又高效的加速方案。

Method: 提出ChebBooster，一种基于切比雪夫多项式理论的免训练外推框架。利用重心插值公式（Barycentric formulation）来评估切比雪夫近似器，以获得高数值稳定性和极小的额外开销；同时将外推过程解耦为离线权重预计算阶段和轻量级的在线应用阶段。

Result: 在DiT-XL/2、PixArt-Σ和FLUX.1-dev三个代表性DiT模型上的大量实验表明，ChebBooster在视觉质量和推理效率上均取得一致的提升，最高达到3.68倍延迟加速和5.12倍FLOPs缩减，在多种生成任务和分辨率下优于现有的免训练基线方法。

Conclusion: ChebBooster通过切比雪夫多项式与重心公式的结合，有效解决了现有外推加速方法在数值稳定性和精度上的瓶颈，实现了对DiT模型稳定且高效的无训练加速，具备良好的通用性与实用性。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have shown strong performance in high-fidelity image generation, but their sampling process remains computationally intensive due to full model execution at every timestep. While cache-based acceleration has been explored to mitigate inference cost, naive reuse schemes suffer from low accuracy over long intervals, and Taylor-series-based extrapolation methods often face instability caused by Runge oscillations. In this paper, we propose ChebBooster, a training-free extrapolation framework based on Chebyshev polynomial theory that achieves stable and efficient acceleration for DiTs. Specifically, we adopt the Barycentric formulation to evaluate Chebyshev approximants with high numerical stability and minimal overhead, and further decouple the extrapolation into an offline weight precomputation phase and a lightweight online application stage. Extensive experiments across three representative DiT-based models, including DiT-XL/2, PixArt-$Σ$, and FLUX.1-dev, demonstrate that ChebBooster achieves consistent improvements in visual quality and inference efficiency, reaching up to $3.68\times$ latency speedup and $5.12\times$ FLOPs reduction, outperforming existing training-free baselines under diverse generation tasks and resolutions.

</details>


### [180] [Traceable Spectral Inference via Influence Functions: Efficient Data Attribution and Error Proxies for the Ariel Mission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23458)
*Nikki Grens,Luís F. Simões,Kai Hou Yip,Theresa Lueftinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文面向科学航天任务（如ESA的Ariel）中无法获取地面真值的场景，提出了一种基于预测而非损失的影响函数（influence functions）方法，用于训练数据归因。通过利用极限学习机（ELM）的闭式岭解高效计算预测影响，并推导出基于影响的保守误差代理，能够有效地进行标签无关的部署和误差评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学航天任务（如Ariel）中的机器学习模型部署需要可解释性，但运行期间无法获取地面真值，物理合理性评估困难。现有的XAI方法多聚焦于特征归因，而非训练数据归因，且传统的基于损失的影响函数需要标签，无法在部署阶段直接使用。因此需要一种适用于无标签场景的训练数据归因方法。

Method: 论文提出三项关键贡献：(1) 将影响函数从基于损失重新表述为基于预测，实现标签无关的部署；(2) 利用极限学习机（ELM）的闭式岭解，高效计算无穷小预测影响； (3) 通过将训练残差沿影响敏感性传播，推导出基于影响的保守误差代理。

Result: 在模拟光谱上的评估表明，所提出的误差代理与基于尺度和形状的光谱误差强相关。此外，影响函数能够识别最具影响的训练样本，并近似找出最有害的样本。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明该方法可以作为科学机器学习中可操作的框架，在无地面真值的运行条件下提供训练数据归因与误差评估能力。

Abstract: Interpretability is critical for machine learning models deployed in scientific space missions such as ESA's Ariel, where ground truth is unavailable during operations and physical plausibility must be assessed. While most explainable AI methods focus on feature attribution, this work investigates training data attribution through influence functions and introduces three key contributions for operational spectroscopy pipelines. First, influence is reformulated in terms of prediction rather than loss, enabling label-free deployment. Second, by leveraging the closed-form ridge solution of an Extreme Learning Machine, infinitesimal prediction influence is efficiently computed. Third, an influence-based conservative error proxy is derived by propagating training residuals through the influence sensitivities. Evaluated against simulated spectra, the proposed proxy correlates strongly with scale and shape-based spectral errors. Furthermore, influence functions enable the identification of the most influential samples and the approximation of the most harmful ones. Together, these results suggest that this approach can serve as an operational framework for scientific machine learning.

</details>


### [181] [Diversity-Based Active Learning: An Evaluation of Metric Spaces for Active Learning Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23461)
*Siddharth Chilamkur,Dorit S. Hochbaum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于池的主动学习中Greedy K-center方法在不同度量空间下的性能表现，发现将未标注实例映射到预测概率空间并进行熵加权通常在主动学习选择中优于其他方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着分类方法快速发展，训练这些模型需要大量标注数据，而获取标注数据的成本高昂。主动学习通过在未标注数据中选择最有信息量的样本进行查询，可以降低标注成本。然而，不同的度量空间会影响主动学习选择策略的效果，需要系统性评估。

Method: 以随机森林分类器作为基线评估器，在合成数据集和真实数据集上，对Greedy K-center方法在三种度量空间（原始特征空间、线性判别分析空间、模型派生概率空间）中的表现进行实证比较，其中概率空间还包含有无熵加权的两种变体。

Result: 实验结果表明，将未标注实例映射到预测概率空间并对结果进行熵加权，在大多数情况下优于其他度量空间下的Greedy K-center主动学习选择策略。

Conclusion: 在基于池的主动学习中，使用模型派生的概率空间配合熵加权可以更有效地选择有信息量的样本，为主动学习的多样性选择策略提供了一种更优的实践方案。

Abstract: With rapid advancement over the last few years, many different methods are now widely used for classification. However, training these models requires substantial labeled data. Active Learning is a potential solution to this problem. Pool-based active learning minimizes costs by querying only the most informative samples from an unlabeled dataset. Diversity-based approaches, on the other hand, attempt to select a representative subset of the data. There are many different objectives for determining the selection process, including exact K-center, exact K-median, and Greedy K-center. In this paper, we will focus on evaluating the performance of Greedy K-center across a variety of metric spaces: the raw feature space, a Linear Discriminant Analysis (LDA) space, and a model-derived probability space (with and without entropy-based weighting). Using Random Forest classifiers as a baseline evaluator, our empirical results on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that mapping unlabeled instances into a predictive probability space and weighting the result by entropy often dominates the other options for active learning selection with Greedy K-center.

</details>


### [182] [ProxyFormer: A Dual-Stream Proxy Architecture for Ultra-Long Context and High-Resolution Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23463)
*Zhongpan Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProxyFormer提出了一种基于代理令牌（proxy tokens）的双流架构，通过将细粒度局部特征压缩为少量代理状态并在压缩空间中执行全局交互，再解压回局部流，从而大幅降低注意力计算和KV缓存对序列长度的二次增长开销，在16GB GPU上将可训练序列长度从约20K扩展到约0.7M，并在多针检索与图像生成任务中展现了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力计算和KV缓存随序列长度呈二次方增长，这是超长上下文语言模型和高分辨率生成模型的核心瓶颈。传统的单次压缩方法会带来不可逆的信息丢失，需要一种既能降低复杂度又能保留细粒度信息的架构。

Method: ProxyFormer采用通用双流架构：每层中将细粒度局部特征自底向上压缩为少量代理状态，仅在压缩的代理空间中执行昂贵的全局交互，再将产生的全局上下文代理自顶向下解压注入局部流。由于局部流跨层持续存在，未被单次压缩捕获的细粒度信息可在后续层中被进一步细化，缓解了传统单次压缩不可逆信息丢失的问题。此外还引入了因子化多级压缩/解压、逐层动态压缩比、非对称双嵌入以及仅代理KV缓存的推理方案。

Result: 在16GB GPU、batch size为1的标准decoder-only模型仅能训练约20K token序列，而ProxyFormer以64的压缩比将可训练序列长度扩展至约0.7M。以64K窗口训练的模型在1,048,576 token的多针检索任务中保持92%-95%的检索准确率；以8K窗口训练的模型外推至256K token时准确率超过94%。初步图像生成实验证明了ProxyFormer在像素空间和潜空间流匹配中的可行性。

Conclusion: ProxyFormer通过压缩代理空间中的全局交互与持久局部流的协同设计，在显著降低计算和显存开销的同时，缓解了信息丢失问题，为超长上下文建模和高分辨率生成提供了一种通用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: The quadratic growth of attention computation and key-value (KV) cache with respect to sequence length is a central bottleneck for ultra-long-context language models and high-resolution generative models. We propose ProxyFormer, a general dual-stream architecture built upon proxy tokens. In each layer, fine-grained local features are compressed bottom-up into a small set of proxy states; expensive global interactions are performed only in the compressed proxy space; the globally contextualized proxies are then decompressed and injected top-down back into the local stream. Because the local stream persists across layers, fine-grained information that is not captured by one compression step remains accessible for later refinement, alleviating the irreversible information loss of conventional one-shot compression. We further introduce factorized multi-level compression/decompression, layer-wise dynamic compression ratios, asymmetric dual embeddings, and a proxy-only KV-cache inference scheme. On a 16GB GPU with batch size 1, a standard decoder-only model can train sequences of only about 20K tokens, whereas ProxyFormer with a compression ratio of 64 extends the trainable sequence length to about 0.7M. A model trained with a 64K window retains 92%-95% retrieval accuracy on a multi-needle retrieval task with 1,048,576 tokens, and a model trained with an 8K window exceeds 94% accuracy when extrapolated to 256K tokens. Preliminary image-generation experiments demonstrate the feasibility of ProxyFormer for both pixel-space and latent-space flow matching.

</details>


### [183] [RAD: Rule-Augmented Relational Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23468)
*Noah Dahle,Anne Tumlin,Ngoc Tran,Xenofon Koutsoukos,Tyler Derr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RAD（Rule-Augmented Relational Anomaly Detector），一种结合异构图表示学习与符号规则信号的规则增强型关系异常检测器，用于多表关系数据库中的异常实体/事件检测，并在LANL网络攻击检测和Amazon/H&M用户流失异常三个基准上取得最佳平均AUROC和AUPRC排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测方法通常将多表关系数据库展平为单一特征矩阵，这会丢失实体身份、模式结构和多跳依赖信息，导致依赖关系上下文而非孤立特征值的异常难以被发现。关系型异常检测还面临如何将符号行为证据融入学习到的关系表示的额外挑战。

Method: RAD首先从随机森林路径中推导候选规则，基于被打分实体/事件的展平摘要生成规则，并将其精炼为紧凑可解释的谓词；然后将规则特征注入异构图表示学习模型中，最后使用基于重建和成对排序的监督信号学习异常分数。

Result: 在三类基准数据集（LANL网络攻击事件检测、Amazon和H&M关系数据库的非预期用户流失异常）上，RAD在自然类别不平衡条件下均优于展平表格检测器和关系基线方法，取得最佳平均AUROC和AUPRC。消融实验表明直接规则注入和排序监督是关键贡献因素，而边重建并非总是有益的。

Conclusion: RAD验证了将符号规则特征与异构图表示学习相结合能够有效捕获关系型数据库中的上下文依赖异常，为关系异常检测提供了一种可行且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Anomaly detection is often applied to data stored in relational databases, yet most existing methods require flattening multiple tables into a single feature matrix. This flattening can obscure entity identity, schema structure, and multi-hop dependencies, limiting the detection of anomalies that depend on relational context rather than isolated feature values. Beyond preserving relational structure, relational anomaly detection raises an additional challenge: how to incorporate symbolic behavioral evidence into learned relational representations. To address these challenges, we study relational anomaly detection, where the goal is to identify anomalous entities or events in a multi-table database. We propose RAD, a rule-augmented relational anomaly detector that combines heterogeneous graph representation learning with refined symbolic rule signals. RAD derives candidate rules from random-forest paths over flattened summaries of the entities or events being scored, refines them into compact interpretable predicates, injects the resulting rule features into the graph model, and learns anomaly scores using reconstruction-based and pairwise-ranking supervision. To evaluate this setting, we introduce a relational anomaly detection benchmark spanning three settings: LANL cybersecurity event detection and two unexpected user-churn anomaly tasks derived from Amazon and H&M relational databases. Experiments show that RAD improves anomaly ranking over flattened tabular detectors and relational baselines under natural class imbalance, achieving the best average rank on AUROC and AUPRC across the benchmark. Ablations show that direct rule injection and ranking-based supervision are key contributors to performance, while edge reconstruction is not uniformly beneficial. Our code and data are available at: https://github.com/noahd15/RAD_RelationalAnomalyDetection.

</details>


### [184] [MetaCaster: Meta-Harness-Optimized Agent for End-to-End Few-Shot Learning of Lightweight Time Series Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23473)
*ChengAo Shen,Wenchao Yu,Fangyu Wu,Dongjin Song,Hanghang Tong,Dongsheng Luo,Wei Cheng,Haifeng Chen,Jingchao Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MetaCaster是一个元优化多智能体框架，解决轻量级时间序列预测模型在少量样本场景下的学习难题。通过代理驱动的数据生成，自动从少量示例和文本上下文中训练出专用轻量预测器，实现了数据效率和计算效率的兼顾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测正朝着多模态和智能体设置演进，但基础模型在资源受限场景下不经济。轻量级预测器虽然更受欢迎，却通常需要大量训练数据，限制了在稀缺、缓慢积累或隐私敏感的时间序列领域的应用。因此需要解决轻量级预测器的少样本学习问题。

Method: 提出MetaCaster框架，采用元挟持优化（meta-harness-optimized）的多智能体架构，通过智能体数据生成机制自动从少量示例和文本上下文训练专有轻量预测器。智能体在此范式中不作为预测器本身，而作为中间工程师角色，为部署准备高效的任务专用预测器。

Result: 在18个数据集、23种最先进轻量级预测器和14个基线上进行实验，MetaCaster在实现数据效率和计算效率的同时保持了高质量的时间序列预测性能。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种新的时间序列预测范式，其中智能体作为中间工程师而非预测器，通过代理数据生成实现轻量预测器的少样本高效训练，为资源受限场景下的TSF任务提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Time series forecasting (TSF) is evolving toward multimodal and agentic settings, yet using foundation models remains uneconomical in resource-constrained scenarios, where compact, specialized forecasters are more desirable. However, lightweight forecasters typically require substantial training data, limiting their use in domains with scarce, slowly accumulated, or privacy-sensitive time series. To address this dilemma, we investigate the challenging problem of few-shot learning for lightweight forecasters. We propose MetaCaster, a meta-harness-optimized multi-agent framework that uses agentic data generation to automatically train specialized lightweight forecasters from only a few examples and textual contexts. Our work highlights a new TSF paradigm in which agents act not as forecasters but as intermediary engineers that prepare efficient, task-specific forecasters for deployment. Experiments on 18 datasets, 23 state-of-the-art lightweight forecasters, and 14 baselines demonstrate that MetaCaster achieves both data efficiency and computational efficiency while maintaining high-quality TSF performance.

</details>


### [185] [Provably adaptive sampling with uniform and remasking discrete diffusion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23554)
*Daniil Dmitriev,Zhihan Huang,Yuting Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对离散扩散模型的采样效率问题，提出了一种基于leave-one-out去噪器的一阶采样器，适用于均匀和remasking前向过程。作者证明采样复杂度由目标分布的内在依赖结构（以双总相关DTC(X₀)衡量）决定，而非环境维度d，仅在O(DTC(X₀)/ε)步内即可达到O(ε_score+ε)的采样误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型是自回归生成的替代方案，支持并行更新，但其采样效率高度依赖前向过程和采样器的选择。以往研究表明，对于均匀前向过程和标准τ-leaping采样器，采样复杂度下界与维度d线性相关，这引发了该依赖是否为前向过程固有性质的疑问。作者旨在回答这一开放问题。

Method: 1) 提出基于leave-one-out去噪器的一阶采样器，支持坐标级并行更新，适用于均匀和remasking前向过程。2) 引入一个贝叶斯最优辅助采样器，将离散化误差与分数估计误差分离分析。3) 推导离散化误差的精确信息论表示，以不同时刻前向过程坐标间的互信息形式给出。4) 在均匀和remasking情形下，该表示可由DTC(X₀)控制，从而建立自适应采样保证。

Result: 主要结果为自适应采样保证：忽略对数因子，N = O(DTC(X₀)/ε)步离散化即可实现O(ε_score+ε)的采样误差。采样复杂度由目标分布的DTC(X₀)决定而非环境维度d。该结果否定了采样复杂度线性依赖维度的固有性，并在结构化合成分布上通过数值实验验证了维度自适应行为。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型的采样复杂度应基于目标分布的内在依赖结构来刻画，而非环境维度。DTC(X₀)是更本质的度量，所提出的采样器能够纠正并行更新中产生的去噪错误，从而在依赖结构简单的分布上实现接近维度无关的采样效率。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models offer a promising alternative to autoregressive generation by enabling parallel updates, but their sampling efficiency can depend strongly on the choice of the forward process and the sampler. For the uniform forward process, existing lower bounds for the standard $τ$-leaping sampler scale linearly with the ambient dimension $d$, raising the question of whether this dependence is intrinsic to the forward process. We answer this question in the negative. We consider a first-order sampler based on the leave-one-out denoiser for uniform and remasking processes whose coordinate updates can be performed in parallel. In both cases, the sampler can correct denoising mistakes during the sampling process, which becomes necessary when many coordinates are updated together. Our main result establishes an adaptive sampling guarantee: up to logarithmic factors, $N = O(\mathrm{DTC}(X_0) / \varepsilon)$ discretization steps suffice to achieve sampling error $O(\varepsilon_{\mathrm{score}}+\varepsilon)$, where $\varepsilon_{\mathrm{score}}$ is the error in score estimation. Thus, the sampling complexity is governed by the intrinsic dependence structure of the target distribution, as measured by its dual total correlation $\mathrm{DTC}(X_0)$, rather than directly by the ambient dimension $d$. Our analysis proceeds through a Bayes-optimal auxiliary sampler that separates discretization error from score-estimation error. We also derive an exact information-theoretic representation of the discretization error in terms of the mutual information between different coordinates of the forward process at different times. This representation applies to general forward processes and, in the uniform and remasking cases, can be controlled by $\mathrm{DTC}(X_0)$. Numerical experiments on structured synthetic distributions illustrate the predicted dimension-adaptive behavior.

</details>


### [186] [How to Train a Critic Stably and Efficiently](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23566)
*Penghui Qi,Xiangxin Zhou,Wee Sun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了最佳实践评论家优化（BPCO）方法，一种结合DPPO、价值预测界定奖励范围、蒙特卡洛价值目标、非归一化策略优势和长度自适应广义优势估计的评论家训练方案，使基于评论家的强化学习在不训练评论家模型的情况下也能稳定高效地训练大型语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于组的强化学习方法（如GRPO）通过为每个提示采样多个响应来避免训练评论家模型，但需采样多个响应；基于评论家的方法只需一个响应即可估计token级优势，但标准训练流程往往不稳定。本研究旨在理解这种不稳定性并开发稳定可靠的评论家训练方案。

Method: BPCO将DPPO、受奖励范围约束的价值预测、蒙特卡洛价值目标、非归一化策略优势以及长度自适应广义优势估计（GAE）相结合。由于评论家仅在训练阶段使用，BPCO可让评论家利用对策略隐藏的奖励定义信息（如参考答案或评分标准）。通过控制实验隔离每个设计选择的影响，并在1.5B参数到30B-A3B混合专家模型上进行数学推理任务验证。

Result: 在1.5B到30B-A3B混合专家模型上，BPCO在数学推理任务中一致改进强评论家基线，在每提示仅采样一个响应的条件下匹配或超过基于组的基线。该方案同样改进了基于评分标准的奖励学习。

Conclusion: 精心设计的评论家模型可以提供比组相对优势估计更可靠的替代方案，使基于评论家的优势估计达到甚至超越基于组的优势估计方法的效果。

Abstract: Group-based reinforcement learning methods such as GRPO for large language models avoid training a critic by sampling multiple responses for each prompt. A reliable critic could instead estimate token-level advantages from one response, but standard critic-based training recipes are often unstable. We study this instability and develop \textbf{Best-Practice Critic Optimization (BPCO)}, a recipe that combines DPPO, value predictions bounded to the reward range, Monte Carlo value targets, unnormalized policy advantages, and length-adaptive generalized advantage estimation. Because the critic is used only during training, BPCO can also condition it on reward-defining information, such as a reference answer or grading rubric, that is hidden from the policy. Controlled experiments isolate the effect of each design choice. Across mathematical reasoning tasks with models ranging from 1.5B parameters to 30B-A3B mixtures of experts, BPCO improves a strong critic-based baseline consistently, and matches or exceeds a group-based baseline while sampling one response per prompt. The same recipe also improves learning with rubric-based rewards. These results show that a carefully designed critic provides a reliable alternative to group-relative advantage estimation. Code is available at https://github.com/QPHutu/golden_critic

</details>
